### [1] [GæniceN: a minimal model to relax magnetization states self-consistently in general artificial spin ices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37889)
*H. Braun,E. Freeman,G. Alatteili,E. Iacocca*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出GaeniceN，基于Gaenice三分量宏自旋框架，采用迭代方式实现Landau-Lifshitz方程的耗散项，从而自洽地弛豫人工自旋冰磁化态，并能与Gaenice动态计算衔接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工自旋冰在GHz频段存在多模态及纳米磁体边缘磁化偏差，简单模型难以描述其动力学，需要每个纳米磁体三个宏自旋的最小模型；而Gaenice虽能处理动态，却缺乏自洽获取稳定初始磁化态的手段。

Method: 在同一Gaenice框架内构造弛豫方法：将Landau-Lifshitz方程中的耗散项以迭代方式实现，快速收敛至弛豫态；并以低幅外场下金字塔型人工自旋冰为算例，通过计算系统平均能量判定相变。

Result: 该方法能正确预测金字塔ASI在外加低幅场下的相间转变，且输出可直接作为Gaenice计算铁磁共振、能带结构等动力学的初始条件。

Conclusion: GaeniceN与Gaenice共用框架、互为补充，可形成从静态弛豫到 GHz 动力学的完整流程，有助于磁振子学应用中人工自旋冰的设计。

Abstract: Artificial spin ices are geometric arrangement of nanomagnets that exhibit reconfigurable magnetization states and GHz dynamics. Their investigation in the GHz regime is challenging because of the existence of multiple modes and the impact of magnetization deviations at the edges of the nanomagnets. A minimal model to capture such dynamics require three macrospins per nanomagnet, an approach that was implemented for generic artificial spin ices in the tool Gaenice. Here, we use the Gaenice framework to realize a relaxation method to stabilize magnetization states self-consistently, called GaeniceN. We implement the dissipative component of the Landau-Lifshitz equation in an iterative approach to efficiently converge to the relaxed state. As an example, we find our approach to correctly predict the transition between phases in a pyramid ASI subject to a low-magnitude external field by computing the system's average energy. Because they are rooted on the same framework, GaeniceN produces the initial condition needed for Gaenice to compute dynamics such as ferromagnetic resonance or band structures. Combined, these tools will be able to aid in the design of artificial spin ices with applications in magnonics.

</details>


### [2] [Anomalous Hall Conductivity as an Intrinsic Property of a Tight-Binding Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37939)
*E. S. Andriyakhina,G. Lemut,P. W. Brouwer*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 弹道体系中dc Hall响应的嵌入依赖性研究：杂质散射弛豫下总Hall电导率σ_xy^H与几何嵌入无关，而带内、side-jump、anomalous-distribution各分项仍依赖嵌入；给出band项本身嵌入无关的对称性判据，但指出其既不能等同于σ_xy^H也不能可靠近似之。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弹道体系中反常dc Hall响应通常依赖微观细节，如轨道几何嵌入方式和测量的具体实现；作者希望厘清在何种条件下反常Hall电导率可作为体系的内禀性质。

Method: 在弹道框架下分析完整dc Hall响应σ_xy^H对几何嵌入的依赖性；引入杂质散射弛豫，分别考察（内禀）band项与（外禀）side-jump、anomalous-distribution项；并推导band项自身嵌入无关的对称性判据。

Result: 存在杂质散射弛豫时，总dc Hall响应σ_xy^H变得与嵌入无关；但band、side-jump与anomalous-distribution三个分项各自仍保持嵌入依赖。所推导的对称性判据仅保证band项本身嵌入无关，既不能据此将其等同于完整Hall电导率σ_xy^H，也使其难以作为σ_xy^H的可靠近似。

Conclusion: 嵌入依赖性可作为判别工具：响应系数若与嵌入无关，则对应材料的定态内禀性质；若仍依赖嵌入，则说明其保留对测量协议的敏感性。该判据为区分内禀材料属性与测量方案依赖的响应提供了原则性诊断。

Abstract: In a ballistic system, the anomalous dc Hall response generically depends on microscopic details, including the geometric embedding of the orbitals and the precise implementation of the measurement. Here, we consider the question under what circumstances the anomalous Hall conductivity can be regarded as an intrinsic property of a system. We show that the full dc Hall response $σ_{xy}^{\rm H}$ becomes embedding-independent in the presence of relaxation by impurity scattering, although the individual (intrinsic) band and (extrinsic) side-jump and anomalous-distribution contributions to the Hall conductivity separately remain embedding-dependent. We further derive a symmetry criterion for when the band term itself becomes embedding-independent, but argue that this alone neither identifies it with the full Hall conductivity $σ_{xy}^{\rm H}$, nor makes it a reliable approximation for $σ_{xy}^{\rm H}$. More broadly, our results establish embedding dependence as a diagnostic of whether a response coefficient represents a stationary intrinsic property of a material or retains sensitivity to measurement protocol.

</details>


### [3] [Few-Electron Partitioning in the Fractional Quantum Hall Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37950)
*T. Jonckheere,J. Rech,B. Grémaud*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究分数量子霍尔点接触处N电子态的分割统计，在可解点g=1/2给出非微扰电流与噪声公式，Levi子脉冲下FCS化为N×N行列式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受少电子分割实验[Shaju et al., Nature 642, 928 (2025)]启发，探究N电子态在分数量子霍尔点接触中的分割统计性质。

Method: 在可解点g=1/2构造非微扰电流与噪声公式，适用于任意驱动；对洛伦兹脉冲(Levi子)将全计数统计化为N×N行列式；计算N=2,3,4的对称化累积量。

Result: 电荷仅以e为单位转移；N=2,3,4的对称化累积量显示强反聚束与高阶关联，可在(ν1,ν2)=(1,1/3)结中观测。

Conclusion: 少电子分割统计表现出以e为基本单位的电荷转移与显著反聚束及高阶关联，提供分数量子霍尔体系中可检验的预言。

Abstract: Motivated by few-electron partitioning experiments [Shaju et al., Nature 642, 928 (2025)], we study the partition statistics of $N$-electron states at a fractional quantum Hall point contact. At the solvable point $g=1/2$, we obtain nonperturbative current and noise formulas for arbitrary drives, while for Lorentzian pulses (Levitons) the full counting statistics reduces to an $N\times N$ determinant. We show that the charge is transferred solely in units of $e$, and that symmetrized cumulants for $N=2,3,4$ reveal strong antibunching and higher-order correlations, observable in a $(ν_1,ν_2)=(1,1/3)$ junction.

</details>


### [4] [Coherent control and high purity single-photon emission from telecom quantum dots on metamorphic InGaAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38060)
*Beatrice Costa,Bianca Scaparra,Hubert Riedl,Gregor Koblmüller,Eugenio Zallo,Jonathan J. Finley,Lukas Hanschke,Kai Müller*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作通过比较准共振与声子辅助激发，分离激发诱导效应与本征性质，在O/C波段InAs量子点中获得21 μeV线宽、单光子纯度约10⁻²及Rabi型振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向光纤量子通信，需要发射在电信O、C波段的单光子源；而嵌入metamorphic InGaAs层中应变弛豫InGaAs基质的InAs量子点的本征光学性质需被澄清。

Method: 采用相干准共振激发与声子辅助激发两种方案，系统对比激发方式；结合光致发光激发谱识别高激发态，并实现三离子跃迁的相干控制。

Result: 观察到低至21 μeV的窄线宽、慢衰减通道消失、二阶自相关达10⁻²量级的单光子纯度；两个电信波段均观测到Rabi型振荡。

Conclusion: 该InAs量子点体系具备优异单光子特性，适合量子通信与量子技术应用。

Abstract: Semiconductor quantum dots emitting in the telecommunication O- and C-bands are promising candidates as single-photon sources for fiber-based quantum communication. In this work, we show how coherent quasi-resonant and phonon-assisted excitation help reveal the intrinsic optical properties of InAs quantum dots embedded in relaxed InGaAs matrices on top of metamorphic InGaAs layers for achieving emission in the telecom bands. By systematically comparing excitation schemes, we separate excitation-induced effects from intrinsic emitter properties. This allows us to observe linewidths as narrow as 21 $μ$eV, the disappearance of slow decay channels, and single-photon purity reaching second-order autocorrelation values in the order of $10^{-2}$. Photoluminescence excitation reveals higher excited states, enabling coherent control of trion transitions and the observation of Rabi-like oscillations in both telecom bands. These results confirm the promising potential of these quantum dots for applications in quantum technologies.

</details>


### [5] [Incoherent dark excitons extend Floquet-like physics across time and momentum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38156)
*Xing Zhu,Jacques Hawecker,Enrico Perfetto,Vivek Pareek,David R. Bacon,Harley Suchiang,Nanami Tomoda,Amrita Mitra,Takumi Fukuda,Sebastien Roux,Ouri Karni,Jack Featherstone,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Michael K. L. Man,Julien Madéo,Felipe H. da Jornada,Gianluca Stefanucci,Keshav M. Dani*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用WSe₂/MoS₂异质结中的暗层间激子密集非相干布居，实现了近纳秒寿命的类Floquet能带杂化，且可通过激子密度与扭转角调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Floquet效应依赖相干、时间周期驱动，仅在驱动相干时间内（亚皮秒）存在，限制其实用性；理论预言超越平均场图像后效应可长寿命存在，但缺乏实验验证。

Method: 采用时间与角分辨光电子能谱（tr-ARPES）测量WSe₂/MoS₂异质结，通过调控暗层间激子密度与扭转角，观测价带及其复制带色散演化。

Result: 暗激子非相干布居使导带与价带能量复制副本发生杂化，类Floquet现象持续近1 ns；随激子密度增加，价带及其复制带由倒抛物线演化为墨西哥帽色散，标志非零静态耦合且耦合强度随激子密度线性增长；改变扭转角可使具有净动量的暗激子产生在动量上也偏移的复制带。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了超越相干零动量驱动、稳健且长寿命的电子态调控途径，拓展了Floquet工程的设计自由度（能量与动量双重偏移）。

Abstract: Floquet phenomena - where a coherent, time-periodic drive hybridizes the conduction band with energy-replicated copies of the valence band - have been demonstrated with intense optical fields and, more recently, other bosonic excitations. However, within mean-field approximations and the current understanding of Floquet phenomena, these effects survive only for the coherence time of the drive, limiting practical use and current demonstrations to the sub-picosecond regime. Here, consistent with recent theoretical predictions beyond the mean-field picture, using time- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy in WSe$_2$/MoS$_2$ heterostructures, we show that incoherent, dense populations of dark interlayer excitons hybridize the conduction band with energy-replicated copies of the valence band, mimicking Floquet-like phenomena - now persisting for nearly a nanosecond. With increasing interlayer exciton density, the valence band and its replica evolve from an inverted-parabolic to a Mexican-hat dispersion, a signature of the predicted non-zero static coupling that hybridizes the bands and scales linearly with the exciton density. By tuning twist angle, we further show that dark excitons with net momentum create replicas that hybridize with bands offset not just in energy but also in momentum, providing another powerful degree of freedom beyond conventional Floquet engineering. These results establish a robust, long-lived route to engineering electronic states, beyond a coherent, zero-momentum drive.

</details>


### [6] [Charge Filling-Dependent Valley Polarization and its Dynamics Driven by Correlated Interlayer Exciton States in WSe2/WS2 Heterobilayer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38251)
*Zhiwei Li,Haonan Wang,Yubei Xiang,Wataru Idehara,Hailin Liu,Shinichiro Matano,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Kazunari Matsuda*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究WSe2/WS2莫尔异质双层中层间激子（IX）随电荷填充依赖的谷弛豫动力学，揭示整数填充（n=±1）处辐射衰变/复合速率增强、谷弛豫速率显著降低的反常行为，归因于载流子局域化导致的波函数交叠与声子散射抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 莫尔体系中强关联电子/激子态已被广泛研究，但其对谷自由度及其动力学的影响尚属空白；谷自由度动力学对基础理解与谷电子学应用至关重要。

Method: 结合时间分辨光谱学与Monte Carlo模拟，测量WSe2/WS2莫尔异质双层中层间激子随电荷填充的谷弛豫动力学，追踪跨越Mott转变的IX动力学演化。

Result: 在整数填充因子n（=-1）处IX的辐射衰变与复合速率增强；在n=±1处谷弛豫速率显著下降；波函数交叠减小与声子散射过程受抑，抑制了非辐射复合与谷间散射。

Conclusion: 整数填充时引入载流子在莫尔格点局域化，改变IX复合路径并增强激子相干性，从而产生上述反常行为；表明激子与谷动力学可作为莫尔强关联态的灵敏探针，并为二维量子材料中激子相干与谷电子学性质的调控提供途径。

Abstract: Moiré heterostructures of transition metal dichalcogenides provide an emerging platform for studying the strongly correlated electronic and excitonic states. The valley degree of freedom and its dynamics in the strongly correlated electronic and excitonic states in the moiré system are crucial for fundamental understanding and applications of valleytronics. Although the strongly correlated states in moiré systems have been extensively investigated, their impact on valley degrees of freedom and its dynamics remains unexplored. Here, we study the charge filling-dependent valley relaxation dynamics of interlayer excitons (IXs) in WSe2/WS2 moiré heterobilayer system. The evolution of moiré IXs dynamics across the Mott transition is elucidated by combining time-resolved optical spectroscopy with Monte Carlo simulation. Our results show that the radiative decay and recombination rate of IXs are enhanced at the integer filling factors n (=-1). Moreover, the valley relaxation rate of IXs at n=$\pm$1 is significantly decreased. The resulting reduction in wavefunction overlap and phonon scattering process suppresses the nonradiative recombination and intervalley scattering of IXs. These anomalous behaviors are attributed to the modification of IXs recombination pathways and enhance excitonic coherence due to the localization of introduced charge carriers at integer filling of moiré site. Our results explore exciton and valley dynamics as sensitive probes of strongly correlated states in moiré systems and highlight possible ways to control excitonic coherence and valleytronic properties in two-dimensional quantum materials.

</details>


### [7] [The Flexoelectric Twist: Persistence of Heavy Fermion Picture in Small-Angle Twisted Bilayer Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38305)
*Qirong Yao,Ka Cheuk Fan,Tianyu Qiao,Hao Shi,Xi Dai,Berthold Jäck*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 小角扭转双层石墨烯（θ≈0.3–0.7°）中，摩尔晶格重构与原子晶格膨胀通过柔性电耦合共同作用，在AA位点产生局域重费米子f态并与AB/BA位点的巡游c电子共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一魔角附近TBG的平带已被广泛研究，但在更小扭角（θ<1°）下晶格弛豫会深刻改变摩尔势场；不同长度尺度的摩尔晶格重构如何通过机电耦合影响电子结构尚不清楚。

Method: 对θ≈0.3–0.7°的小角TBG器件进行扫描隧道显微镜/扫描隧道谱（STM/STS）测量，获得空间分辨的dI/dV谱；并在唯象层面构建包含摩尔晶格重构与原子晶格膨胀的模型。

Result: 在AA位点观察到显著的dI/dV峰，谱线相对电荷中性点不对称；证实摩尔尺度晶格重构与AA位点原子晶格膨胀；模型表明摩尔重构支持AA位点局域的紧致重费米子f态，原子晶格膨胀引入柔性电响应、显式破缺粒子-空穴对称性并将f态下移，使其与AB/BA位点的巡游c电子能量上共存；空间分辨谱定量呈现该共存。

Conclusion: 柔性电效应显著调控小角TBG的低能电子结构；小角TBG可作为研究相互作用近藤晶格问题的新型摩尔材料平台，并可通过扭角与静电背栅分别调节f占据数和c电子密度。

Abstract: Twisted bilayer graphene (TBG) near the first magic angle ($θ\approx1.05^{\circ}$) hosts flat bands that provide a fertile ground for exploring strongly correlated and topological phases. At smaller twist angles ($θ<1^{\circ}$), attice relaxation profoundly alters the moiré landscape. The extent to which moiré lattice reconstruction at different length scales affects the electronic structure through electro-mechanical coupling remains unresolved. Here, using scanning tunneling microscopy on small-angle TBG devices ($θ\approx0.3-0.7^{\circ}$), we observe a pronounced $dI/dV$ peak at the AA site of the moiré lattice on top of a spectrum that is asymmetric about the charge neutrality point, lattice reconstruction over moiré lattice length scales, as well as a dilation of the atomic lattice at the AA sites. We demonstrate at a phenomenological level that the inclusion of moiré lattice reconstruction supports compact localized heavy fermion $f-$ states localized at AA sites. The additional inclusion of atomic lattice dilation enerates a strong flexo-electric response that explicitly breaks particle-hole symmetry, pushing compact, localized $f$-states at the AA sites down in energy. Consequently, these heavy fermion states energetically coexist with itinerant $c$-electrons at the AB/BA sites. Our spatially resolved spectroscopic measurements quantitatively visualize this coexistence, confirming the persistence of the heavy fermion model in the small-angle regime $θ<1^{\circ}$. Our findings provide microscopic insights into how flexo-electricity affects the low-energy electronic structure of small-angle TBG. More broadly, our study establishes small-angle TBG as a new class of moiré materials to explore the interacting Kondo lattice problem with high tunability of the $f-$occupation and $c-$density through twist-angle and electrostatic back gates, respectively.

</details>


### [8] [Time evolution in the multi-component dynamical Keldysh models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38338)
*M. N. Kiselev*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 构建多分量随机高斯场动力学的含时演化算符，分别处理SU(2)、U(1)、Z_2三类对称性；在无限关联时间（Keldysh模型）极限下证明非零均值二分量随机场的统计服从Rice分布，并精确求解对角/非对角单粒子Green函数的耦合线性微分方程组，用连分式方法处理有限关联时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多分量随机场（含长关联时间）驱动的量子动力学缺少可精确处理的解析框架，尤其缺乏与对称性分类（非阿贝尔与阿贝尔类）对应、并涵盖单分量/双分量/三分量噪声的统一描述。

Method: 构造长关联多分量随机高斯场下的时间演化算符；按SU(2)（三分量）、U(1)（两分量）、Z_2（单分量）三类对称性组织分析；从无限关联时间的Keldysh极限出发，导出并求解单粒子Green函数（对角与非对角）的耦合线性微分方程组；以连分式（continuous fractions）方法推广至有限关联时间。

Result: 在Keldysh极限下，非零均值二分量随机场的统计由Rice分布描述；单粒子Green函数的耦合线性微分方程组被精确导出并求解；有限关联时间效应可通过连分式展开系统纳入。

Conclusion: Keldysh模型可作为按对称性分类精确求解随机场量子动力学的可控出发点，Rice分布刻画相干分量叠加的噪声统计，连分式方法为有限关联时间提供了自然的系统扩展路径。

Abstract: We construct a time evolution operator for a dynamical quantum models with a multi-component random Gaussian field characterized by a long correlation time. We analyze three paradigmatic cases: non-Abelian $SU(2)$ symmetry class for the three-component Gaussian noise and Abelian symmetry classes $U(1)$ and $Z_2$ for the two- and one-component noises correspondingly. Starting with a limiting case of the infinite correlation time (Keldysh model) we shown that statistics of the two-component random field with a non-zero mean is described by the Rice distribution. We derived and solved the system of coupled linear differential equations for the exact diagonal and off-diagonal single-particle Green's functions. We showed how the finite time correlation in Keldysh models are accounted by a continuous fractions method.

</details>


### [9] [Time Reflection-Refraction Crossover at Topological Kramers Edge States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40442)
*Junpeng Hou,Ya-Jie Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在保持时间反演对称的量子自旋霍尔相内，空间均匀的时间调制可实现时间维度上的反射—折射跨越，并借此在固定动量下相干转换反向传播的边缘通道，从而操控拓扑边缘态与量子几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间反演对称性保护螺旋边缘态免受静态非磁背散射，但这也意味着静态手段难以调控这类受保护通道。作者希望找到一种不破坏瞬时哈密顿量时间反演对称性、又能有效控制边缘态输运的手段，即时间调制。

Abstract: Free fermions coupled to particle gain and loss relax to a Gaussian steady state, in which every natural orbital has its own occupation. Each orbital is filled and emptied at its own rates, and the Hamiltonian, moving particles across the levels during their lifetime, averages the differences between the orbitals. At large system size the distribution depends on the grain Hamiltonian only through its density of states $ν(E)$. The spread is bounded by the baths alone: even Hamiltonians with unbounded, heavy-tailed spectra cannot widen it beyond this bound. Abstract: We study the freedom in choosing Wannier centers and the associated symmetry representations in noninteracting Wannierizable insulators. We compare Wyckoff deformations of atomic configurations with symmetry-preserving adiabatic deformations of occupied states, both with and without adding common occupied atomic orbitals. Explicit examples show that stable Wyckoff equivalence and adiabatic equivalence at fixed filling do not imply each other. In spinless $Fm'm'm'$, an Euler class distinguishes stably Wyckoff-equivalent configurations. In spinless $P\overline1'$, adiabatically equivalent configurations remain distinct under stable Wyckoff deformations. We also classify when stable Wyckoff and stable adiabatic equivalence coincide. They agree in two or fewer dimensions and in three-dimensional spinful systems. These groups preserve spacetime orientation and have a point whose site-symmetry group contains $222$, $\overline1'$, or $\overline4'$.

We find an upper bound on $ W $, a nonreciprocity requiring both effects and the absence of freely propagating modes, depending on the orientation of $ \mathbf{G} $, for the planar interface, and, for the cylindrical one, a novel coupling between the linear and orbital angular momenta of the modes, which already arises from the chiral magnetic effect alone and persists when the anomalous Hall effect is also present. This level structure was initially calculated within the Kang--Wise model, which employs the standard boundary condition of vanishing carrier wave functions at the NC surface. This model, however, does not allow one to account for the effect of the NC surface on the level structure. Here, the effect of the NC surface is modeled using general boundary conditions (GBCs) imposed on the envelope wave functions at the surface. We show that the surface properties modify the size dependence of the electron and hole confinement levels and lead to an additional spin--orbit splitting of the $1P_{h,e}$ levels. Using the GBCs, we are able to quantitatively describe the size dependence of the lowest optical transitions in bare PbS and PbSe NCs and explain the differences between the absorption spectra of bare and Cl-passivated PbS NCs

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 本文提出HERA——面向量子器件低温输运测量的自主智能体，其运行依托实验室成员整理的知识库（含测量协议、软件工具与文献参考）。评估采用三类设计：(1) 针对两个多层石墨烯器件堆叠顺序，与专家进行盲法对照试验（专家结论密封）；(2) 知识库消融实验；(3) 数百次离线重放，由不同模型在不实际执行的情况下设计试验的核心测量。

Result: HERA在两个测试器件上的结论与专家密封结论一致。消融与重放研究表明，在具备知识库的情况下，各模型设计的测量方案更优、推理更严谨。为检验可扩展性，HERA被指派在另一材料体系中寻找并表征超导性，仅需数小时专家时间开发新实验协议，之后HERA即可自主完成发现与表征。

Conclusion: 配有为任务定制的知识库时，语言模型智能体能够开展可靠的实验科学工作；知识库是提升跨模型测量设计与推理质量的关键因素，且智能体可借助少量专家投入快速迁移至新问题。

Abstract: Large language model agents have begun to carry out laboratory measurements, but their scientific conclusions are useful only if they hold up under scrutiny. Demonstrating this requires controlled studies because models are stochastic and the context a model is given steers its conclusions. Here we introduce HERA, an autonomous agent designed to carry out cryogenic transport measurements of quantum devices. HERA draws on a knowledge base curated by lab members that holds measurement protocols, software tools, and literature references. We assessed HERA with a blinded trial against an expert on the stacking order of two multilayer graphene devices, an ablation of its knowledge base, and hundreds of offline replays in which models designed the trial's central measurement without running it. HERA's conclusions matched the expert's sealed conclusions on both tested devices. The ablation and the replay studies measured the impact of the knowledge base across different models, showing that with it every model designed better measurements and reasoned more soundly. To test how readily HERA extends to a new problem, we tasked it with finding and characterizing superconductivity in a different material system. This required a few hours of expert time to develop a new experimental protocol, after which HERA did both autonomously. These results show that, with a knowledge base designed for the task, language model agents are capable of sound experimental science.

</details>

Abstract: Quantum three-wave mixing is an important resource in superconducting quantum circuits. The traditional implementation uses Josephson junction arrays and is activated by magnetic flux. Here we introduce the Berry curvature dipole (BCD) quantum circuit element, which achieves quantum three-wave mixing without breaking time-reversal symmetry. This quantum three-wave mixing results from the BCD nonlinear Hall effect in noncentrosymmetric quantum materials taken to a dissipationless, quantum coherent few-photon regime. We show how nonlinear Hall materials can be integrated to superconducting microwave circuits to accomplish multiphoton vacuum Rabi splittings and dynamical stabilization of bosonic Schrödinger cat states. Such BCD circuit quantum electrodynamics are a concrete exemplar of a physical resource for quantum information processing enabled by nonlinear response in quantum materials.

Abstract: We investigate the thermally driven magnon spin current across a junction between a $d$-wave altermagnet (AM) and a ferromagnet (FM) or an antiferromagnet (AFM).
  The current consists of a component independent of the interface angle and an angularly dependent component with the $d$-wave form.
  For a sublattice-symmetric interface, the two junctions behave oppositely under reversal of the altermagnetic domain: in the FM/AM junction the angularly dependent component is odd and cancels on averaging over the domains, whereas in the AFM/AM junction the angularly independent component vanishes by symmetry and the angularly dependent component survives the domain average at a fixed Néel vector of the AFM.
  Both follow from a selective tunneling: a pair of spins bonded across the interface transmits magnons when the spins are parallel and not when antiparallel, which makes the AFM/AM junction a magnon valve: the configuration in which the bonded spins are antiparallel blocks the current up to the sublattice admixture of the magnons.
  The anisotropic tunneling is detectable as the sign reversal of the current between the interfaces normal to $[310]$ and $[130]$, and the valve reads the Néel vector of the altermagnet relative to that of the antiferromagnet through the magnitude of the current at a fixed interface.

</details>

Abstract: Solid-state color centers embedded into mechanical resonators, where orbital states mediate interactions between spins and long-lived phonons, are a promising platform for hybrid quantum systems. The negatively charged silicon vacancy center in diamond (SiV-) is particularly favorable for strain-coupling to nanomechanical resonators because of its large spin-strain susceptibility and compatibility with device integration. Tensioned nanomechanical resonators offer an attractive route due to their ultra-long mechanical coherence times enabled by diluting defect-induced dissipation, though it is unclear if the same dilution effect undermines strain-mediated coupling to material defect centers. Here, we experimentally demonstrate a tensioned diamond nanobeam platform that enables enhanced strain coupling to SiV- orbital states. Through monitoring the optical response of the SiVs to the static and dynamical strain of the mechanical resonator, we observe that dynamical strain localizes near the clamps of these resonators under static strain. This allows us to enhance SiV-mechanical coupling and the mechanical coherence simultaneously, and provide a SiV-strain sensitivity with greatly improved accuracy over previous literature values. Thus, our work opens a pathway towards seconds-long coherence hybrid quantum systems utilizing point defects in diamond nanomechanics for quantum information processing.

Abstract: Multilayer graphenes have emerged as a versatile avenue towards exploring novel electronic phases of matter. Recently, nanoscale multilayer graphene islands have been realized experimentally, where the island is less than 50 nm in diameter and distinct in layer configuration from the contact portion of the same graphene sheets, the latter of which act as leads for transport probes. Here, we theoretically investigate how the proximity between these islands and their chemically identical environment modifies the electronic structure. Within the tight-binding approximation, we find that the proximity effect is enhanced by the closely matched momenta between both parts, creating an unusually strong band renormalization which persists as the island size is increased, and only limited by the coherence length. An effective single-unit-cell Hamiltonian to capture these modifications is derived, expressed in terms of interlayer hopping parameters that scale with the relative island area. We predict a substantially altered low-energy density of states for 3- and 4-layer graphene islands, demonstrating the tunability of the electronic structure by spatial size.

</details>

Conclusion: 准周期调制、AFM序、能带填充与电子关联协同调控持续电流的幅度与自旋分辨性质，产生多重增强区及相互作用诱导的回归行为；MFT在该体系中可给出与ED/DMRG一致的定性可靠描述。

Abstract: We investigate persistent charge and spin currents in a one-dimensional quantum ring threaded by an Aharonov-Bohm (AB) flux, considering generalized Aubry-André-Harper (AAH) modulation with staggered onsite energies and antiferromagnetic (AFM) ordering in both the noninteracting and interacting regimes, with the latter treated using a self-consistent Hartree-Fock mean field theory (MFT). At half filling, two distinct current-enhancement peaks emerge as the modulation strength increases, with quasiperiodic hopping modulation playing a crucial role in establishing the two-peak structure within a restricted parameter regime. Spin-resolved bond-current profiles reveal a switch in the dominant spin contribution between the two peaks, while the inverse participation ratio (IPR) and flux-dependent spin-resolved ground-state energies provide complementary insights into the spatial characteristics of the eigenstates and their spin-dependent flux sensitivity. At quarter filling, the spin current exhibits a single enhancement peak, in contrast to the two-peak response observed at half filling. The Hubbard interaction affects the two half-filled peaks differently: it suppresses the primary peak, whereas the secondary peak exhibits a nonmonotonic, re-entrant response, with the current recovering at finite interaction strength. The interacting results are further characterized through the Drude weight, and comparisons with exact diagonalization (ED) and density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) calculations for a finite half-filled system support the reliability of the MFT description. Our findings elucidate how quasiperiodic modulation, AFM order, band filling, and electronic correlations collectively govern the magnitude and spin-resolved character of persistent currents, giving rise to multiple enhancement regimes and interaction-induced re-entrant behavior.

</details>

Motivation: 高阶Chern绝缘体具有每条手征边缘态对应一个Chern数单位的多个无耗散边缘通道，但其边缘集体（等离激元）模式的物理，尤其是Chern数如何调控等离激元诱导的磁响应，尚不清楚；本文旨在厘清Chern数对边缘等离激元及其诱发的轨道磁化的控制作用，并与常规边缘等离激元形成对照。

Method: 采用含库仑相互作用的半经典模型，考察Chern数𝒞与-𝒞畴壁处的边缘等离激元；通过分析边缘等离激元的色散关系，并利用其产生的椭圆偏振电场驱动体内电子做回旋运动（逆法拉第效应），计算静态轨道磁化，分析其在空间、频率与温度上的依赖关系。

Result: 长波极限下等离激元色散为线性，速度随𝒞线性增长；边缘等离激元的电场向体内衰减并驱动电子做圆轨道运动，产生静态轨道磁化。拓扑边缘等离激元的磁化空间分布由边缘态有限宽度与等离激元波长两个竞争尺度决定；在固定边缘电荷振幅下随𝒞显著增强；畴壁处低频趋于有限平台、高频按1/ω²衰减；温度依赖非单调，在中间温度出现极大值。

Conclusion: Chern数可有效调控等离激元诱导的磁化，拓扑边缘等离激元在磁响应上与常规边缘等离激元存在本质差异，为以拓扑量子数工程化设计边缘磁光/磁化效应提供了依据。

Abstract: Higher Chern insulators host multiple dissipationless chiral edge states, one per unit of Chern number. We study the collective plasmon modes of these edge states at a domain wall between Chern numbers $\mathcal{C}$ and $-\mathcal{C}$, using a semiclassical model with Coulomb interactions. The plasmon dispersion is linear at long wavelengths, with a velocity that increases linearly with $\mathcal{C}$. As in conventional two-dimensional metals, the edge plasmon generates an elliptically polarized electric field that decays into the bulk and drives electrons in circular orbits, producing a static orbital magnetization (inverse Faraday effect). For topological edge plasmons, the magnetization has a nontrivial spatial profile set by two competing length scales: the finite extent of the edge states and the plasmon wavelength. At fixed edge-charge amplitude, it grows strongly with $\mathcal{C}$. At the domain wall, it approaches a finite plateau at low frequency and decays as $1/ω^2$ at high frequency. Its temperature dependence is nonmonotonic, with a maximum at an intermediate temperature. These results show how the Chern number controls plasmon-induced magnetization, in contrast to conventional edge plasmons.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脑以神经元与突触实现模式识别与自适应学习，突触强度的可塑性是学习与记忆的基础。在硬件上实现复杂神经网络需要兼具类突触可调电导与非易失存储的器件，而eNVM恰好满足高密度、低功耗神经形态电路的需求。

Method: 采用综述性分析：以生物神经元—突触机制为类比框架，系统梳理ReRAM、相变存储器（PCM）、自旋电子类存储器件等eNVM类型，从电导调制模拟突触可塑性、非易失性、可微缩性等维度评估其在神经形态计算中的适用性。

Result: eNVM器件通过电导调制可有效模拟突触可塑性，兼具非易失存储与良好可微缩性，适合构建大规模神经形态架构；同时各类器件在集成到神经形态系统时仍面临自身挑战，但近期已有显著进展。

Conclusion: eNVM是神经形态计算实现致密、低功耗硬件的理想候选，未来需在器件挑战与系统集成之间取得平衡，以推动大规模神经形态架构的落地。

Abstract: This chapter explores the critical role of emerging non-volatile memory (eNVM) devices in advancing neuromorphic computing. Inspired by the human brain's remarkable ability to perform complex tasks like pattern recognition and adaptation, neuromorphic computing offers immense potential for implementing complex neural networks (NNs) in real hardware. Despite ongoing research into the brain's complexities, its fundamental operation relies on neurons (processing units communicating via electrochemical signals) and synapses (junctions between neurons). The modifiable strength of the synapse is key to learning and memory functionality, allowing the brain to adapt and rewire itself. For dense and low-power circuits in neuromorphic computing, these eNVM devices are ideal due to their synaptic-like behavior and non-volatile information storage. Properties such as conductance modulation of these devices emulate synaptic plasticity, which is crucial for learning, and their scalability makes them promising for large-scale neuromorphic architectures. This chapter will delve into the various types of eNVM devices such as resistive RAM (ReRAM), phase-change memory (PCM), spintronics-based memory devices, and their applications in neuromorphic computing, discussing their challenges and opportunities, and highlighting recent advances in their integration within neuromorphic systems.

</details>

Abstract: Twisting two stacked graphene layers superimposes a moiré superlattice that reshapes their electronic structure, yet how it shapes the collective optical response of finite, nanoscale bilayers remains largely unexplored.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 0-型对称性$G$与1-型对称性$\Pi_2$可混合成2-群$\mathcal{G}$，但此类高阶群对称性及其反常缺乏具体的晶格（微观）表述，缺少可直接操作的定义与反常探测手段。

Abstract: Preparing complex quantum states of light for quantum information processing tasks often proceeds by converting from simpler states via experimentally accessible operations. We provide fundamental bounds on such probabilistic conversions, both exact and approximate. To do so, we establish a theory of weak, fully relative majorization that compares the disorder of phase-space functions. Our framework is versatile and can be tailored to different systems and sets of allowed operations. Focusing on Wigner functions in multimode photonic systems, we establish rigorous no-go results and tradeoff relations for exact and approximate conversions involving squeezed states, cat states, Gottesman--Kitaev--Preskill states, and Fock states. In doing so, we derive novel bounds and recover known tight ones. Our results demonstrate that phase-space disorder imposes strong limitations on photonic state preparation.

TL;DR: 本文刻画 Grover 算法全流程中非稳定子性（magic）的增长：无编译的每次迭代末 magic 随规模保持 intensiveness；把电路分解为基本门时，已知解可实现 magic 有界的有效分解，而非知解实现（如 3-SAT）产生 volume-law 非稳定子性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grover 算法是量子优势的典型范例，但其相对于可高效模拟的稳定子态的量子复杂度（即 magic）如何演化尚不清楚；同时实际运行需编译为基本门，编译引入的额外 magic 开销及其对可观模拟性的影响也未被量化。

Method: 以稳定子 Rényi 熵为度量，首先计算每次 Grover 迭代末的编译无关 magic，给出解析预测并分析其随系统规模的标度；随后研究将电路分解为基本门（含大量 Toffoli 门）所带来的 magic 开销，分别考察已知解与解未知（agnostic）两类实现，并以 3-SAT 作为实例。

Abstract: Grover's algorithm is a textbook example of quantum advantage, providing a quadratic query speedup over classical methods for unstructured search. In this Letter, we characterize the non-stabilizerness, or magic, developed throughout Grover's protocol, quantifying quantum complexity with respect to the efficiently simulable stabilizer states. First, we compute the compilation-independent magic at the end of each Grover iteration, deriving an analytic prediction for the stabilizer Rényi entropy and proving that it remains intensive as the system size grows. We then investigate the magic overhead due to decomposing the circuit into elementary gates. When the solution to the search problem is known, we show that an efficient decomposition exists that keeps magic bounded despite using an extensive number of Toffoli gates. In contrast, agnostic implementations are expected to generate volume-law non-stabilizerness, as we confirm for the 3-SAT problem, which rules out efficient simulation by stabilizer-based methods in practically relevant cases. Our findings clarify the growth of magic in Grover's algorithm and the role of solution-awareness in its circuit compilation.

</details>

Abstract: The characterization of possible transformations between quantum states and the quantification of entanglement are central for understanding quantum resources. Explicit results are, however, scarce since they require complicated optimizations over transformation protocols or state decompositions. Our approach can also be used to determine non-polynomial measures, such as the entanglement of formation, and to characterize the dimensionality of entanglement. Moreover, extracting the decomposition allows us to formulate criteria to determine state transformations under local operations and classical communication (LOCC). We review modern versions of Shor's algorithm for RSA, ECDLP and FFDLP, and make their logical circuits and trade-offs explicit. We design a parity-Cnot gate for cat qubits, extend a multi-target Cnot gate to the LDPC-cat architecture, and adapt an active injection procedure to cat qubits. Building on these primitives, we adapt to cat qubits the adder from Gidney's 2025 resource estimate for factoring RSA-2048. We combine our adder compilation and a coarser estimate for the phaseup with the factory designs and lookup compilation from the FeMoco resource estimation of Kroell et al. Together, these lead to chip layouts and schedules for factoring RSA-2048.

Reusing the compiled operations, solving ECDLP on secp256k1 requires at most 30 000 cat qubits and 1 hour, and FFDLP-512, which we propose as an intermediate benchmark, about 6 700 cat qubits and 21 minutes. Trading space for time, RSA-2048 runs in 1.25 hours on about 611 000 cat qubits. The no-bit-flip requirement (a bit-flip time of about 100 years) can be relaxed to a bit-flip time of about one hour by adding a concatenation layer with a distance-3 bit-flip code, in which case RSA-2048 needs about 45 000 cat qubits.

We organize them around a common Bell-inspired methodology that extends beyond quantum nonlocality, and emphasize how increasingly rich operational structures change which physical assumptions can be tested.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: With D. Aerts, we showed in 2014, within the extended Bloch representation of quantum mechanics, that the Born rule can be understood as resulting from a universal average over all the possible ways in which the measurement-interactions can be selected. The key is to recognize that a universal measurement is a random way of selecting a measurement which does not privilege any portion of the structure describing the available interactions, a property we call indifference. We prove that every indifferent universal measurement, however constructed, produces exactly the Born probabilities: the universal average is unique, and the cellular construction of 2014 is one among infinitely many ways of obtaining it, exact at every finite resolution. Abstract: Random unitaries scramble quantum information into highly nonlocal degrees of freedom, thereby providing protection from local noise. However, as demonstrated by Turkeshi and Sierant (Phys. Rev. Lett. 132, 140401, 2024 and arXiv:2603.00235), this protection may fail once local noise grows sufficiently strong. These works describe a phase transition in a noisy random encoding-decoding circuit model between an error-protecting phase, in which encoded information is recoverable, and an error-vulnerable phase, in which encoded information is lost, with the transition occurring at a critical coherent noise angle $α_c \approx 1.14$. Here, we present the first experimental observation of this transition, conducted on IBM's ibm_pittsburgh Heron r3 device for system sizes of up to $N = 26$ qubits. We detect this transition via three order parameters: fidelity, Rényi-2 entanglement entropy, and stabilizer Rényi-2 entropy. For the latter two, we employ randomized measurement protocols to construct estimators, and for all three, we rely on zero-noise extrapolation and M3 SPAM error mitigation. We then jointly model the finite-size scaling collapse across all three order parameters with a Gaussian process regression, yielding a critical angle $α_c \approx 1.01$, well below the theoretical value. Inputting noiseless simulated data into the same model yields $α_c \approx 1.05$, implying that the discrepancy between experiment and theory is due to our encoding circuits, which are not deep enough to be truly Haar-random. Finally, we observe that the same model cannot adequately identify critical behavior in unmitigated, raw experimental data. This indicates that error mitigation techniques are crucial for detecting the transition on hardware.

Abstract: Passive loss removes probability from a system and therefore seems unable to improve an unconditional target. This intuition is exact for classical population dynamics under sink-only removal: passive killing obeys a finite-strength monotonicity principle even after optimization over the remaining controls. Here we show that coherent quantum dynamics can violate this classical monotonicity because coherent contributions to the final target can interfere destructively. We derive a trajectory-level criterion that determines when such a detrimental contribution is physically accessible to passive loss and accumulates into a net unconditional benefit. The criterion is encoded in a response operator constructed entirely from the lossless dynamics: its spectrum determines whether weak passive loss can help, while its principal eigenvector identifies the optimal environmental mode. Applying this criterion to vibronically assisted transfer, we identify a beneficial mode formed by coherently combining two individually detrimental local loss directions. The predicted mode remains near-optimal at finite loss, producing an \(8.73\%\) enhancement over the independently optimized lossless benchmark, compared with \(9.52\%\) under full finite-loss optimization. Our results establish how coherent quantum dynamics can evade classical passive-loss monotonicity and provide a general task-dependent principle for designing beneficial passive environments from lossless dynamics.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在容错Clifford+T电路中，布尔oracle消耗T门和额外qubit；4量子比特及以上的精确Toffoli门都需要额外qubit。Gosset-Kothari-Zhang(GKZ)证明对每次调用采样新鲜随机电路可使Toffoli门的T-count与规模无关，但其采样器直接实现每比特精度约需一个额外qubit，资源开销大。作者希望消除这一额外qubit开销。

Method: 在门的自身控制位上原地(in-place)计算采样的奇偶(parities)，而非使用独立的额外qubit；并推广到仿射子空间指示函数的异或（如相等测试、线性方程检验）。通过分析随机化无测量电路的T-count下界与最优性，以及精确布尔函数情形与精确多控Toffoli门T-count常数项的关系进行理论刻画。

Result: 门足够大时，原地计算消除了全部额外qubit：每随机电路每比特精度约用8个T门，仅借用门自身的空闲输入；比保留一个干净anchilla的最佳无测量变体仅多一个T门每电路。同样适用于仿射子空间指示函数的异或。对无测量随机电路，在加性常数意义下这一结果与最优相差不超过8倍（即使允许额外qubit）；当每随机电路精确计算布尔函数时，渐近闭合该因子等价于确定精确多控Toffoli门T-count的首项常数。此外，固定密度的随机集合指示函数oracle在常数误差下需指数多个T门，而同密度仿射子空间仅需常数个T门；额外qubit仅能将指数代价二次地降低。

Conclusion: 原地采样奇偶可完全消除额外qubit开销，代价微小（仅多1个T门/电路），并将Toffoli及其他仿射指示函数异或的T-count改进为与规模无关且近最优（加性常数下8倍以内）。结果揭示了精确多控Toffoli门T-count首项常数这一开放问题的等价关系，并区分了随机集合与仿射子空间oracle在T-count上的本质差异。

Abstract: In fault-tolerant Clifford+T circuits, a Boolean oracle costs T gates and extra qubits, and on four or more qubits every exact Toffoli gate needs an extra qubit. Gosset, Kothari and Zhang showed that sampling a fresh random circuit for each call makes the T-count of the Toffoli gate independent of its size, but a direct implementation of their sampler uses about one extra qubit per bit of precision. We show that computing the sampled parities in place, on the gate's own controls, removes every extra qubit once the gate is large enough: each random circuit then uses about 8 T gates per bit of precision and borrows only the gate's own idle inputs. This is one T gate more per circuit than the best measurement-free variant that keeps one clean ancilla. The same holds for exclusive-ors of indicators of affine subspaces, such as equality tests and checks of linear equations. For randomized circuits that do not measure, this is within a factor of eight of optimal up to an additive constant, even with extra qubits, and when each random circuit computes a Boolean function exactly, closing the factor asymptotically is equivalent to determining the leading constant in the T-count of exact multi-controlled Toffoli gates. Finally, the oracle of the indicator of a random set of fixed density needs exponentially many T gates at constant error, while that of an affine subspace of the same density needs a constant number, and extra qubits reduce the exponential cost only quadratically.

</details>

Abstract: Magic, a quantum resource distinct from entanglement, measures departure from states stabilized by products of Pauli operators. Such stabilizer states can be maximally entangled yet admit efficient classical description: quantum advantage requires magic. We develop a powerful framework to evaluate the degree of magic in generic many-body states. We use it to study the fate of magic under thermal and dynamical evolution. In the transverse field Ising model, we find a thermal sudden death of magic that saturates with system size, and occurs after the death of entanglement. We reach similar conclusions in generic spin chains, and in the Kitaev quantum spin liquid on the honeycomb lattice, where the threshold shows an intricate dependence on subregion shape. We then study how magic equilibrates after a quantum quench. In quenches of generic spin chains, the magic of a subregion typically dies before entanglement, contrary to thermal trajectories. An explanation is given in terms of the geometry of non-magical states. As a third type of trajectory, we examine the fate of magic with separation with an appropriate notion of non-local magic and establish a sudden death. Finally, we show how to experimentally witness a volume law of frame-robust magic in quantum materials by using neutron scattering.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相干求和将多个量子线路分支叠加，干涉效应会改变训练信号（梯度/损失），而现有分析（如贫化/浓度现象）通常针对单一线路或假设Haar随机，缺乏对LCU结构与干涉项如何影响梯度方差的精确刻画，因此需要区分单次LCU与重复交错LCU两类结构并给出定量分析。

Method: 1) 区分单次线性组合单元（LCU）与反复交错分支操作与ancilla混合的线路；2) 对单次LCU，用矩阵均值为零的独立分支论证梯度方差下界，证明其与门位置无关且不要求Haar随机；3) 推导精确的损失与梯度公式，分解出分支间干涉附加项，其中包括依赖相对系数相位的项（当分支集合对乘以 i·I 不变时该项消失），并用四比特matchgate实例说明其可非零；4) 对完全特殊酉ancilla控制的重复交错，由分支的结合代数确定生成的李代数及中心相位方向；5) 用matchgate实例分析采样假设下的浓度行为，并推导系数选取规则与后选择、测量误差和近似矩的界。

Result: 单次LCU下总方差至少为分支方差乘以该分支系数模的四次方，此界在所有门位置成立且无需Haar随机性；得到精确损失与梯度公式，量化干涉的额外贡献，其中相对相位依赖项在特定对称性下消失、在四比特matchgate例子中可非零；完全特殊酉ancilla控制下重复交错的生成李代数由分支结合代数决定，该控制性结果仅在附加采样假设下预测浓度；matchgate实例显示单次LCU方差为多项式级，而在联合Haar随机采样下呈指数浓度。

Abstract: A coherent sum of quantum circuits introduces interference that can alter the training signal. We distinguish a single linear combination of unitaries (LCU) from a circuit that repeatedly alternates branch operations and ancilla mixing. For a single-pass LCU, independent branches with zero matrix means cannot erase the differentiated branch's gradient variance: the total variance is at least the branch variance times the fourth power of its coefficient magnitude. This bound holds at any gate position and does not require the circuit unitaries to be Haar-random. Beyond this lower bound, we derive exact loss and gradient formulas that quantify the additional contributions from interference between branches. One of these terms depends on relative coefficient phases and vanishes when the branch ensemble is invariant under multiplication by $i \mathbb{I}$; a four-qubit matchgate example shows that it can be nonzero. For repeated interleaving with full special-unitary ancilla control, we determine the generated Lie algebra from the associative algebra of the branches, including the available central phase directions. This control result predicts concentration only with additional assumptions on circuit sampling. Matchgate examples exhibit polynomial single-pass LCU variances and exponential concentration under joint Haar-random sampling. We also derive coefficient-selection rules and bounds for postselection, measurement error, and approximate moments.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加密量子克隆中噪声如何从物理信道传播到恢复态尚缺乏系统刻画，需要区分噪声强度之外的传播机制（位置依赖、纠缠对奇偶性等）以指导容错设计与资源评估。

Abstract: We investigate noise propagation in encrypted quantum cloning by formulating the protocol in terms of effective recovery channels. For local single-qubit completely positive trace-preserving noise, a Pauli-basis expansion of the Kraus operators reveals a strong dependence on the location of the physical channel. Noise on an auxiliary signal qubit leaves the reduced recovered state unchanged, whereas noise on an auxiliary key qubit induces a Pauli-diagonal effective channel on the recovered qubit. For the core qubits, the error propagation additionally depends on the parity of the number of entangled pairs. In particular, for noise on the core key qubit with odd parity, distinct physical Pauli sectors merge into the same effective sector, converting isotropic depolarizing noise into an anisotropic recovery channel. We also relate Werner-type resource imperfections to local depolarizing noise and distinguish distributed product depolarization from genuine global depolarization. The results demonstrate that the effective recovery noise is governed not only by noise strength, but also by its physical location and by the structure of the encryption-decryption protocol.

For the parameters studied, this reduces the time to reach a fixed residual-excitation threshold by up to approximately 25% compared with free decay. To prepare a target Dicke state, we introduce a two-step protocol that initially leaves one emitter undriven before restoring the full drive that stabilizes the target. This local control redistributes spectral weight into the more subradiant modes, driving them closer to their target amplitudes, which reduces the preparation time by up to approximately 45% while consuming less driving energy than continuous driving. Finally, we show how directional waveguide coupling shifts collective modes toward a preferred boundary through the non-Hermitian skin effect, providing guidance for selecting the preferred site for local operations. We term this fundamental dependence of the relaxation dynamics on the system's chirality the ``non-Hermitian Skin-Mpemba effect.'' These results demonstrate how selective local control can accelerate state preparation and reset in open quantum systems.

Abstract: Heralded atom loss is a dominant noise channel in neutral atom quantum computing platforms, and losses at a two-qubit gate are strongly correlated: when one atom is lost, its gate partner is often lost too. This work shows how to exploit this correlation to construct a better decoder, focusing on [[144,12,12]] Gross code syndrome extraction circuits. Given a loss readout, the possible pairings of the lost qubits according to shared gates form a graph whose maximum matchings carry almost all of the conditional probability mass. Our maximal-matching-mixture (MMM) decoder builds a per-readout decoding hypergraph as a weighted mixture over exactly these loss scenarios. This decoding hypergraph can be used by an ordinary belief-propagation decoder. On the [[144,12,12]] Gross code, MMM lowers the logical error rate by as much as ~25x relative to a loss-unaware decoder and ~2x relative to a loss-aware uniform-mixture baseline from Baranes et al. (2026).

</details>

Abstract: Coherent noise can give rise to unconventional regimes of quantum information recovery and mixed-state order. We study these phenomena in two settings related by Wegner duality: the triangular-lattice surface code subject to single-qubit Pauli-$Z$ rotations followed by syndrome measurements, and a $\mathbb{Z}_2$ paramagnet subject to strongly symmetric decoherence. Increasing the rotation angle drives a transition from a decodable phase to a \emph{partially decodable} critical phase in the code, and from a strongly symmetric phase to a phase with \emph{algebraic} $\mathbb{Z}_2$ strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking (SWSSB) in the dual paramagnet. We develop a common description of these phenomena in terms of a weakly coupled $\mathrm{SO}(n)$ nonlinear sigma model in the replica limit $n\to1$. Taking this replica limit analytically, we derive universal signatures of the critical phase: an optimal Pauli decoding fidelity that approaches a universal function of the system's aspect ratio in the thermodynamic limit, and a dual fidelity correlator that decays as a universal power law. These observables relate to correlations of twist operators in the sigma model description. Large-scale numerical simulations confirm these analytical predictions. We also argue that the algebraic SWSSB phase cannot be realized by any Pauli dephasing channel acting on the $\mathbb{Z}_{2}$ paramagnet. We probe this "non-classical" character through the output state's sensitivity to well-separated $\mathbb{Z}_{2}$ fluxes inserted in the initial state, which reveals persistent coherence between distinct $\mathbb{Z}_{2}$ charge trajectories at long distances.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 对i.i.d.拷贝，模型在置换下不变，借助Schur–Weyl对偶去除置换多重度空间、以其维数为权重，构造约化统计模型；在约化模型上给出紧致Cramér–Rao界的精确锥规划，以及Nagaoka–Hayashi松弛的半定规划；用精确二次恒等式修正有限射线对偶解，构造严格上下界，并分析网格可行性、对偶解的紧致搜索半径与收敛性。

Result: 固定单拷贝维数时，约化算子表示随拷贝数多项式而非指数增长；充分细网格下存在可行原始点，有界目标间隙的对偶解具一致有界的紧致搜索半径；在投影覆盖半径上呈二次收敛，数值算例验证了网格修正项的二次标度与约化NH形式的计算可达范围。

Conclusion: Schur–Weyl对称约化为有限拷贝多参数量子估计提供了可计算且具严格界保证的框架，将指数复杂度降为多项式，并实现二次收敛的紧致界逼近。

Abstract: In multiparameter quantum estimation, incompatible measurements can impose trade-offs among estimation errors. Understanding the precision attainable with a finite number of jointly measured copies is therefore important, but direct optimization becomes costly as the tensor product space grows exponentially. For independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.) copies, the quantum statistical model is invariant under permutations of the copies. We exploit this symmetry through Schur--Weyl duality to remove permutation multiplicity spaces while using their dimensions as weights. This gives a reduced statistical model equivalent to the original under arbitrary collective measurements. On this reduced model, we derive an exact conic formulation of the tight Cramér--Rao bound and a semidefinite program for its Nagaoka--Hayashi (NH) relaxation. For fixed single-copy dimension, the reduced operator representation grows polynomially rather than exponentially with the number of copies. To approximate the tight bound, we use an exact quadratic identity to correct finite-ray dual solutions and obtain rigorous upper and lower bounds. For fixed copy number and parameter dimension, sufficiently fine meshes admit feasible primal points, and dual solutions with bounded objective gaps have uniformly bounded compact-search radii. These results yield quadratic convergence in the projective covering radius. Numerical examples illustrate the quadratic scaling of the mesh correction term and the computational reach of the reduced NH formulation.

</details>

*Mingxin He,Yifei Wang,Hao Song,Yingfei Gu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出高维平移不变p元拓扑CSS码可解耦（映射为高维环面码堆叠）的充要条件：粗粒化后各超选择层上平移作用平凡且层间"粘合"消失；并证明有限激发类型下额外粗粒化p^{m-1}倍即可消除粘合，同时给出显式SUM/SWAP电路算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维拓扑CSS码具备横截非Clifford门、单次解码等容错量子计算优势，但缺乏系统理解。已有工作表明可用有限深度局域CSS保持Clifford电路将码解耦为高维环面码堆叠，然而二维情形判据（平移在任意子类型上平凡）在高维不充分，需刻画新的阻碍。

Result: 证明解耦等价于：粗粒化后平移在每个超选择层上作用平凡，且层间粘合为零。对每层有限激发类型、共m个非零层的码，平移在每层平凡后再粗粒化p^{m-1}倍即可消除粘合。同一判据亦决定码在有限环面上是否编码最大逻辑qudit数。

Conclusion: 高维拓扑CSS码的解耦判据被完全刻画：粘合为二维判据失效后唯一的新阻碍，且可由有限倍粗粒化消除；解耦过程可由显式SUM/SWAP电路实现，并与有限环面上逻辑qudit数最大化问题等价。

Abstract: Topological Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS) codes in more than two spatial dimensions offer features useful for fault-tolerant quantum computation, such as transversal non-Clifford gates and single-shot decoding, but a systematic understanding of them is still lacking. One approach is to decouple a code: map it to a stack of higher-form toric codes by a finite-depth local CSS-preserving Clifford circuit, possibly after adding ancillas. For translation-invariant $p$-ary CSS codes, where $p$ is prime ($p=2$ for qubits), we require the circuit to commute with the translations of a superlattice. In two dimensions, such a decoupling exists once these translations act trivially on every anyon type. In higher dimensions, excitations are sorted by their dimension into superselection layers, which can remain glued together even when translations act trivially on each layer. We show that this gluing is the only remaining obstruction: a code can be decoupled on a superlattice exactly when, after coarse-graining to it, translations act trivially on every layer and the gluing vanishes. Moreover, for a code with finitely many excitation types in each layer and $m$ nonzero layers, once translations act trivially on every layer, further coarse-graining by a factor of $p^{m-1}$ in every direction removes the aforementioned gluing. Physically, the same criterion determines when the code encodes the maximal number of logical qudits on a finite torus. Finally, we give an explicit algorithm that constructs the decoupling as a circuit of SUM (qudit CNOT) and SWAP gates.

</details>

Abstract: We quantify the extra Bayes risk of locating a quantum change point and estimating its amplitude with one measurement. The joint and marginal optimizations use the full record and the same joint prior, with both parameters unknown. For product records on a known pure state curve, position has clipped absolute loss in resolution units, and amplitude offset has squared loss in fixed calibrated units. Ideal steps exhibit certified conflict at finite signal strength, including in phase records of sixteen qubits, and recover both informative marginal optima at strong signal. For finite offset prior width, a retained quantum boundary region serves position, and the remaining suffix serves amplitude. Physical recovery holds when strength grows more slowly than the square root of record length. Known smooth rises also admit leading recovery in the coherent experiment as strength grows and width shrinks. For the quintic profile, we establish two residual cost scaling laws. The residual cost is $Θ(s^{-2})$ as $s\to\infty$. Taking the strong signal limit at fixed $w$ yields a limiting cost of $Θ(w^3)$ as $w\to0$. In the resolved local model, quadratic position loss admits a common optimum within the covariant class. These results link onset geometry and decision loss to Bayesian measurement compatibility in quantum change point estimation.

Abstract: The reduced density matrices of Haar-random bipartite states form a fixed-trace version of the Wishart ensemble of random matrices, with an asymptotic eigenvalue density given by the Marchenko--Pastur (MP) law. We establish a similar universal form for the maximum-entropy distribution of mixed states subject to a fixed ensemble average, arguing that its eigenvalue density converges asymptotically to that of the correlated Wishart ensemble (CWE). The density is thus obtained by a free multiplicative convolution formula, which we apply to the transverse-field Ising model (TFIM) with a thermal average state to derive a high-temperature leading-order correction to the MP law. We then consider measurement by a random POVM on the purification of the mixed reference state. We show that the resulting mixed-state ensemble is asymptotically equivalent to the CWE, and provide confirming numerical evidence. We return to the TFIM example to compute the entanglement of the system with the ancilla after measurement, and give finite-temperature Page curves for the average entanglement entropies.

Abstract: Quantum singular value transformation (QSVT) provides a unifying framework for quantum algorithms by applying the polynomials of quantum signal processing (QSP) to block-encoded matrices. Extending QSVT beyond matrices to possibly infinite-dimensional operators (operator QSVT), we establish the following. First, it transforms every block of the operator by the QSP polynomials; no singular value decomposition is needed. Second, a spectral mapping theorem determines the full spectrum of the QSVT operator, including continuous spectra, from the embedded operator through the QSP polynomial, allowing spectral analysis without translation invariance. Third, the spectral mapping turns QSP into spectral design: the QSP phases can stretch, fold and separate the bands and place the eigenvalues. Finally, the kernel sectors that conventional QSVT usually sets aside carry a topological index, invariant under the choice of QSP phases, local perturbations and gap-preserving deformations, which guarantees topologically protected states. These results unify two quantum walks: Szegedy's walk from quantum algorithms and the split-step walk from condensed matter physics. Their kernel sectors encode the graph's Euler characteristic and the difference of bulk winding numbers, respectively. For the split-step walk, QSP breaks chiral symmetry but not the topological protection, and can move the protected state into a continuous band to form a bound state in the continuum (BIC).

</details>

Abstract: Quantum systems are typically controlled by classical devices that necessarily dissipate energy to the surrounding environment. In the most experimentally relevant regime of fast control and high fidelity, we find a tradeoff between dissipation and control errors. Abstract: Understanding how local perturbations influence global quantum dynamics is important for analyzing and designing large-scale quantum systems. Existing quantum centrality measures often rely on static correlations or entanglement and do not directly capture perturbation propagation under unitary evolution. We formulate controllability allocation scoring for quantum input-output dynamics governed by the Schrodinger equation. Using the multiobservable quantum-control Gramian, we introduce virtual local Hamiltonian perturbations along a nominal trajectory and decompose the terminal-output response into channel-wise Gramian contributions. A simplex-constrained log-determinant optimization combines these contributions to define a volumetric controllability allocation score (V-CAS), specifying an optimal allocation of virtual input intensities among local Hamiltonian perturbation directions. The score measures their cooperative dynamical influence and depends on the output and trajectory. We characterize the output Gramian's range and minimum-energy properties and derive a drift-independent sufficient condition ensuring score uniqueness for almost every terminal time at which the problem is feasible. For quantum many-body systems, we develop a matrix-product-state and time-evolving-block-decimation implementation that avoids explicitly constructing exponentially large state-transition matrices. We also derive an error bound propagating Trotter and MPS-truncation errors to the discrete output Gramian. Numerical experiments show that the score captures finite-time influence patterns distinct from those identified by static correlation- and entanglement-based measures and can inform target-independent control-channel preselection.

</details>

Here we take a thermodynamic perspective and ask whether such memory can be operationally harnessed to enhance quantum-battery capacity. In a complementary continuous-time setting, we show using random telegraph dephasing that memory accumulated during the evolution can enhance capacity retention relative to a matched Markovian reference under the same control class. These results identify non-Markovian memory as an operational resource for quantum-battery capacity.

</details>

Abstract: Coherent errors leave stabiliser-code-encoded magic states in superpositions of syndrome sectors. We ask how much magic these coherent errors add, and whether syndrome extraction depletes logical magic. Syndrome extraction removes this copy, returning the logical robustness. The removed magic is positive for every non-Clifford error: adding a non-stabiliser state to any state multiplies one plus the robustness by a (tightly- bounded) factor above one. Abstract: Grover-Mixer QAOA (GM-QAOA) is a promising quantum framework for constrained combinatorial optimization. However, its practical implementation requires the efficient preparation of a uniform superposition over feasible states. In this work, we propose a two-stage variational approach. First, a parameterized quantum circuit is trained to prepare this superposition, using an entropy-based cost function that favors a uniform distribution over feasible bitstrings. Overall, these findings highlight both the potential of variational Grover mixers and the trade-off between their performance gains and the additional resources required for their training.

While single-photon ($n=|1\rangle$) emission tracks the electrical excitation pulse as expected, the two-photon ($n=|2\rangle$) statistics exhibit pronounced fluctuations at multiple temporal positions within the pulse. These fluctuations vanish upon demagnetization of the spin-injection layer, establishing a direct link between the multi-photon emission dynamics and the spin polarization of the injected carriers. The behavior is phenomenologically captured by inserting both a variable excitation mechanism and a set of multi-emitter collapse operators within a stochastic wave function evolution. Abstract: Can the ground state of a local Hamiltonian be prepared by local dissipation? We address this question for free-fermion Hamiltonians, using a quadratic Lindbladian with linear jump operators for the dissipative ground-state preparation. For translation-invariant targets, Fourier correspondence relates spatial locality to momentum-space analyticity, allowing us to derive a complete spectral criterion for the existence of exponentially local linear jump operators that prepare the ground state. This criterion leads to a no-go theorem that, sign-changing Fermi-level crossings exclude exponentially local linear jump operators for preparing the ground state. While these crossings are the complete obstruction in one dimension, additional obstructions generally arise in higher dimensions. We illustrate these additional obstructions using two quadratic-band-touching models with algebraically decaying ground-state correlations. We further show that short-range ground-state correlations, although not necessary, are sufficient for local dissipative preparation even without translation invariance. Our results establish how the spectral structure of the target Hamiltonian governs the locality of dissipative ground-state preparation.

Abstract: A parallel two-qubit layer can often be realized with several nonoverlapping sets of physical couplers, but isolated calibrations do not reveal how each realization performs after optimizing its own simultaneous control pulse. We formulate physical placement and pulse synthesis as a coupled decision problem for parallel controlled-$Z$ gates in heterogeneous frequency tunable transmon arrays. The proposed processor-aware structural quantum multi-domain matrix (QMDM) links static processor, edge, incidence, and task information to a dynamic candidate response block. For each candidate placement $P$, QMDM instantiates the active Hamiltonian $H_P$; quantum optimal control (QOCT) generates a placement-specific pulse, direct coupled propagation returns the projected computational subspace overlap $F_{\rm proj}$ and computational survival; and these pulse-conditioned responses are fed back to QMDM to rank and progressively promote physical placements. This placement pulse response feedback defines pulse-informed physical placement. Here, to see the performance of the proposed method on 30 held-out synthetic $3\times3$ processors, the median final $F_{\rm proj}$ is $0.993826$ for pulse-informed placement and $0.984981$ for calibration-based placement. Also, when tested under different system conditions, the proposed pulse-informed approach uses $59.1\%$ fewer candidate QOCT optimizer iterations than the exhaustive finite-budget reference. The QMDM representation and outer search are formulated for arbitrary $L$ simultaneous two-qubit gates. Here, the present global candidate ranking validation is used for $L=2$.

Abstract: Stochastic resetting drives quantum systems into nonequilibrium stationary states unreachable under uninterrupted unitary evolution, and their entanglement depends on whether the reset history is resolved or averaged over. We study this interplay in the transverse-field Ising chain, deriving closed-form results for two qubits and evaluating chains of up to 12 qubits exactly. In the two-qubit system, these closed-form results show that phase matching between energy eigenstates produces resonances, and their visibility in the stationary state is determined by the symmetry of the reset state. Measurements on an IBM superconducting processor show the entropies evaluated before and after the reset average closely following noiseless predictions with no fitted parameter, while concurrence lies below prediction across most of the hardware measurements. The two diagnostics respond differently because they read different components of the same reconstructed state, and a depolarizing model captures the concurrence suppression with one fitted parameter. The difference between the two entropies, i. e., the Jensen gap of entanglement entropy, equals the Holevo information that a subsystem retains about the time since the last reset. With a Bell-state reset, this information grows with chain length across a half-chain cut over the sizes studied, without visible saturation at the smaller reset probabilities. Since reset times are sampled and recorded classically, the trajectory-averaged entropy, and with it the Jensen gap, is obtained without postselection, in contrast to monitored circuits, where trajectory-resolved entanglement requires sampling exponentially rare measurement records.

Conclusion: 该综述可作为超导量子门实现的技术参考，将门合成理论与硬件限制对齐，指向硬件-软件协同设计框架，并为实验验证提供方法学指引与实用工作流。

Abstract: This review aims to provide researchers, experimentalists, and quantum engineers with an updated and technically grounded perspective on the implementation of quantum gates in superconducting NISQ devices. The goal is to establish a comprehensive bridge between theoretical quantum circuit synthesis and its practical, experimental implementation, focusing on superconducting quantum technologies. We systematically examine the diverse landscape of superconducting qubits and processor architectures, ranging from conventional transmons to alternative and unconventional designs, evaluating their unique design trade-offs, connectivity constraints, device physics, and materials impact. Moving from theory to actual implementation, we detail how single- and two-qubit operations are synthesized via microwave-driven and flux-activated pulses. We here present our pulse engineering, wave shaping, calibration workflows, demonstrating how to bridge the gap between abstract gate models and real-world hardware limitations in the direction of a hardware-software co-design framework. To validate these operations, we choose benchmarking methodologies with the purpose to get the more efficient metric for the specific evaluation aspect, including truth tables and quantum process tomography. Therefore, we both discuss the theoretical concepts behind the gate synthesis and show experimental implementation of most fundamental gates by proposing a practical workflow.

Abstract: Integrating quantum communication into existing fiber infrastructures requires coexisting with high-power classical traffic, which generates noise in the quantum channel. Spontaneous Raman Scattering (SpRS) is the dominant noise source, generating a broadband photon background that degrades quantum states and, in Quantum Key Distribution (QKD), can entirely suppress secure key generation. In this work, we implement single-copy entanglement distillation utilizing energy-time and frequency hyperentanglement, and we exploit it to perform Quantum Privacy Amplification (QPA) directly at a physical layer. Strict frequency anti-correlations of spontaneous parametric down-conversion are used to selectively discard uncorrelated Raman photons while preserving genuine entangled pairs, suppressing accidental coincidences by a factor $1/N$, where $N$ is the number of frequency channel pairs. Using passive borosilicate-glass photonic integrated circuits and standard DWDM demultiplexers over a 20~km single-mode fiber testbed, we demonstrate a 4~dB improvement in tolerance to classical laser launch power $P_C$ when QPA is performed, extending the secure key threshold from $P_C=-13.3$ dBm to $P_C=-9.3$ dBm. The result shows that off-the-shelf telecom components can be repurposed as physical-layer quantum privacy amplifiers.

### [87] [Homodyne versus heterodyne detection for classifying families of optical quantum states from finite records of detection events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38671)

TL;DR: 在有限探测记录下比较零差与外差测量对七类光量子态（含GKP态）的识别能力：卷积网络在外差情形于N=100、20%退相位时显著优于滤波反投影零差14.1个百分点，但该差距随N增大而消失，且属重建流程问题而非零差探测本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络可由相空间函数图像识别量子态，但真实测量只给出N个有限探测事件；外差直接采样Husimi Q函数，零差需经多相位重建才得到Wigner函数。作者欲厘清：在损耗、退相位与高斯噪声下，哪种测量方案更利于区分七类量子态。

Method: 对同一批态在等平均光子数集合与Ahmed等编码集合上模拟有限记录；用卷积网络做七类分类，比较多相位滤波反投影零差与外差；另设无重建零差网络、似然比观测者作对照，并在热态附近做解析展开，另对GKP态、光子减压缩态及EPR单模的实验记录做验证性分类。

Result: 卷积网络下，第一集合在N=100、20%退相位时外差比滤波反投影零差高14.1个百分点（跨24个单元校正后p=0.003），且结果依赖相位数目与重建方式；N=10^3与10^4时差异不显著但置信区间宽。N=100时无重建零差在第一集合与外差无显著差异，第二集合名义上更差。解析上，热态附近外差以指数方式抑制n阶相空间结构，零差则在n+1个角向分量间均分单位权重，属局部结论，不解释有限记录的差异。似然比观测者在N=100无此差距；低功率实验记录的分类与模拟一致，但仅检验计数噪声模型。

Conclusion: 证据支持的是"基于重建的零差流程"弱于外差，而非零差探测普遍劣于外差；有限记录下的差距仅出现在小N与特定重建设置，随事件数增加而消失，因此结论应限定于卷积网络加重建管线的范围。

Abstract: Neural networks can identify an optical quantum state from an image of its phase-space function, but a real measurement gives a finite record of $N$ detection events. Heterodyne detection samples the Husimi $Q$ function directly, whereas homodyne detection gives the Wigner function only after a reconstruction from several phases. We ask which scheme better identifies one of seven families of states, including Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) states, under loss, dephasing and Gaussian noise. We simulate finite records on identical states for two sets of families, one with equal mean photon number and one built from the code of Ahmed et al. For convolutional networks, heterodyne detection outperforms homodyne detection with filtered back-projection by 14.1 percentage points at $N=100$ events and 20% dephasing in the first set (adjusted $p=0.003$ over $24$ cells across training runs), and it depends on the number of phases and on the reconstruction. At $N=10^3$ and $10^4$ no difference is significant, but the intervals are wide. At $N=100$, a homodyne network without reconstruction does not differ significantly from heterodyne detection in the first set and is nominally worse in the second. Near thermal states we show to leading order that heterodyne detection damps phase-space structure of order $n$ exponentially, whereas homodyne detection shares unit weight among $n+1$ angular components, a local result that does not explain the finite-record differences. Experimental records of a GKP state, photon-subtracted squeezed states and one mode of an Einstein-Podolsky-Rosen source are classified like simulated ones, but these low-power tests probe our counting-noise model, not the seven-class accuracy. A likelihood-ratio observer shows no such gap at $N=100$, so our evidence concerns a reconstruction-based pipeline, not homodyne detection in general.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将数论中的哥德巴赫表示（偶数表为两素数之和）与量子光学统计建立联系，为哥德巴赫表示提供量子光学物理实现，并将其统计性质引入量子计量学。

Method: 将偶数Fock态注入透射率均匀分布的随机分束器，把两个输出模投影到素数子空间；对成功结果与哥德巴赫表示作一一对应，并用Hardy–Littlewood渐近公式估计哥德巴赫表示数目，进而分析测量后态在相位估计中的精度极限标度。

Result: 每个成功探测结果对应一个哥德巴赫表示，平均探测概率恰正比于哥德巴赫表示数；固定透射率分束器产生的测量后态在相位估计中仅达到标准量子极限标度，而哥德巴赫表示的等权叠加态达到海森堡标度。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了哥德巴赫表示的量子光学实现，并把其统计性质与量子计量学中的精度极限联系起来。

Abstract: We derive a relation between Goldbach representations and photon-number statistics in a quantum-optical setup. Considering an even-number Fock state injected into a stochastic beam splitter with uniformly distributed transmissivity and projections of the two output modes onto prime subspaces, we demonstrate that each successful outcome corresponds to an Goldbach representation and that the averaged detection probability is exactly proportional to the number of Goldbach representations. Then, employing the Hardy--Littlewood asymptotic formula, we demonstrate that the post-measurement state generated by a beam splitter with fixed transmissivity exhibits standard quantum limit scaling of the ultimate precision limit in terms of phase estimation, whereas an equally weighted superposition of Goldbach representations exhibits Heisenberg scaling. These results provide a quantum-optical realization of Goldbach representations and relate their statistics of with quantum metrology.

</details>


### [89] [Sample-Based Quantum Diagonalization for Variable Molecular Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38701)
*Matthieu Fortin-Deschenes,Ibrahim Shehzad*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出变几何采样量子对角化(VGSQD)算法，将SQD从固定分子几何扩展到变几何，通过复用参考几何的量子采样显著降低量子成本，并在124量子比特处理器上完成几何优化与反应路径计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有子空间方法（如SQD）能处理经典上困难的体系，但仅限于电子基态与激发态的能量计算，缺乏支持几何优化、反应路径等实际化学应用的可扩展量子算法。

Method: VGSQD在SQD基础上引入变几何能力：在位移几何处复用参考几何的量子采样，仅在经典端进行对角化构建子空间，从而避免为每个几何点重新执行量子线路，大幅减少量子资源消耗。实验使用IBM Heron r2 (ibm-quebec)处理器，线路规模最高124量子比特。

Result: 确定了弱束缚强关联铍二聚体及水分子的平衡几何与能量；进一步将VGSQD扩展到化学反应，计算了氢交换反应的最小能量路径与能垒。结果与CCSD(T)、DMRG等最先进经典方法相当。

Conclusion: VGSQD将量子算法在化学中的应用范围从能量计算拓展至几何优化与反应路径，二者对实际应用至关重要，展示了混合量子-经典方法在实用化学问题上的可行性。

Abstract: Quantum and hybrid quantum-classical algorithms have the potential to improve the accuracy and scale of quantum chemistry and impact a broad range of applications such as drug discovery and catalysis. In particular, subspace methods such as sample-based quantum diagonalization (SQD) can already tackle classically challenging systems. However, practical algorithms that go beyond the computation of electronic ground and excited states are still scarce. Here, we introduce the variable-geometry sample-based quantum diagonalization (VGSQD) algorithm, which extends SQD to variable molecular geometries by reusing quantum samples from a reference geometry at displaced ones, thus significantly reducing quantum computational cost. Using circuits of up to 124 qubits on ibm-quebec, an IBM Heron r2 quantum processor, we determine the equilibrium geometry and energy of the weakly bound, strongly correlated beryllium dimer, as well as the water molecule. Finally, we extend VGSQD to chemical reactions and compute the minimum energy path and energy barrier of a hydrogen exchange reaction. The results are comparable with state-of-the-art classical methods such as CCSD(T) and DMRG. This work broadens the scope of quantum algorithms for chemistry, from energies to geometry optimization and reaction pathways, both essential for practical applications.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 利用测量记录可访问阻挫自旋系统的热力学，且无序分布可与线路采样频率独立选取，为淬火无序热力学提供可行量子方案。

Abstract: Quenched thermodynamics combines the equilibrium physics of fixed coupling configurations with a prescribed distribution of disorder. Problems related to quenched thermodynamics are generally intractable to approximate. We provide a quantum algorithm to calculate quenched disorder averages for the transverse-field Ising model, whose coupling configurations are organized into gauge classes. We show how to estimate these averages by preparing approximate Gibbs states through ancilla-assisted imaginary-time evolution. At each measurement, the ancilla record identifies the gauge class to which the energy estimate contributes.
  We validate our framework through an open $3\times3$ lattice. A one-step circuit resolves all sixteen flux classes, and a finite-shot calculation estimates the quenched energy for independently distributed bond signs. Thermodynamic integration extends the reconstruction to a quenched free-energy estimate, while comparison with exact diagonalization identifies the finite-step approximation as the dominant source of error. This use of the measurement record provides access to the thermodynamics of frustrated spin systems with a disorder distribution chosen independently of the circuit's sampling frequencies.

</details>


### [91] [Generalized Mean-Field Ising Model as a Benchmark Family with Exact Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38803)

  <summary>Details</summary>
