Conclusion: GMFI模型在精确多体理论、数论、优化与量子硬件之间架起桥梁，为识别和理解经典与量子计算的算法局限提供可扩展基准。

Abstract: Exactly solvable models provide opportunities to understand complex optimization problems while offering controlled benchmarks for computational methods. However, nontrivial models that combine analytical tractability with challenging optimization structure remain scarce. Here, we introduce the Generalized Mean-Field Ising (GMFI) family, a densely connected and inhomogeneous class of Ising Hamiltonians whose ground states can nevertheless be obtained exactly. This exact solution reveals a hidden low-dimensional structure and enables a complete number-theoretic classification of different system sizes and degenerate ground states. Uniquely, the GMFI family can be used to benchmark both quantum and classical optimization methods over system sizes far beyond exact diagonalization. We investigate the GMFI family using several approaches, including theoretical analysis of quantum annealing, experiments on a D-Wave quantum processor, the quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA), imaginary-time evolution (ITE), greedy algorithm, simulated annealing, and a quantum-inspired greedy algorithm. Despite the GMFI model's exact solvability, it exhibits nontrivial algorithmic behavior: although the quantum-annealing gap is expected to decrease with increasing system size, it shows pronounced and unexpected system-size-dependent oscillatory signatures. These results establish GMFI models as a rare bridge between exact many-body theory, number theory, optimization, and quantum hardware, providing a scalable benchmark for identifying and understanding algorithmic limitations in classical and quantum computing.

</details>


### [92] [Generating Multipartite Entanglement via Structured-Bath Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38807)

Method: 在弱耦合理论下研究相互作用Ising链；借助阿贝尔强对称性将非二次生成元的稳态构造约化为经典问题，适用于任意Ising耦合；用结构化浴把时间浴关联转化为经典空间关联，再由强对称性提升为量子关联；以精确马尔可夫嵌入和张量网络模拟检验超出解析区间的机制，并提出基于协同冷却运动模式的囚禁离子实现方案。

Conclusion: 独立局域浴结合强对称性与时间浴关联可实现真多体纠缠的耗散制备，方法适用于超导与囚禁离子平台。

Abstract: We present how to dissipatively prepare genuinely multipartite-entangled steady states with mutually independent local reservoirs by harnessing an Abelian strong symmetry and temporal bath correlations. In interacting Ising chains, we demonstrate that, for a broad class of system-environment couplings, flat baths exponentially erase Ising-axis correlations. Structured baths overcome this obstruction by converting temporal bath correlations into classical spatial correlations. Strong symmetry then promotes these correlations to the quantum domain, enabling global multipartite entanglement. Within weak-coupling theory, we obtain exact steady states despite the generator's nonquadratic nature: strong symmetry reduces their construction to a classical problem for arbitrary Ising couplings. This reduction reveals an exact connection between classical distinguishability and entanglement. We then show how to engineer the bath spectrum to increase the correlation length and enable the preparation of GHZ-like states from separable initial conditions. We test the mechanism beyond the analytical regime using exact Markovian embeddings and tensor-network simulations. Our approach is particularly suited to superconducting and trapped-ion platforms, and we propose a concrete implementation for the latter using sympathetically cooled motional modes.

</details>


### [93] [Key Evaluation of a Relativistic Quantum Key Distribution Over a Turbulent Free-space Optical Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38808)
*G. Bebrov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了湍流自由空间信道下相对论量子密钥分发（RQKD）在单光子与相干态两种实现下的性能，将湍流相位涨落引入密钥率分析，量化了大气湍流对干涉可见度与成钥的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由空间RQKD系统受大气湍流影响，但湍流对干涉可见度与安全密钥生成的定量影响尚不明确，缺乏系统设计与优化的实用指导。

Result: 湍流通过降低干涉可见度恶化协议性能，恶化程度由干涉臂间距r（或信号态与参考态的时间间隔ΔT）与Fried参数之比决定；单光子协议在所考察的全部湍流区间均可工作，而相干态实现强烈依赖干涉仪几何（尤其ΔT），存在超过后即无法生成安全密钥的阈值。

Abstract: We investigate the performance of relativistic quantum key distribution (RQKD) over turbulent free-space optical channels for both single-photon and coherent-state implementations. A theoretical model is developed to describe the effect of atmospheric turbulence on interferometric visibility and secret-key generation by incorporating turbulence-induced phase fluctuations into the key-rate analysis. Numerical simulations are performed under realistic channel conditions to evaluate the achievable communication distance and secret-key rate. The results show that atmospheric turbulence degrades the protocol performance by reducing interference visibility, with the severity of the degradation determined by the ratio between the interferometer-arm separation $r$ (or temporal separation of signal and reference states $ΔT$) and Fried's parameter. The single-photon protocol remains operational over all investigated turbulence regimes, whereas the coherent-state implementation exhibits a critical dependence on the interferometer geometry (especially on $ΔT$), leading to a threshold beyond which secure key generation is no longer possible. These findings quantify the impact of atmospheric turbulence on relativistic quantum key distribution and provide practical guidelines for the design and optimization of free-space RQKD systems.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We investigate the operational limits of entanglement distribution through quantum channels reused without reinitialization. Building on an effective repeated-use channel description, we show that residual excitations left in the channel from previous uses act as a memory source that progressively suppresses coherent quantum transmission. To quantify this limitation, we derive an analytical lower bound on the quantum capacity and combine it with the Holevo-Werner semidefinite-programming upper bound. We also provide an analytical criterion to identify entanglement-breaking channels, with thresholds that depend jointly on chain length and reuse. For perfect-state-transfer chains, the resulting capacity window yields tighter estimates than previous bounds, with the relative improvement becoming substantially more pronounced closer to the entanglement-breaking threshold. Moreover, on the second use, our lower bound certifies a maximal relative timing-error tolerance across all chains with internal memory that lies just below 22.63% and is attained by the three-site chain. We further derive a fundamental relative timing-error threshold of 36.41%, above which we analytically guarantee zero unassisted quantum capacity for every chain with internal memory, independent of the number of uses. These results establish a clear benchmark model for repeated entanglement distribution, and identify timing precision, chain length, and memory accumulation as fundamental constraints on scalable quantum-network architectures.

</details>

Abstract: Atomic clocks achieve extraordinary precision through quantum control over large ensembles of atoms, with precision increasing and atom number advancing steadily. Emerging nuclear clock technology encodes clock states in nuclear rather than electronic transitions, offering a new frontier for precision measurement. Here we study whether a large ensemble of such clock nuclei could be used to observe their mutual gravitational interaction. We investigate the gravitational redshift induced by neighboring atoms, including nuclear energy superpositions as sources of gravity, giving rise to a genuinely quantum gravitational interaction between pairs of nuclei. We find that the resulting clock frequency shift due to mutual gravity may become non-negligible for a macroscopic number of nuclei, for instance in a solid-state thorium clock, and we propose approaches to mitigate electromagnetic field effects by exploiting spatial symmetry and high-precision spectroscopy. We further discuss how energy-induced mass superpositions could be directly probed using external gravitational field sensors. By contrast, observing gravity-induced entanglement involving clocks would require substantially greater experimental advances due to decoherence from spontaneous emission. Taken together, our results suggest that nuclear clocks, either alone or in combination with complementary sensors such as optomechanical systems, may offer a future route toward probing gravity at short distances and testing gravitating quantum source masses beyond the Newtonian limit.

</details>

*Riccardo Zampieri,Sougato Bose*

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析含重复分裂阶段的量子引力探测协议，每个阶段使占据路径数加倍；证明n阶段密度矩阵与n−1阶段通过局域宇称退相位相联系，并给出负度变化下界、纠缠寿命与读出优越性的精确判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用空间分离质量间的纠缠检验引力的量子性已成为热点，现有方案通过受控分裂产生多条干涉分支；但多级/重复分裂如何影响纠缠度、引力相位的作用以及纠缠寿命尚缺乏系统性刻画。

Result: 给出负度变化的精确下界；两级分裂下得到额外分裂不降低负度的三相位显式条件；证明进一步分裂不能缩短所生成纠缠的寿命；若局域内系统保留完整分支标号，复合后位置纠缠得以保留；无存储读出下导出额外分裂改善读出的精确条件；数值模拟显示位置负度优于所考察速率，且该优势在最终读出时刻所有消相干率下经受限读出后仍存在。

Conclusion: 重复分裂在多级干涉中不会损害（并可能增强）引力诱导的位置纠缠，纠缠寿命不受额外分裂缩短；该协议可用可复用二能级系统与局域存储实现，即使采用受限读出仍具优势，为量子引力实验设计提供理论指导。

Abstract: Proposals to probe the quantum nature of gravity through entanglement between spatially separated masses have attracted considerable interest. In such schemes, spatial superpositions are prepared through controlled splitting operations that produce several interferometric branches. Here we propose and analyse protocols involving repeated splitting stages, each of which doubles the number of occupied paths. We show that the density matrix after $n$ splitting stages is related to that of the $(n-1)$-splitting protocol through local parity dephasing. Two of the resulting sectors reproduce the preceding state, while the other two acquire additional gravitational phases. Removing the locally generated parts gives an exact lower bound on the change in negativity, expressed in terms of the preceding state's negative partial-transpose eigenspace. For two splitting stages, this leads to an explicit condition on three gravitational phases under which the additional split cannot reduce negativity. We also show that further splitting cannot shorten the lifetime of the generated entanglement. If the local internal system preserves the full branch label, the positional entanglement is retained upon recombination. We describe an implementation using a reusable two-level system and local memories, and compare it with a readout without a memory register. For the latter, we derive an exact condition for when an additional splitting stage improves the readout, based on a $2\times 2$ Schur complement. Numerical simulations show an advantage in positional negativity over the rates considered, with an improvement surviving the restricted readout at all decoherence rates considered at the final readout time.

</details>


### [97] [Inferring the Number of Quantum State Transfers with Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38844)
*Hayden Zammit,Tony J. G. Apollaro,Gianluca Valentino,Johann A. Briffa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究"重复使用"量子态传输信道（未重置时信道内累积记忆），提出用前馈神经网络仅凭单次使用的收发Bloch矢量识别当前是第几次使用，准确率>90%，利用U(1)对称性的两分量约化表示可提升至约95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子态传输研究基于单次使用范式，而实际信道在连续使用间不重置时会累积记忆，使第n次使用的映射随使用次数变化并携带此前各次使用的"指纹"。作者由此提出一个逆向问题：能否仅从传输数据中反推出使用序号。

Method: 以XX自旋链为信道模型，取单次使用中发送与接收态的Bloch矢量作为输入，训练简单前馈神经网络进行使用序号分类；并利用信道的U(1)对称性构造两分量约化态表示以改进性能；系统刻画分类器对训练集规模与组成（采样态数与采样时刻数）的依赖，并确定达到给定精度所需的数据量；最后用混淆矩阵上的推土机距离（earth-mover distance）量化残余误分类的严重程度。

Result: 前馈网络识别使用序号的准确率超过90%；两分量约化表示将准确率进一步提升至约95%；采样态数与采样时刻数对性能同等重要；给出了达到目标精度所需的数据集规模；并用EMD指标刻画了残余错误的结构性严重程度。

Conclusion: 使用序号可作为可提取的信道记忆特征，简单的监督学习即可从单次使用的传输数据中高精度恢复它；对称性引导的降维表示与训练数据设计（态采样与时序采样）是提升性能的关键因素。

Abstract: Repeated-use quantum state transfer (QST) goes beyond the single-use paradigm by accounting for the memory that accumulates inside the transmission channel when it is not reset between uses. Consequently, the map governing the $n^{\mathrm{th}}$ use of the channel differs from use to use and carries a signature of each previous use. We ask whether this use number can be recovered from transmission data alone, and show that it can: a simple feed-forward neural network, given as input the Bloch vectors sent and received over a single use of an XX spin-chain channel, is able to identify the use number with an accuracy exceeding 90%. Exploiting the U(1) symmetry of the channel, we find that a reduced two-component representation of the transmitted states improves the accuracy further, to about 95%. We characterise the dependence of the classifier on the size and composition of the training data, finding that the number of sampled states and of sampled timings are equally important. We also identify the data set sizes required to reach a given accuracy. Finally, we quantify the severity of the residual misclassifications with an earth-mover distance on the confusion matrix.

</details>


### [98] [Local Quantum Fault Tolerance Without Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38847)
*David Ponarovsky*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究固定局部纠错规则与几何简洁码（立方(d,d')-Toric码含4D Toric码）的容错量子计算，证明在弱局部随机Pauli噪声下，仅用常数深度、基于当前syndorme的sweep纠错循环即可实现电路级阈值定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Toric码容错方案多依赖多尺度解码器状态、尺度相关的通信延迟或基于expansion的编码证明，缺乏几何简单、恒定深度且无需多尺度处理的局部纠错规则；Kubica与Preskill提出高维sweep规则的局部存在性问题亦未解决。

Method: 将错误与syndorme视为立方复形中的链，结合sweep规则下的几何收缩与受Toom定理启发的时空explanation-tree论证（不依赖expansion），构造立方(d,d')-Toric复形上的显式高维sweep规则，并证明含逻辑门交错的噪声sweep循环的电路级阈值定理。

Result: (1)给出高维sweep规则的局部算法，解决Kubica–Preskill局部存在性问题；(2)证明电路级阈值定理，4D Toric码可用固定常数深度、基于syndorme的纠错循环而无需多尺度解码或expansion编码（不声称总深度开销为常数）；(3)对低于阈值的速率p局部随机Pauli初始错误，n^{d+1}次无噪声sweep循环以至少1-O(poly(n))p^{Ω(n)}的概率恢复边长n码的编码态，推广了独立错误下的既有保证。

Conclusion: 局部、常数深度、几何简洁的sweep纠错循环足以支撑高维Toric码的容错计算，无需多尺度解码器与expansion论证，为几何简单码的实用容错方案提供理论依据。

Abstract: We study fault-tolerant quantum computation using a fixed, local error-correction rule and a geometrically simple code: the cubical $(d,d')$-Toric code with $d',d-d'\geq 2$, including the 4D Toric code. Under sufficiently weak local-stochastic Pauli noise, our construction uses noiseless, bounded-depth classical processing per physical time step. Its correction cycle acts only on the current syndrome, has constant depth, and is repeated unchanged during idle logical computation; it requires neither multiscale decoder states nor scale-dependent communication delays.
  Our proof treats errors and syndromes as chains in a cubical complex, combining geometric shrinking under the sweep rule with a space--time explanation-tree argument inspired by Toom's theorem. It does not rely on expansion. We establish three results:
  1.Explicit higher-dimensional sweep rule. We give a local algorithm for choosing sweep corrections on cubical $(d,d')$-Toric complexes, resolving the local-existence question of Kubica and Preskill.
  2. Fault-tolerant computation. We prove a circuit-level threshold theorem for noisy sweep cycles interleaved with logical gates. In particular, the 4D Toric code admits a fixed, constant-depth syndrome-based correction cycle without a multiscale decoder or expansion-based encoding. We do not claim constant total-depth overhead.
  3. Ideal decoding under local-stochastic noise. For an initial local-stochastic Pauli error of rate $p$ below threshold, $n^{d+1}$ noiseless sweep cycles recover the encoded state on a side-length-$n$ $(d,d')$-Toric code with probability at least $1-O(\operatorname{poly}(n))p^{Ω(n)}$, extending earlier guarantees for independent errors.

</details>


### [99] [Drive-tunable Floquet exchange spectroscopy with Hong--Ou--Mandel interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38878)
*Eric R Bittner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 理论上证明：在集体耦合腔中，延迟分辨的HOM符合测量可探测Floquet驱动对双光子交换的重塑；符合轨迹的傅里叶变换给出随驱动变化的两个交换分支，并将其与Floquet极化激元谱及Fisher信息定量关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Floquet驱动会重塑腔光谱，但单光子边带布居无法揭示其如何改变双光子交换过程，缺乏针对该响应的双光子探测量。

Method: 考虑一个受驱动的集体耦合腔；频率纠缠的信号光子与驱动腔发生线性散射，其闲频光子作为延迟参考，进行延迟分辨的Hong–Ou–Mandel符合测量。对不同步的驱动相位取平均，消除不同净输出阶之间的相干性，同时保留每个阶内的交换干涉。对符合轨迹做傅里叶变换，得到与Floquet极化激元谱对应的两个驱动依赖的交换分支；并在驱动频率与幅度上扫描符合轨迹响应，与计数模型相关的Fisher信息相联系。

Result: 傅里叶变换后出现两个随驱动变化的交换分支，与Floquet极化激元谱对应；驱动改变时分支位置移动且交换权重重新分配，占主导的物理输出阶也发生转移，而单光子边带布居无法反映这一转移。此外给出符合轨迹响应在驱动频率—幅度平面上的映射及其与计数模型相关的Fisher信息。

Conclusion: Floquet控制为HOM干涉术增添了可调的交换谱，并为实验提供了定量目标；延迟分辨的HOM符合测量可作为探测驱动腔双光子交换响应的有效手段。

Abstract: Floquet driving reshapes cavity spectra, but single-photon sideband populations do not reveal how it changes two-photon exchange. Here we show theoretically that delay-resolved Hong--Ou--Mandel (HOM) coincidences can probe this response in a collectively coupled cavity. A frequency-entangled signal photon scatters linearly from the driven cavity while its idler provides a delayed reference. Averaging over an unsynchronized drive phase removes coherence between different net output orders but preserves exchange interference within each order. Fourier transformation of the coincidence trace yields two drive-dependent exchange branches associated with the Floquet-polariton spectrum. As the drive changes, the branches shift and exchange weight; the dominant physical output order also changes, a transfer not reproduced by single-photon sideband populations. We further map the coincidence-trace response over drive frequency and amplitude and connect it to a count-model-dependent Fisher information. Floquet control thus adds a tunable exchange spectrum to HOM interferometry and gives quantitative targets for an experiment.

</details>


### [100] [Execution bias, repeatability and mitigation cost in parameter-frozen molecular VQE energy evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38885)
*Julen Larrucea*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在IBM两台156比特Heron处理器上评估分子变分量子本征求解器(VQE)能量评估的统计误差与真实精度关系，发现更小的误差棒并不代表更准确，原始测量的残差远超报告不确定度，并系统比较ZNE与TREX等误差缓解手段的收益与成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 业界常以统计误差棒衡量分子能量评估精度，但作者质疑"更小误差棒≠更准确结果"。需要在真实超导硬件上检验报告的不确定度是否可信，并量化误差缓解方案的实际价值。

Method: 在IBM Aachen与Pittsburgh两台156比特Heron处理器上进行无噪声优化的VQE态评估：仅能量评估在硬件上运行，参数、布局与编译电路冻结，关闭动态解耦。实验包含五分子试点、三分子隔离队列(三个时间窗)及已知态对照，共110个作业、28.2 QPU分钟。对比Raw 1024测量、ZNE与TREX三种方案。

Result: Raw 1024下O2与BeH2平均残差超报告不确定度12–32倍；Pittsburgh上BeH2跨作业标准差为平均返回不确定度的5.6倍。ZNE使O2绝对残差在Aachen降低49.7%、Pittsburgh降低83.5%，代价约4倍。Pittsburgh上TREX每作业电荷消除80 mHa，ZNE为50 mHa。对照实验显示qubit-124制备或读出误差达校准值5.4–5.6倍；13个缓解后能量点估计落在映射谱之外而无Raw对应。

Conclusion: 统计误差棒不能作为分子能量准确性的可靠指标，误差缓解可能引入谱外偏差。建议在增加采样前进行工作负载检查与重复测量。

Abstract: A smaller statistical error bar need not mean a more accurate molecular energy. We evaluate noiselessly optimized molecular variational quantum eigensolver states on IBM Aachen and Pittsburgh, two 156-qubit Heron processors (provider-reported revision 3). Only energy evaluation runs on hardware, with parameters, layouts and compiled circuits frozen and dynamical decoupling disabled. A five-molecule pilot, three-molecule isolated cohort in three windows and known-state controls comprise 110 jobs and 28.2 billed quantum processing unit (QPU) minutes; the isolated cohort spans 30.2 h within a 31.7 h campaign. Raw 1024 O2 and BeH2 mean residuals exceed returned uncertainty by factors 12-32. Pittsburgh BeH2 cross-job standard deviation is 5.6 times mean returned uncertainty. The zero-noise extrapolation (ZNE) package reduces O2 absolute residuals by 49.7% on Aachen and 83.5% on Pittsburgh at about fourfold charge. On Pittsburgh, twirled readout error extinction (TREX) removes 80 millihartree (mHa) per Raw 1024 job charge versus 50 mHa for ZNE. Controls reveal qubit-124 preparation or readout errors 5.4-5.6 times calibration; 13 mitigated energy point estimates fall outside the mapped spectrum, with no Raw counterpart. These results support workload checks and repeated measurements before additional sampling.

</details>


### [101] [High-Threshold Fusion-Based Quantum Computation with Optical Hybrid Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38914)
*Seong-Yeop Lim,Jaehak Lee,Nuri Kang,Seung-Woo Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于DV-CV混合资源态与混合融合的混合FBQC框架，实现2.231%光子损耗阈值且每表面码单元仅需12次融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散变量(DV)与连续变量(CV)编码各存操作性缺陷，混合编码可互补；同时FBQC旨在解决光子量子计算走向容错的可扩展性难题。作者希望将二者结合，兼得混合编码优势与FBQC的可扩展性。

Method: 采用小型光学DV-CV混合资源态，通过混合融合(hybrid fusions)连接构建FBQC；设计无需Hadamard等局域混合逻辑的直接资源态生成协议，并配套利用单次混合融合结果中错误信息的定制解码策略。

Result: 混合FBQC达到2.231%光子损耗阈值，每表面码单元仅需12次混合融合；相比此前混合测量型方案的0.89%阈值与60次融合需求显著改善。

Conclusion: 光学混合FBQC是通往可扩展容错光子量子计算的高阈值、资源高效路径。

Abstract: Hybrid discrete- and continuous-variable (DV-CV) encodings have been developed to overcome operational drawbacks of each individual encoding, while fusion-based quantum computation (FBQC) has emerged to address the practical challenges of scalability toward fault-tolerant photonic quantum computation. Here we introduce hybrid FBQC using small optical DV-CV hybrid resource states connected through hybrid fusions, to combine the advantages of both approaches. We develop protocols for directly generating these resource states without requiring local hybrid logic such as the Hadamard gate, together with a tailored decoding strategy that exploits the error information contained in individual hybrid-fusion outcomes. We demonstrate that hybrid FBQC achieves a photon loss threshold of 2.231% while requiring only 12 hybrid fusions per surface-code unit cell, substantially improving upon the 0.89% threshold and 60-fusion requirement of previous hybrid measurement-based scheme. These results establish optical hybrid FBQC as a high-threshold resource-efficient route to scalable fault-tolerant photonic quantum computation.

</details>


### [102] [Adaptive Local Measurements For Quantum Learning Can Exponentially Outperform Nonadaptive Global Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38922)
*Alireza Goldar,Zhen Qin,Zhihui Zhu,Zhe-Xuan Gong,Michael B. Wakin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：逐副本测量且副本间不保留量子记忆的**自适应**协议，相比可对每份副本做任意全局测量的**非自适应**协议，存在**指数级信息优势**；根源是测量不可对易性（incompatibility），与纠缠和量子记忆无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应测量是否比预先固定测量具有指数级信息优势，是一个核心问题。已有量子设定中自适应最多带来多项式增益，已知的指数优势要么依赖跨副本的相干测量，要么依赖受限测量集合。本文希望在最不利的条件下（逐副本测量、无量子记忆、非自适应方拥有最强能力）考察是否存在指数分离。

Method: 构造显式的多量子比特态族：标量参数被编码在指数多个候选可观测量之一中，并附一个辅助"标记"指示哪一个相关。自适应协议先借标记识别相关可观测量，再用仅单比特测量加一轮自适应完成估计；非自适应协议则只能对每份副本做固定的全局测量。态本身可分离，且在依赖标记的乘积基下是经典的。

Result: 在固定精度下，自适应协议的副本数随比特数仅按对数增长，而任何非自适应协议需指数多份副本；同一机制在局部渐近极小极大均方误差常数上给出指数间隙，且指数底数紧。当候选可观测量对易时分离消失，完全观测的经典样本中亦不可能出现该分离。

Conclusion: 测量不可对易性（measurement incompatibility）本身是量子学习的一种资源，区别于纠缠与量子记忆，且仅靠经典反馈即可被利用：固定测量必须在互不对易的候选中分配其信息预算，而自适应可选择信息量最大者。

Abstract: Can measurements chosen adaptively, in response to earlier outcomes, be exponentially more informative than measurements fixed in advance? In many quantum settings, adaptivity helps at most polynomially; known exponential advantages require coherent measurements across copies or restricted measurements. We show an exponential advantage measuring one copy at a time, with no quantum memory kept between state copies, even when the nonadaptive learner may perform arbitrary global measurements on each copy. We construct explicit families of multi-qubit states in which a scalar parameter is encoded in one of exponentially many candidate observables, with an auxiliary marker indicating which one. An adaptive protocol identifies the relevant observable and then measures it using only single-qubit measurements and one round of adaptation. At fixed accuracy, it needs logarithmically many copies as the qubit count grows, whereas every nonadaptive protocol needs exponentially many. The same mechanism yields an exponential gap in local asymptotic minimax mean squared error constants, tight up to the exponential base. The states are separable and classical in label-dependent product bases; no entanglement or other nonclassical correlations are required. The gap comes from measurement incompatibility: the candidate observables cannot be measured jointly, so a fixed measurement must divide its information budget among them, while adaptivity selects the informative one. Accordingly, the separation disappears when the candidate observables commute, and cannot occur with fully observed classical samples. Measurement incompatibility thus emerges as a resource for quantum learning, distinct from entanglement and quantum memory, that classical feedback alone can exploit.

</details>


### [103] [Fully Quantum Latent Diffusion for Synthetic Malware Records: Effective Denoising, Limited Class Information, and Costly Post-Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38974)
*Ban Q. Tran,Quang Duy Tran,Anh H. Nguyen,Ziwen Pan,Tommy Dang,Susan Mengel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出首个用于恶意软件记录的"全量子"潜变量扩散模型——由单中途测量的参数化电路去噪振幅编码的opcode潜向量，无经典神经层；在17个家族、10个种子上与随机态、完美生成器、参数匹配经典对照及经典潜扩散模型对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶意软件样本危害大且会泄露持有者事件，难以共享；合成替代品又可能泄露训练记录。量子扩散模型此前仅在图像或量子态上评估，其样本的效用、隐私与采样成本未知。

Method: 构造全量子潜变量扩散模型：单中途测量的参数化电路对振幅编码的opcode序列潜向量去噪，不含经典神经层。评估：17个恶意软件家族×10种子，对照随机态、完美生成器、参数匹配的经典控制、以及规模相当的经典潜扩散模型；测去噪不保真度、下游宏F1、真实数据增强效应、后选择采样接受率、训练惩罚、训练记录复制检测、基于长度的成员推断AUC（并对比DP长度直方图）。

Result: 电路将去噪不保真度降低63%；但其记录训练的判别器在真实数据上宏F1仅0.11（随机态0.08，完美生成器0.61，经典扩散0.44）；加入真实训练集使F1从0.635降至0.593。后选择采样接受率仅3.0×10⁻⁷，提高接受率至0.14的训练惩罚基本破坏去噪。无合成记录复制训练记录；解码长度平滑使长度成员推断AUC从0.80降至0.51，DP长度直方图以形式化保证达到同等效果。

Conclusion: 可共享安全数据的量子生成器必须联合评估效用、隐私与采样成本；本模型虽去噪有进展、无记忆泄露隐患可控，但可用性远逊经典模型且采样成本极高。

Abstract: Malware benchmarks are difficult to share, because the samples are harmful and a corpus can reveal the incidents of its holder, while synthetic substitutes can leak training records. Quantum diffusion models, however, have been evaluated only on images or quantum states, leaving the utility, privacy, and sampling cost of their samples unknown. In this research, we introduce a fully quantum latent diffusion model for malware records, in which a parameterized circuit with one mid-circuit measurement denoises amplitude-encoded latent vectors of opcode sequences without classical neural layers. On 17 malware families with ten seeds, we compared it with random states, a perfect generator, a parameter-matched classical control, and a classical latent diffusion model. The circuit lowers its denoising infidelity by 63\%, yet classifiers trained on its records reach a macro-F1 score of 0.11 on real records, against 0.08 for random states, 0.61 for a perfect generator, and 0.44 for a classical diffusion model of comparable size, and adding its records to real training data lowers the score from 0.635 to 0.593. Post-selected sampling accepts $3.0\times10^{-7}$ of runs, and a training penalty that raises the acceptance to 0.14 removes most of the denoising. No synthetic record copies a training record, and smoothing the decoded lengths lowers the area under the curve of a length-based membership test from 0.80 to 0.51, which a differentially private length histogram matches with a formal guarantee. These results indicate that quantum generators of shareable security data require a joint evaluation of utility, privacy, and sampling cost.

</details>


### [104] [High bandwidth heterodyne sensing with bulk NV diamond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39022)
*H. A. M. Taylor,J. P. Duff,L. B. Miller,A. Tritt,M. S. Oldfield,K. Helmerson,M. S. J. Barson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用强光/微波驱动的外差混频与强磁场梯度＋多路独立调制微波，显著扩展了金刚石NV色心磁量子传感器的瞬时带宽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 便携高性能传感器对地面与卫星通信意义重大，量子传感器在灵敏度和带宽上有潜力；但NV色心因自旋共振线宽窄，瞬时带宽受限，需在保持灵敏度的同时扩展带宽。

Method: 先采用强光与强微波驱动下的外差混频磁传感方案；再引入强磁场梯度与多路独立调制的微波控制场，将单一共振展宽为多个共振以扩展带宽。

Result: 外差方案在2.5×10⁻⁴ mm³有效金刚石体积、2×10¹¹个NV色心下实现MHz级瞬时带宽，灵敏度548 pT/√Hz；梯度＋多路调制方案将单共振带宽扩展至48 MHz，峰值灵敏度8.5 nT/√Hz。

Conclusion: 通过外差混频与磁场梯度多路微波调制，可在NV色心平台上实现从MHz到数十MHz的带宽扩展，为宽带磁量子传感及通信应用提供可行途径，但带宽与灵敏度之间存在权衡。

Abstract: Developing high performance portable sensors is of significant importance for both terrestrial and satellite communications, with quantum sensors offering potential advantages in sensitivity and bandwidth. The nitrogen vacancy center in diamond is a popular quantum sensing platform for its relative simplicity and wide range of acceptable operating conditions, but suffers a limited instantaneous bandwidth due to the narrow linewidth of the spin resonance. In this work, we first present a magnetic quantum sensing scheme utilizing heterodyne mixing with strong optical and microwave driving, achieving MHz-scale instantaneous bandwidth with a sensitivity of $548 \; \text{pT/}\sqrt{\text{Hz}}$ in an active diamond volume of $ 2.5\times 10^{-4} \; \text{mm}^3$ with $2 \times 10^{11}$ NV centers. We then demonstrate expansion of the sensing bandwidth by employing a strong magnetic gradient and many individually modulated microwave control fields, expanding the single resonance bandwidth to 48 MHz with a peak sensitivity of $8.5 \; \text{nT/}\sqrt{\text{Hz}}$.

</details>


### [105] [Individually Addressed Logical Pauli Measurements by QLDPC Surgery on a Trapped Ion Quantum Computer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39067)
*Surabhi Luthra,Alex Owens*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在Quantinuum H2-2离子阱处理器上，基于[[10,2,3]]广义自行车码首次演示了QLDPC手术（generalised lattice surgery），实现单轮X̄₁测量错误率0.72%，三轮解码后降至0.26%以下，并验证了旁观逻辑比特保持与共轭基gadget的组合性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QLDPC码有望数量级降低容错量子计算的量子比特开销，但需能选择性寻址多比特码块内单个逻辑比特的逻辑操作；QLDPC手术（利用辅助gadget系统做非破坏Pauli测量）是关键技术，此前缺乏硬件验证。

Method: 构建[[10,2,3]]广义自行车码，使用七个gadget比特执行单轮手术测量X̄₁；进行三轮综合征提取并跨轮解码；通过术后综合征确认回到码空间并评估旁观逻辑比特保持；进一步顺序测量X̄₁再Z̄₂（复用gadget比特）演示共轭基gadget组合。

Result: 单轮X̄₁手术测量错误率0.72 [0.32,1.41]%（95%可信区间），与后续transversal测量一致（1.03 [0.60,1.65]%）；三轮解码将gadget测量错误率压低至<0.26%；旁观比特在三轮测量中错误率0.21 [0.04,0.67]%；组合测量Z̄₂错误率1.12 [0.60,1.94]%。

Conclusion: 结果构成对QLDPC架构下基于Pauli计算所需的广义手术原语的硬件验证，证明gadget辅助逻辑测量、解码抑制、旁观比特保护与基变换组合在真实硬件上可行。

Abstract: Quantum low-density parity check (QLDPC) codes may lead to order-of-magnitude reductions in the qubit overhead required for fault-tolerant quantum computation. Achieving this improved overhead requires logical operations that selectively address individual logical qubits encoded within multi-qubit code blocks. Recently proposed architectures achieve this through QLDPC surgery, in which non-destructive Pauli measurements are performed using ancillary ''gadget'' systems transiently entangled with the code block, generalising lattice surgery. Here we implement QLDPC surgery on a [[10,2,3]] generalised bicycle code using Quantinuum's H2-2 trapped ion processor. A single-round surgery measurement using seven gadget qubits measures the $\bar{X}_1$ operator with a 0.72 [0.32, 1.41]% (95% credible interval) error rate and agrees with subsequent transversal measurement with 1.03 [0.60, 1.65]% error. Decoding across three rounds of syndrome extraction suppresses the gadget measurement error, reducing the error rate to below 0.26%. The syndrome after surgery confirms return to the code space and the unaddressed ''spectator'' qubit is preserved with error 0.21 [0.04, 0.67]% during the three-round measurement. We further demonstrate composition of conjugate basis gadgets by sequentially measuring $\bar{X}_1$ then $\bar{Z}_2$, reusing the gadget qubits, and obtain a 1.12 [0.60, 1.94]% error in the $\bar{Z}_2$ measurement. These results constitute a hardware validation of the generalised surgery primitive underlying Pauli-based computing for QLDPC architectures.

</details>


### [106] [Time-Switching Energy-Harvesting Quantum Relay-Assisted FSO Communication with Displaced Squeezed Signaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39091)
*Nizar Khalfet,Francisco A. Monteiro,Ioannis Krikidis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子中继辅助的自由空间光通信：中继采用时间切换光能收集与解码转发，并以位移压缩态（DSS）转发信息。通过将每跳建模为截断Gamma-Gamma透射率的纯损耗玻色信道，推导Helstrom最优检测下的平均错误率、高斯保真度与吞吐量界，证明压缩保持湍流分集阶数，其增益为有限光子预算效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非经典（压缩）信号在湍流FSO中的物理一致建模与性能收益缺乏严格量化：需刻画湍流损耗对量子态的映射，并厘清压缩相对相干中继的增益本质与适用范围。

Method: （1）双跳FSO系统：时间切换能量收集中继、解码转发、DSS转发；（2）每跳为纯损耗玻色信道，随机透射率服从截断Gamma-Gamma律（约束于物理可容许透射范围）；（3）相干态保持纯态，压缩态映射为混合高斯态；（4）相干跳：Helstrom最优检测下以精确Laplace级数形式给出平均错误；（5）DSS跳：推导输出态、闭式高斯保真度、Helstrom误差的双侧界，并证明存在损耗感知的最优光子分配；（6）建立保协方差基准的精确级数表示，得到吞吐量上界。

Result: 相干跳平均错误率获得精确Laplace级数表达；DSS跳获得输出态与闭式高斯保真度，并给出Helstrom误差的严格双侧界；证明存在损耗感知的最优光子划分；证明优化压缩同时保持湍流诱导分集阶数与相干中继的渐近主常数，故压缩增益为有限光子预算效应；证明存在内点吞吐量最优的收集比例；保协方差基准给出吞吐量解析上界，其绝对间隙随中继光子预算增长而消失；数值结果基于精确混合态Helstrom误差量化压缩收益及其有效区间。

Conclusion: 压缩转发不改变湍流分集与渐近主常数，其优势主要体现于有限光子预算区间；系统存在内点最优能量收集比例，为资源受限的量子中继FSO设计提供严格性能界与参数准则。

Abstract: This paper investigates a quantum relay-assisted free-space optical (FSO) communication system in which a decode-and-forward relay is powered by time-switching optical energy harvesting and forwards the decoded information using displaced squeezed signaling (DSS). To obtain a physically con sistent description of turbulence-induced loss for non-classical signaling, each hop is modeled as a pure-loss bosonic chan nel whose random transmissivity follows a truncated Gamma Gamma law, i.e., the Gamma-Gamma irradiance conditioned on the physically admissible range of a transmittance. Under this channel, coherent states remain pure, whereas squeezed states are mapped to mixed Gaussian states. Under Helstrom-optimal detection, the average error of the coherent hop is derived in exact Laplace-series form. For the DSS hop, we derive the output states, a closed-form Gaussian fidelity with rigorous two sided bounds on the Helstrom error, and prove the existence of a loss-aware optimal photon partition. Furthermore, we prove that optimized squeezing preserves both the turbulence-induced diversity order and the leading asymptotic constant of coherent relaying, so that its benefit is a finite-photon-budget effect, and that an interior throughput-optimal harvesting fraction exists. A covariance-preserving benchmark with an exact series representation yields an analytical upper bound on the achievable throughput whose absolute gap vanishes as the relay photon budget grows. Numerical results based on the exact mixed-state Helstrom error quantify the benefit of squeezing and the regime in which it is useful.

</details>


### [107] [Reverse Quantum Annealing for Ground-State Energy Calculation in Molecule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39129)
*Pradyot Pritam Sahoo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文采用反向量子退火与量子退火本征求解器计算碱土金属氟化物分子的电子基态能量，通过将Dirac–Coulomb电子哈密顿量划分为子QUBO并以优化的反向退火调度精化正向退火解，系统性地抑制残余构型误差，为在量子退火框架下实现亚毫哈特里化学精度提供路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子电子结构的高精度计算是量子化学的核心目标，而现有量子退火方法在求解电子基态时存在残余构型误差、精度难以达到化学精度（sub-milliHartree）的问题；作者希望验证反向量子退火在提升碱土金属单氟化物基态能量精度方面的可行性，并为量子退火范式下的分子电子结构计算建立一条可推广的技术路线。

Method: 1) 将Dirac–Coulomb电子哈密顿量按子QUBO（sub-QUBO）方式进行划分，以降低单次退火问题的规模与复杂度；2) 先运行正向退火获得初始解，再采用经过优化的反向退火调度（reverse-annealing schedule）对解进行精化；3) 使用量子退火本征求解器（Quantum Annealing Eigensolver, QAE）算法提取电子基态波函数与能量；4) 研究对象为碱土金属单氟化物（alkaline-earth monofluoride）分子。

Result: 通过对正向退火解施加优化的反向退火调度，残余构型误差被系统性地最小化，从而获得更精确的电子基态波函数与基态能量；结果指向可实现亚毫哈特里（sub-milliHartree）量级的化学精度。

Conclusion: 反向量子退火结合子QUBO划分与QAE算法可有效抑制构型误差、提升基态能量精度，为量子退火范式下的分子电子结构计算及达到亚毫哈特里化学精度提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We present the results of our reverse quantum annealing computations of the ground-state energies of alkaline-earth monofluoride molecules. By partitioning the Dirac--Coulomb electronic Hamiltonian into sub-QUBOs and refining forward-annealing solutions through optimized reverse-annealing schedules, residual configuration errors are systematically minimized. The electronic ground-state wave functions and energies are obtained using the Quantum Annealing Eigensolver algorithm. This work provides a pathway for achieving sub-milliHartree chemical accuracy and advancing molecular electronic structure calculations in the quantum annealing paradigm.

</details>


### [108] [Beyond energy bounds: comparing Schrödinger eigenstates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39136)
*Xiao-Ke Wang,Han Jia,Shengjun Wu,Zeng-Bing Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文从有序Schrödinger算子导出本征态比较，将能量界推广至重叠、空间分布与节点；给出三算子的重叠界、低能子空间与激发态重叠估计、一维/径向的似然比与节点序比较，并应用于Yukawa–Hulthén体系得到临界屏蔽的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典比较定理（如Sturm比较、能级单调性）主要针对本征值，缺乏对本征态本身（重叠、密度分布、节点位置）的定量比较工具。作者希望把"有序算子"的能量比较思想系统推广到本征态层面，并用其解决Yukawa/Hulthén这类无解析解体系的临界屏蔽估计问题。

Method: (1) 对三个按形式排序、具有离散比较基态的算子，在"上方基态能量落入下方算子基态能隙"的假设下，用三个比较谱值界定三个基态平方重叠，并保证存在简单的离散中间基态；(2) 借助能隙估计控制低能子空间及附加能隙条件激发态的重叠；(3) 在一维与径向情形，用势差相对能量平移的单次穿越（非增差即可）对基态似然比与单调可观测量排序；(4) 对次模参考势用坐标方向比较；(5) 用双侧Sturm论证对非减势差排序内部节点，可处理正交激发态。

Result: 覆盖非对称势阱与缩短的右Dirichlet壁情形；对Yukawa基态，Hulthén比较给出重叠、径向矩及核处密度双侧界（屏蔽0<δ<2/√6时成立）；优化缩放Hulthén优函数得到n²δ_c(n,0) ≳ 1.13611的下界，且该界在两参数比较族内最优。

Conclusion: 有序Schrödinger算子的比较原理可一致地扩展至本征态的重叠、分布与节点结构，形成统一的定量框架；该框架为无解析解体系的临界参数（如临界屏蔽）提供可证且族内最优的严格下界。

Abstract: We derive eigenstate comparisons from ordered Schrödinger operators, extending energy bounds to overlaps, spatial distributions and nodes. For three form-ordered operators with discrete comparison ground states, suppose the upper ground-state energy lies in the lower operator's ground-state gap. Three comparison spectral values then bound all three squared ground-state overlaps and guarantee a simple discrete intermediate ground state. Related gap estimates control low-energy subspaces and individual excited-state overlaps under additional gap conditions. In one dimension and radially, a single crossing of the potential difference relative to the energy shift orders ground-state likelihood ratios and monotone observables; a nondecreasing difference suffices. The results cover asymmetric wells and shortened right Dirichlet walls, with coordinatewise comparisons for submodular reference potentials and coordinatewise nondecreasing perturbations. A two-sided Sturm argument orders corresponding internal nodes for nondecreasing potential differences, even between orthogonal one-dimensional excited states. For Yukawa ground states, Hulthén comparisons bound overlaps, radial moments and the density at the nucleus; the latter two-sided bounds hold for screening $0<δ<2/\sqrt6$. Optimizing scaled Hulthén majorants gives a lower bound of approximately $1.13611$ for $n^2δ_c(n,0)$, where $δ_c(n,0)$ is the critical screening for the $n$th $s$-wave bound state. This bound is optimal within that two-parameter comparison family.

</details>


### [109] [Signatures of Narrowband and Ultrashort-Pulse-Like Probing in Coherent Anti-Stokes Raman Scattering Using Twin Beams and Sum-Frequency Generation Readout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39138)
*Yujiro Eto,Hideaki Kano,Masahiro Takeoka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 理论上研究了由明亮双光束驱动、以和频产生(SFG)作为读出手段的相干反斯托克斯拉曼散射(CARS)，并用量子场关联函数揭示了多种关联结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CARS探测中探针脉冲的光谱卷积会掩盖拉曼弛豫等本征信息，需寻找避免探针诱导光谱卷积的机制，并厘清双光束驱动下不同相互作用路径的贡献。

Method: 将SFG输出用高阶量子场关联函数展开，按bra侧与ket侧的不同相互作用路径对关联结构分类；分析CARS探针场与SFG门控场之间的频率关联。

Result: 发现探针场与门控场的频率关联使二者集体充当有效窄带探针，从而避免探针诱导的光谱卷积；当该行为出现在bra或ket一侧时，另一侧仍保留如同被超短脉冲探测的拉曼弛豫信息。

Conclusion: SFG读出的双光束CARS可通过量子关联在时频分辨上实现"一侧窄带、一侧超短"的效果，为抑制光谱卷积、提取拉曼弛豫动力学提供理论依据。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate coherent anti-Stokes Raman scattering (CARS) driven by bright twin beams with sum-frequency generation (SFG) readout. Expressing the SFG output in terms of quantum-field correlation functions yields multiple correlation structures with distinct properties associated with different interaction pathways on the bra and ket sides. Among them, frequency correlations between the CARS probe and SFG gating fields enable the two fields to act collectively as an effective narrowband probe, avoiding probe-induced spectral convolution. When this behavior appears on either the bra or ket side, the other retains Raman relaxation information as if probed by an ultrashort pulse.

</details>


### [110] [Resonance and Transition-Channel Competition in Cavity-Assisted Floquet-State Stabilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39169)
*Zhuocheng Ma,Hongzheng Zhao,Liang-You Peng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 静耦合耗散腔虽能通过能量选择性弛豫稳定Floquet本征态，但在中等驱动频率下，仅靠共振条件不足，稳态由共振选择与腔诱导Floquet态间跃迁强度的竞争/协同决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前方案认为腔光子能量与准能差（模驱动量子数）匹配即可自发稳定Floquet本征态，但该共振判据在中等驱动频率下是否充分尚不明确，需要厘清稳定成立的条件。

Method: 分析驱动量子系统与静态耗散腔的耦合：腔提供能量选择性弛豫通道，经Floquet态间跃迁后伴随不可逆光子损耗；考察共振选择与腔诱导跃迁强度的相互作用，并推广至多能级spin-S体系。

Result: 中等驱动频率下共振条件并不充分：二者可协同导致成功稳定，也可在共振下产生混合稳态，并在Floquet区杂化附近出现完全失效。该机制可推广至spin-S多能级系统。

Conclusion: 静态耗散可靠稳定驱动量子态取决于共振选择与跃迁强度的协同，而非仅共振匹配；该工作给出了判断稳态能否可靠稳定的一般框架。

Abstract: Static coupling to a dissipative cavity has been proposed as a route to autonomously stabilize Floquet eigenstates. The cavity provides an energy-selective relaxation channel: when its photon energy matches a quasienergy difference modulo drive quanta, it mediates transitions between Floquet states, followed by irreversible photon loss. Here, we show that at intermediate driving frequencies, this resonance condition alone is not sufficient for stabilization. Instead, the steady state is controlled by the interplay between resonance selection and the cavity-induced transition strengths between Floquet states, which can either cooperate or compete. This interplay leads to successful stabilization, mixed steady states despite resonance, and complete breakdown near Floquet-zone hybridization. We further show that the same mechanism extends to multilevel spin-S systems, providing a general framework for understanding when static dissipation can reliably stabilize driven quantum states.

</details>


### [111] [Force Sensing in a Recoverable Bosonic Quantum Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39208)
*Tairong Bai,Wen Zheng,Haoyang Cai,Xianke Li,Yangyang Ge,Kun Zhou,Xiangmin Yu,Yu Zhang,Shaoxiong Li,Xinsheng Tan,Jie Zhao,Yang Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 构建有限能量玻色子码，使单一振子同时实现对外力的感知与对未知逻辑量子比特的主动保护，在连续光子损耗下实现与逻辑态无关的端点探针读出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续变量量子信息中，传感与量子纠错在同一模式上存在内在权衡：传感器需累积微弱外部位移，而玻色子码通常将此类位移当作误差抑制。本文旨在使二者兼容。

Method: 将逻辑振幅编码于离散波包位点，并把力信号收集到共享的内部坐标中；采用二项式位点恢复方案，同时抵消依赖于波包的探针畸变与逻辑误差；并针对超导量子电路在光子数退相位与Kerr非线性竞争下进行基准测试。

Result: 实现与逻辑态无关的端点探针读出；对(2r+2)波包码，存储保真度损失被抑制至O(γ^{r+1})，残余的存储-探针关联降至O(γ^{r+2})；基准测试识别出优选的编码区间。

Conclusion: 有限能量玻色子码可在光子损耗下兼容弱力传感与逻辑量子比特保护，并为超导硬件中的编码方案选择提供依据。

Abstract: In continuous-variable quantum information, sensing and quantum error correction in the same mode face an intrinsic trade-off: sensors must accumulate weak external displacements, whereas bosonic codes typically suppress such shifts as errors. Here, we establish compatibility by constructing finite-energy bosonic codes that enable a single oscillator to sense an external force while actively protecting an unknown logical qubit. By encoding logical amplitudes across discrete wave-packet sites and capturing the force signal in a shared internal coordinate, we achieve an endpoint probe readout that is independent of the logical state under continuous photon loss $γ$. Crucially, binomial site recovery cancels packet-dependent probe distortions alongside logical errors: for a $(2r+2)$-packet code, memory infidelity is suppressed to $O(γ^{r+1})$ while residual memory-probe correlations scale as $O(γ^{r+2})$. Finally, superconducting quantum circuit benchmarks under competing photon-number dephasing and Kerr nonlinearity identify preferred encoding regimes for hardware implementation.

</details>


### [112] [Geometric Rényi divergence and RLD Fisher information under quantum processes without definite causal order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39217)
*Zixin Huang,Mark M. Wilde*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文证明了在不具有确定因果顺序的量子过程中，确定性完全正过程输出端的几何Rényi散度不超过各单次信道使用散度之和，对所有Rényi阶数(0,1)∪(1,2]及任意数量可独立替换的信道成立；并将该散度不等式转化为RLD Fisher信息加性界与Cramér–Rao型精度极限，进而给出信道判别、排除、通信与私密态生成的有限次使用逆定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不定因果序量子实验可用于区分器件、估计物理参数与通信，但缺乏统一的基本限制。作者希望给出一个统一的不等式框架，同时约束不定因果序下器件分辨与参数估计等任务的性能。

Method: 以几何Rényi散度为工具，利用"任意独立信道选择都必须产生归一化输出"这一约束，证明确定性完全正过程输出的散度不超过各信道使用散度之和（加性不等式）；当各信道取参考辅助输入并行实验时可逼近该界，并在对过程优化后达到等式。进一步通过小信道微扰，将散度不等式转为固定过程下参数依赖信道的RLD Fisher信息加性界，并推导不假设确定因果顺序的Cramér–Rao界。

Result: 建立了对所有Rényi阶(0,1)∪(1,2]的几何Rényi散度加性上界及优化后精确等式；证明了每固定过程下RLD Fisher信息的加性界，得到局部无偏估计的Cramér–Rao界，并有擦除概率实例达到该界；还给出信道判别与排除的有限次使用界，以及在过程保持比较条件时的通信与私密态生成逆定理。

Conclusion: "归一化输出"要求统一控制正局部操作，是导出散度与Fisher信息界限的核心机制；小信道微扰可将散度不等式转换为Fisher信息界（含奇异信道情形）。该框架为不定因果序下的信息计量任务提供了通用上限。

Abstract: Quantum experiments without a definite causal order offer new ways to distinguish devices, estimate physical parameters, and communicate. We prove a common limit on these tasks: the geometric Rényi divergence at the output of a deterministic completely positive process cannot exceed the sum of the divergences of its individual channel uses. The inequality holds for every Rényi order in $(0,1)\cup(1,2]$ and any number of independently replaceable channels. Parallel experiments with separate reference-assisted inputs approach the bound, giving an exact equality after optimization over processes. We also prove an additive bound on the right logarithmic derivative Fisher information of parameter-dependent channels under every fixed process in this class. A resulting Cramér--Rao bound limits locally unbiased estimation precision without assuming a definite causal order, and an erasure-probability example attains the bound. The proofs for the geometric Rényi divergence and RLD Fisher information follow from the requirement that every independent choice of channels produce a normalized output. We show how this requirement controls positive local operations, and how small channel perturbations convert the divergence inequality into a Fisher-information bound, including for singular channels. Further applications give finite-use bounds on channel discrimination and exclusion, together with communication and private-state-generation converses when the processes preserve the relevant comparison conditions.

</details>


### [113] [Beyond Direct Imaging: Quantum Measurement Design for Small-Target Detection in UAV Hyperspectral Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39226)
*Qipeng Qian,Jialiang Jin,Shihao Wang,Yuntao Qian*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出量子辅助空间模式高光谱探测框架，用于无人机场景下检测被衍射混合的微小矿物斑块，通过自适应接收器在共享曝光预算下优于完整直接成像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小矿物斑块在衍射作用下其光谱与周围岩石混合，传统高光谱探测器只分析记录强度，检测困难；作者希望从光学测量层面本身获取目标证据。

Method: 建立统一的辐射度与衍射模型，同时描述直接成像与空间模式测量；理论上刻画经背景拟合后仍保留的目标变化、模式测量的信息优势条件、模式失配与未分辨输出带来的损失，并给出共基条件（固定几何下物质光谱可共享空间测量）；采用成像先导选择候选模式的自适应接收器，结合两阶段做全区域存在性检验，基于实测矿物光谱与已发表UAV采集尺度进行仿真。

Result: 在共享主动曝光预算（含先导开销与光学损耗）下优于完整直接成像；在经验1%区域级虚警率匹配时，代表性目标检测率由62.95%提升至69.65%；配对分析支持在配准不确定性与受控非均匀背景下仍有增益；标定的模式误差可揭示测量损失削弱或反转增益的情形。

Conclusion: 将背景可辨识性与接收器选择及其工作条件相联系，为在光谱通道之外协同设计空间测量以检测微小物质特征提供框架。

Abstract: Small mineral patches can be spectrally distinct yet difficult to detect when diffraction mixes their light with the surrounding rock. Conventional hyperspectral detectors analyze recorded intensities, leaving an opportunity to recover target evidence through the optical measurement itself. We develop a quantum-assisted framework for exploiting this opportunity in prospective unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) hyperspectral sensing. A common radiometric and diffraction model describes direct imaging and spatial-mode measurements of the same light. The theory identifies target changes that survive background fitting and explains when mode measurements retain more detection information. It quantifies losses from mode mismatch and unresolved outputs, linking the quantum benchmark to finite receiver design. A common-basis result identifies when material spectra can share a spatial measurement at fixed geometry. An adaptive receiver uses an imaging pilot to select candidate modes and combines both stages in a full-region presence test. Simulations based on measured mineral spectra and published UAV acquisition scales demonstrate gains over complete direct imaging at a shared active-exposure budget, including pilot cost and optical loss. At matched empirical 1\% region-level false alarms, detection increases from 62.95\% to 69.65\% for a representative target. Paired analysis supports gains with registration uncertainty and, separately, under controlled nonuniform backgrounds. Calibrated mode errors reveal where measurement losses weaken or reverse the benefit. These results connect background identifiability to receiver choices and their operating conditions. They provide a framework for designing spatial measurements alongside spectral channels to detect small material features.

</details>


### [114] [A VLSI-Like Design and Verification Flow for Quantum Circuits: A Case Study in PI Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39263)
*Yu-Ting Kao,Yeong-Jar Chang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出类似VLSI的四级量子电路设计与验证流程（行为级、RTL级、门级、含量子算法的全电路集成），以量子蒙特卡洛估计π为案例，并指出网格采样可带来二次改进，暗示QAE的二次加速可能源于采样策略而非电路本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电路设计缺乏系统化、可复用的设计方法学，现有工作多依赖预定义电路模板，难以支持Shor、Grover、量子振幅估计等应用专用操作，成为通向容错量子计算（FTQC）的瓶颈。

Method: 借鉴传统VLSI数字设计原则，构建涵盖行为建模、寄存器传输级（RTL）、门级、以及与量子算法集成的全电路四级设计与验证流程；以量子蒙特卡洛法估计π为案例进行实现与经典模拟；并用网格采样替代随机采样进行对照实验。

Result: 流程给出从规格到大规模量子电路实现的系统化路径，支持从规格、RTL、门级等多个入口灵活启动设计；经典模拟显示，在不使用量子计算的情况下，网格采样相对随机采样可获得二次改进。

Conclusion: 该四级流程可缓解量子电路设计瓶颈，推动量子计算实用化部署；同时提示π估计案例中的二次加速可能来自网格采样策略而非QAE电路本身，需谨慎归因量子加速来源。

Abstract: This paper proposes a VLSI-like design and verification flow for quantum circuit implementation, covering four levels: behavioral modeling, register-transfer level (RTL), gate level, and full-circuit integration with quantum algorithms. Using quantum Monte Carlo methods for pi estimation as a case study, the proposed flow provides a systematic path from initial specifications to large-scale quantum circuit implementation. The paper highlights two key contributions. First, quantum digital design supports a broad range of applications beyond predefined circuit templates. This capability is particularly important for application-specific operations in algorithms such as Shor's, Grover's, and quantum amplitude estimation (QAE), and can significantly advance fault-tolerant quantum computing (FTQC). Second, classical simulation shows that replacing random sampling with grid sampling can achieve a quadratic improvement in this pi-estimation case without quantum computation. This suggests that the quadratic speedup may arise from the grid sampling strategy rather than from the QAE circuit itself. By adapting conventional VLSI digital design principles, this four-level flow addresses a key bottleneck in quantum circuit design and enables flexible design initiation from multiple entry points, including specifications, RTL, and gate-level descriptions, paving the way for the practical deployment of quantum computing.

</details>


### [115] [Efficient Quantum Optimization via Dissipative False Vacuum Decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39266)
*Zhi-Hao Huang,Tian-Mu Hu,Shuaifan Cao,Ye Lu,Xiaopeng Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于腔耗散的量子求解器，利用组态空间中的"假真空衰变"机制逃离崎岖能量景观的亚稳局域极小，用于求解编码于原子自旋哈密顿量中的NP难组合优化问题，其估计时间复杂度优于标准Grover搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期量子平台迫切需要高效求解计算困难问题的量子求解器；NP难组合优化问题的能量景观崎岖、存在大量亚稳局域极小，标准方法易被陷入，需要可调控的物理资源来辅助逃逸。

Method: 构造基于腔的耗散量子求解器，处理仅含少数几种离散单自旋翻转跃迁能的原子自旋哈密顿量所编码的NP难组合优化问题；机制上利用组态空间中的耗散性假真空衰变，使系统从亚稳局域极小中逃逸；并通过MaxCut与最大独立集（MIS）的数值研究确定有利于基态制备的参数区域，结合中位混合时间与实例平均基态制备概率的有限尺寸标度分析估计时间复杂度。

Result: 数值研究识别出有利于基态制备的参数区域；有限尺寸标度显示中位混合时间与实例平均基态制备概率对应的估计时间复杂度优于标准Grover搜索。

Conclusion: 腔耗散可作为一种可控资源用于量子优化，为近期量子平台上的组合优化问题提供了有前景的耗散型求解途径。

Abstract: Efficient quantum solvers for computationally hard problems are highly desirable for near-term quantum platforms. Here, we propose a cavity-based dissipative quantum solver for a class of NP-hard combinatorial optimization problems encoded in atomic-spin Hamiltonians with a few discrete single-spin-flip transition energies. We show that the cavity-based quantum solver exploits dissipative false-vacuum decay in configuration space to facilitate escape from metastable local minima in rugged energy landscapes. Numerical studies of MaxCut and Maximum Independent Set identify favorable parameter regions for ground-state preparation. Based on the finite-size scalings of the median mixing time and instance-averaged ground-state preparation probability, we estimate time complexities for our solver that are more favorable than those of standard Grover search. These results highlight cavity dissipation as a controllable resource for quantum optimization.

</details>


### [116] [NAKO: A Benchmarking Framework for Evaluating and Diagnosing Neutral-Atom Quantum Compilers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39296)
*Wenjie Sun,Zhigang Wang,Xiaoyu Li,Desheng Zheng,Lianhui Yu,Geng Chen,Guowu Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出NAKO基准框架，为可重构中性原子编译器提供可达资源目标与构造性最优参考调度，并将多余开销归因到具体编译决策。对Enola与OLSQ-DPQA的648次评测定位了各自瓶颈，诊断引导干预使Enola平均移动减少78.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性原子处理器中门调度、原子放置与并行传输相互耦合，编译决策共同决定物理执行成本；现有评测缺乏可达的资源参考，也无法将超额成本追溯到具体编译决策。

Method: NAKO按量子比特数、逻辑深度及一/二比特门密度参数化电路，为Rydberg阶段、路由区间与移动层设定可达目标；每个基准配可执行参考调度，在给定执行模型下保证Rydberg阶段与路由区间数最少、且最小Rydberg阶段调度中移动最小；通过参考映射与中间物理状态进行受控干预以定位成本来源。

Result: 648次运行评测中：Enola的放置评分与跨阶段布局管理导致额外移动，诊断引导干预在5个匹配电路上平均移动减少78.9%；OLSQ-DPQA的过早候选选择与预备状态探索不足造成可避免的纠缠阶段和路由区间，四个固定检查点续算各消除一个Rydberg阶段与一个路由区间且不增加移动。

Conclusion: NAKO将具有构造性最优性保证的资源目标与受控诊断、验证相结合，可量化并验证特定编译器的资源节省。

Abstract: Reconfigurable neutral-atom processors couple gate scheduling, atom placement, and parallel atom transport, so compiler decisions jointly determine physical execution costs. Evaluating these compilers requires attainable resource references and a way to trace excess costs to their causes. We present NAKO, a benchmarking framework that parameterizes circuits by qubit count, logical depth, and requested one- and two-qubit gate densities, and assigns attainable targets for Rydberg stages, routing intervals, and movement layers. Each benchmark is paired with an executable reference schedule; under the stated execution model, construction guarantees minimum Rydberg-stage and routing-interval counts, and minimum movement among schedules with the minimum Rydberg-stage count. Comparing compiler outputs with these references quantifies resource gaps, while reference mappings and intermediate physical states enable controlled interventions that trace excess costs to specific compiler decisions. In a 648-run evaluation of Enola and OLSQ-DPQA, NAKO identifies distinct bottlenecks. For Enola, placement scoring and cross-stage layout management contribute to excess movement; diagnosis-guided interventions reduce mean movement by 78.9% across five matched circuits. For OLSQ-DPQA, premature candidate selection and limited exploration of preparatory states produce avoidable entangling stages and routing intervals; four fixed-checkpoint continuations each eliminate one Rydberg stage and one routing interval without increasing movement. NAKO thus links resource targets backed by constructive optimality guarantees to controlled diagnosis and validation of compiler-specific resource savings.

</details>


### [117] [Provably efficient experimental classification of quantum data with quantum kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39350)
*Keitaro Anai,Akimoto Nakayama,Shintaro Ooka,Hiroko Tomoda,Yuto Morohoshi,Yohji Chin,Shigehito Miki,Masahiro Yabuno,Hirotaka Terai,Peter van Loock,Kosuke Mitarai,Shuntaro Takeda*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出高效量子态分类的理论框架并在可编程连续变量光子处理器上实验验证，成功实现 stellar rank（非高斯性度量）分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分析量子系统对量子信息处理至关重要，但若不利用系统或任务结构，测量次数可能呈指数增长。量子机器学习有望通过学习量子数据降低这一成本，需要建立理论保证并实验验证。

Method: 建立量子态分类效率的理论框架，将学习效率与量子数据结构关联，不依赖针对每个任务的物理描述；推导测量次数随系统规模多项式增长的准确分类充分条件；将保证应用于连续变量量子计算中的 stellar rank 分类问题；开发可编程 CV 光子处理器，能制备与处理多种高斯及非高斯态并进行虚拟投影。

Result: 实验实现单模 CV 量子态 stellar rank 0 与 1 的分类，尽管存在实验缺陷仍实现准确分类；该处理器突破了多数现有平台仅支持高斯的局限。

Conclusion: 建立了理论指导且实验验证的量子态分类框架，可在当今可用的量子设备上实现。

Abstract: Analyzing quantum systems is essential for quantum information processing, but can require exponentially many measurements without exploiting the structure of the system or task. Quantum machine learning offers a promising way to reduce this cost by learning from quantum data. Here, we theoretically establish a framework for efficient quantum-state classification and experimentally demonstrate the resulting scheme on a programmable photonic processor. Our analysis links learning efficiency to the structure of quantum data, without relying on a physical description tailored to each task. We derive sufficient conditions for accurate classification with a number of measurements polynomial in system size. We apply this guarantee to a resource problem in continuous-variable (CV) quantum computing: classifying stellar rank, a measure of non-Gaussianity. Experimentally, we implement the classification of single-mode CV quantum states with stellar rank zero and one. This is enabled by developing a programmable CV photonic processor capable of preparing and processing diverse Gaussian and non-Gaussian states and performing virtual projection onto such states, going beyond the Gaussian-only capabilities of most existing photonic platforms. The experiment demonstrates accurate classification despite experimental imperfections. Our results establish a theory-guided and experimentally validated framework for quantum-state classification that can be implemented on quantum devices available today.

</details>


### [118] [Composable Device-Independent Quantum Digital Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39360)
*Jing-Wei Bian,Bing-Hong Li,Xu-Yang Lu,Shi-Gen Li,Hua-Lei Yin,Zeng-Bing Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在构造性密码学框架下首次建立了量子数字签名（QDS）的可组合安全框架，并设计实现了一个设备无关（DI）QDS协议，给出底层LFSR-Toeplitz认证的相关密钥安全性界，数值仿真表明可在城域尺度上高效签署多比特文档。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将设备无关量子密码学扩展到可组合地支撑全部基本信息安全目标，对安全量子网络至关重要，而这需要可组合的量子数字签名以提供完整性、真实性与不可否认性。然而现有QDS协议通常依赖对量子设备的假设，其安全分析多局限于独立（stand-alone）场景，且未处理基于LFSR的Toeplitz认证所面临的相关密钥攻击。

Method: 在构造性密码学（constructive cryptography）中建立首个QDS可组合安全框架；采用一次性通用哈希（one-time universal hashing）与多信道复用设计DI-QDS协议以实现相应的理想QDS资源；推导底层LFSR-Toeplitz认证的相关密钥安全界，从而在有限码长情形下证明不可伪造性。

Result: 建立了QDS的可组合安全框架并实现了对应理想资源；给出相关密钥安全界，扩展了以往QDS安全分析的伪造边界；有限码长下的数值仿真表明，协议可在城域尺度距离上高效签署多比特文档，具备实际可行性。

Conclusion: QDS由此成为可组合的密码学原语，设备无关量子密码学被扩展到在未来量子网络中以可组合安全保证支撑完整的信息安全目标。

Abstract: Extending device-independent (DI) quantum cryptography to composably support all fundamental information security objectives is crucial for secure quantum networks. Achieving this goal requires composable quantum digital signatures (QDSs), which support integrity, authenticity, and nonrepudiation when integrated into quantum networks. Yet existing QDS protocols generally rely on assumptions about the underlying quantum devices, while their security analyses remain largely restricted to stand-alone settings, leaving related-key attacks on the underlying linear feedback shift register (LFSR)-based Toeplitz authentication unaddressed. Here, we establish the first composable security framework for QDS within constructive cryptography, and realize the corresponding ideal QDS resource by developing a DI-QDS protocol employing one-time universal hashing and multi-channel multiplexing. We further derive a related-key security bound for the underlying LFSR-based Toeplitz authentication, thereby establishing unforgeability while extending the forgery boundary considered in previous QDS security analyses. We also demonstrate the practical feasibility of our DI-QDS protocol under realistic quantum network conditions in the finite-size regime, with numerical simulations showing that it enables efficient signing of multibit documents over metropolitan-scale distances. Our results establish QDS as a composable cryptographic primitive and extend DI quantum cryptography to support the full range of information security objectives with composable security guarantees in future quantum networks.

</details>


### [119] [Analytic Solutions To Fully Quantized Parametric Amplification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39387)
*Wanli Xing,Timothy C. Ralph*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对全量子三线性哈密顿量（三波混频与参量放大）给出可观测量动力学的解析解，通过对海森堡展开的重整化（再求和），在无参量近似、无拟合参数的前提下给出可直接计算的1–3阶关联函数，并在泵浦耗尽全程达到约1e-12的数值精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三波混频与参量放大的标准处理依赖参量近似（泵浦视为经典、不耗尽），在强泵浦耗尽和振荡周期区域失效；同时缺乏对高阶关联、无自由参数且可直接求值的解析结果，因此需要建立不依赖参量近似的精确解析框架。

Method: 从相干泵浦与真空信号、闲频初态出发，在完全量子化的三线性哈密顿量下，将海森堡方程的解按“再求和的泵浦耗尽项”与“回转换项”重组，构造解析的耗尽谱与回转换谱；由此得到一阶、二阶、三阶关联函数的显式可求值表达式，并进一步处理一阶耗尽情形以获得四阶关联，同时推广至亮压缩泵浦输入。

Result: 数值基准显示，在整个泵浦耗尽过程中误差量级为1e-12；在数十个耗尽—回转换周期内、直至振荡首次完全坍缩前，误差低于1e-3。一阶耗尽的处理额外给出四阶关联，并可容纳亮压缩泵浦输入。

Conclusion: 该框架无需参量近似与拟合参数即可高精度解析描述强耗尽的三波混频动力学，涵盖高阶关联与压缩泵浦情形，并可推广至更高阶矩，对光学量子技术具有潜在价值。

Abstract: We derive analytic expressions for observable dynamics under the fully quantized trilinear Hamiltonian, governing quantum three-wave mixing and parametric amplification. Starting from a coherent pump and vacuum signal and idler, the calculation reorganizes the Heisenberg expansion into resummed depletion and return profiles, yielding directly evaluable expressions for all 1st-, 2nd- and 3rd-order correlations. The construction uses no parametric approximation or fitted parameters. Representative numerical benchmarks show errors of order $10^{-12}$ throughout the entire pump depletion, and below $10^{-3}$ across dozens of depletion-and-back-conversion cycles, up to the first total collapse of the oscillation. The first-depletion treatment additionally provides 4th-order correlations, accommodates bright squeezed-pump inputs and reveals phenomena important to future optical quantum technologies. The framework can be extended to higher moments.

</details>


### [120] [Conditional Information Flow and Neural Representability of One-Dimensional Rotated Cluster States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39392)
*Ziqi Xu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 以一维旋转团簇态（各转角下精确MPS键维恒为D=2）为例，证明远距离测量结果的条件依赖不需要大的内部记忆；并通过多种学习器对比说明把小的虚拟记忆嵌入模型架构可显著简化优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究"远距离条件依赖"（条件互信息CMI）是否必然要求量子态具备大的内部记忆/键维，并考察不同学习器能否在仅凭部分读出分布的情况下复原目标态及其相位。

Method: 用精确MPS收缩计算两种读出协议（纠缠交换端点读出与常规团簇态的稳态体读出）的CMI随转角θ的变化；以自旋-1 AKLT链作为精确对照（I_m=(4/9)3^{-m}）；在N=64、θ=0.35 的同一目标上比较正自回归GRU、复数RBM、通用循环变分蒙特卡罗，以及以键矢量为隐状态的MPS诱导循环模型（χ=2与χ=1对照）。

Result: 端点CMI从θ=0的1比特降至θ=π/2的0；体读出在两极限均消失。AKLT链CMI长度恰等于两点关联长度（可辨识但非普适原因）。GRU可从数千样本学到计算基Born分布，但精确可算的Bell基读出表明该分布不能锁定相位；复数RBM在精确目标下达到N=8基态；通用循环VMC在短程试验结束时仍在改进；MPS诱导循环模型在χ=2时把基态优化到接近机器精度，χ=1对照失败。

Conclusion: 长程CMI不要求大键维；把小的虚拟记忆直接构建进网络架构可大幅缓解优化难度，而仅匹配Born分布不足以确定量子态的相位信息。

Abstract: I study how measurement outcomes can remain conditionally dependent over long distances while the underlying state carries only a small internal memory, using one-dimensional rotated cluster states, whose exact matrix product state (MPS) bond dimension is $D=2$ at every rotation angle. Using exact MPS contraction, I compute the conditional mutual information (CMI) of two readout protocols: the entanglement-swapping endpoint CMI decreases from one bit at $θ=0$ to zero at $θ=π/2$, whereas a stationary bulk readout of the locally equivalent conventional cluster state vanishes at both limits. As an exact control, the spin-1 AKLT chain obeys $I_m=(4/9)\,3^{-m}$, so its CMI length equals its two-point correlation length, for an identifiable but non-generic reason. I then compare learners on the same $N=64$, $θ=0.35$ target. A positive autoregressive GRU learns the computational-basis Born distribution from a few thousand samples, but an exactly computable Bell-basis readout shows that this distribution does not fix the phases. A complex RBM reaches the $N=8$ ground state under an exact objective, while a short generic recurrent variational Monte Carlo pilot is still improving when its runs end. As a positive control, an MPS-induced recurrent model whose hidden state is the bond vector reaches the ground state to near machine precision at $χ=2$, whereas a $χ=1$ control fails. These results show that long CMI does not require a large bond dimension, and that building the small virtual memory into the architecture greatly eases optimization.

</details>


### [121] [Pöschl-Teller type of Jaynes-Cummings Hamiltonians: SUSY transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39401)
*İsmail Burak Ateş,Şengül Kuru,Javier Negro,Efe Serbest*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文借助超对称量子力学方法定义并研究广义Jaynes-Cummings (JC) 哈密顿量，将三角型与双曲型Pöschl-Teller (PT) 势作为修正标准JC模型的两个自然要素，并系统比较其与标准JC模型的共有特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准JC模型是光与二能级原子相互作用的基本模型，但形式较为特殊。作者希望确认JC哈密顿量是否只是更广泛的广义哈密顿量类中的一个实例，从而为更一般的相互作用问题提供框架。

Method: 采用超对称量子力学(SUSY QM)方法，选取三角型与双曲型Pöschl-Teller势作为构造要素，对标准JC模型进行推广，得到广义JC哈密顿量，并分析其代数与物理性质。

Result: 广义JC哈密顿量与标准JC哈密顿量共享多项特性：矩阵交织(matrix intertwining)、激发数对称性、Rabi频率、原子布居反转公式，以及经典回复时间与量子回复时间(revival time)公式。

Conclusion: JC哈密顿量可视为更广泛的一类广义哈密顿量的一个具体实例；该类广义哈密顿量有望用于更广泛的相互作用问题研究。

Abstract: The aim of this work is to define and investigate the generalized Jaynes-Cummings (JC) Hamiltonian by means of supersymmetric (SUSY) quantum mechanics methods. We have considered trigonometric and hyperbolic Pöschl-Teller (PT) potentials as two natural ingredients to modify the standard JC model. We show that these generalized JC Hamiltonians have a list of characteristics in common with the JC Hamiltonian, for example: matrix intertwining, excitation number symmetry, Rabi frequency, formulas for atomic inversion and classical and revival times. Therefore, we conclude that JC Hamiltonians can be considered as one example of a wider a class of generalized Hamiltonians which can be useful in broader interaction problems.

</details>


### [122] [Optimal Linear Combination of Hamiltonian Simulation for Dissipative Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39541)
*Hongkang Ni,Lexing Ying*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明 Ni 与 Ying 提出的核族在极小极大意义下最小化了 LCHS 的查询复杂度，最优主项常数为 2e/π，并给出渐近最优代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LCHS 是模拟耗散动力学的有效量子算法，其代价由核函数的选择决定；此前缺乏对核族最优性的严格证明，本文旨在确立查询代价的极小极大最优性。

Method: 将弱对偶性与一个"实现引理"相结合：该引理用耗散演化的矩阵元逼近极值对偶函数，从而构造查询下界。

Result: 对目标精度 ε，LCHS 的最小查询代价渐近为 (2e/π)(ln(1/ε) − ln ln(1/ε) + o(1))，最优主项常数为 2e/π。

Conclusion: Ni–Ying 核族在极小极大框架下达到最优，下界证明同时给出该问题的精确渐近刻画。

Abstract: Linear combination of Hamiltonian simulation (LCHS) is an efficient method for simulating dissipative dynamics on quantum computers. The choice of the LCHS kernel controls the cost of the algorithm. We prove that the kernel family proposed by Ni and Ying~\cite{NY26} minimizes the LCHS query cost in the minimax sense. For target accuracy $ε$, the minimum cost is asymptotically $\frac{2\mathrm{e}}π(\ln\frac1ε-\ln\ln\frac1ε+o(1))$, with optimal leading constant $\frac{2\mathrm{e}}π$. Our lower-bound proof combines weak duality with a realization lemma that approximates extremal dual functions by matrix elements of dissipative evolutions.

</details>


### [123] [Adiabatic training of VQAs for entanglement detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39577)
*E. Acosta,C. Cano,D. Manzano,G. Botella*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种受绝热量子计算启发的混合量子框架，通过参数空间离散化与分层分割将VQA训练转化为QUBO模型，用于纠缠检测问题，并与经典神经网络和常规VQA训练方法进行初步比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠检测是量子信息科学中的基础性问题，既有经典方法也有量子方法；作者希望借助绝热量子计算/量子退火的能力改进变分量子算法（VQA）的训练过程，探索其在该问题上的有效性与适用性。

Method: 构建混合量子框架：采用绝热启发的优化策略训练VQA，将参数空间离散化并结合分层分割方案，把优化问题表述为二次无约束二值优化（QUBO）模型，以利用量子退火求解；应用于纠缠检测任务。

Result: 给出初步实验结果，并与经典神经网络及传统VQA训练技术进行比较（摘要未报告具体定量结论）。

Conclusion: 该工作属于探索性初步研究，旨在激发学界对所提方法有效性与适用性的进一步研究与讨论，而非给出确定性结论。

Abstract: This paper introduces a hybrid quantum framework for training Variational Quantum Algorithms (VQAs) aimed at addressing the fundamental problem of entanglement detection using adiabatic quantum computing. This problem is of foundational significance in quantum information science and has been approached through both classical and quantum methodologies. We propose an adiabatic-inspired optimization strategy for training VQAs, which combines a discretization of the parameter space with a hierarchical segmentation scheme. The optimization problem is formulated as a Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) model to leverage the capabilities of quantum annealing. Preliminary results are compared against classical neural networks and conventional VQA training techniques. The findings aim to stimulate further investigation and discussion within the scientific community regarding the efficacy and applicability of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [124] [Probabilistic imaginary-time evolution via compiled diagonal representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39600)
*Hengyue Li,Huazhe Lou,Yang Li,Mingyu Huang,Yusheng Yang,Jianyong Sheng,Jian Sun,Shenggang Ying*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于编译近似对角表示的哈密顿量的概率虚时演化方案，离线优化基变换，在线电路结构与门数不随虚时变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于Pauli项逐项处理的概率虚时演化在线成本高、且与演化时间相关，需降低在线开销并实现表示复用。

Method: 离线编译一个近似的对角化基变换表示；固定该表示后，在线采用概率虚时演化电路，其电路结构与单次尝试门数与虚时无关；推导成功概率增强的条件。

Result: 相同精度阈值下，数值实验显示在线成本相比逐项Pauli方法降低；并分析近似误差与离线制备成本对整体收益的影响。

Conclusion: 编译式对角表示可复用、在线成本低，在给定精度下优于逐项Pauli方法，但收益受近似误差与离线开销制约。

Abstract: We introduce a probabilistic imaginary-time evolution scheme based on a compiled approximate diagonal representation of the Hamiltonian. A basis transformation is optimized offline. For a fixed compiled representation, the online circuit structure and gate count per attempt are independent of the imaginary time, and the same representation can be reused across different input states and evolution times. We derive a condition for enhanced success probability. Numerical tests show reduced online cost relative to termwise Pauli-based methods at a common accuracy threshold. We also examine how approximation errors and offline preparation costs affect the overall benefit.

</details>


### [125] [Detection and Inhibition of Decoherence Through Collisions: A dynamical approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39657)
*Sagnik Chakraborty,Srijon Ghosh,Federico Amato,Gniewomir Sarbicki,Dariusz Chruściński,Rosario Lo Franco*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出两种基于碰撞模型的噪声无关量子态保护协议：一种保护单量子比特态，另一种保护逻辑量子比特，二者均无需完整先验噪声知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统内在脆弱、易受环境噪声影响，量子态保持是实现实用量子技术的基本挑战；现有保护方案通常依赖对噪声动力学的完整先验知识，适用性受限。

Method: 基于碰撞模型（collisional model）构造两种与噪声模型无关（noise-agnostic）的保护协议；第一种针对量子比特态，第二种针对逻辑量子比特，均在不完全知晓噪声动力学的条件下运行。

Result: 协议可对任意单量子比特态与纠缠态在一般噪声过程下提供鲁棒保护，并支持任意数目量子比特的态保护，从而提升量子架构的可靠性、鲁棒性与可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该框架为实用化量子信息处理提供了一种有前景的量子态保护途径。

Abstract: Quantum systems are intrinsically fragile and highly vulnerable to environmental noise, making the preservation of quantum states one of the fundamental challenges in realizing practical quantum technologies. We propose two noise-agnostic quantum state protection protocols based on a collisional model. The first protocol preserves quantum states of qubits without requiring complete prior knowledge of the underlying noise dynamics, and the second even protects a logical qubit under similar circumstances. These protocols provide robust protection for arbitrary single-qubit and entangled states against general noise processes. Furthermore, their capability to safeguard quantum states with an arbitrary number of qubits enhances the reliability, robustness, and scalability of emerging quantum architectures. These results establish the proposed framework as a promising approach for practical quantum information processing.

</details>


### [126] [Error-Corrected Quantum Optimization via Rydberg Atom Array Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39670)
*Andrew Byun,Berend Klaver,Arinjoy De,Pedro Ildefonso,Davit Khachatryan,Alexei Bylinskii,Michael Schuler,Wolfgang Lechner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: Rydberg原子嵌入中的辅助原子（gadget）可作为内禀纠错资源：Parity Architecture嵌入自然构成一类严格非线性的经典纠错码，配以Cluster解码器可实现高达~26%的错误阈值，并在QuEra Aquila上对最多107原子系统恢复精确优化解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rydberg原子量子优化器天然编码几何图上的最大独立集问题，但求解一般组合优化问题需借助gadget嵌入来突破有限的相互作用距离，而这类嵌入不可避免地引入辅助原子，通常被视为额外开销。作者希望将这一"开销"转化为纠错资源。

Method: 证明基于Parity Architecture的Rydberg嵌入自然形成经典纠错码，并推广到其他基于Rydberg阻塞的嵌入；在superatom子结构中，精确的Rydberg激发数约束在无额外硬件代价下提供局部错误综合征，构成严格非线性码；针对比特翻转错误设计Cluster解码器，利用局部约束与gadget结构进行纠错。

Result: 数值上，该方法在不同布局几何下实现错误阈值 p_th ≳ 26%；实验上，在QuEra Aquila QPU上对最多107个原子的系统，Cluster解码器以≳8%的平均成功率恢复精确优化解，即使在裸硬件与标准贪婪后处理均无法找到解的情况下依然有效。

Conclusion: Rydberg原子阵列嵌入不仅是优化问题的硬件映射，而是一种协同设计的量子优化架构——嵌入、计算与纠错源于同一物理结构。

Abstract: Rydberg atom quantum optimizers naturally encode the maximum independent set problem on geometric graphs. However, to solve general combinatorial optimization problems on more complex topologies, an embedding is required to overcome the limited-range native interactions. Such embeddings use gadgets, specific arrangements of atoms, which inevitably introduce ancillary atoms. Here, we show that this apparent overhead instead provides an intrinsic resource for error correction. Specifically, we demonstrate that the Parity Architecture-based Rydberg embedding naturally forms a classical error-correcting code, a principle that extends to other Rydberg blockade-based embeddings. Within superatom substructures, exact Rydberg-excitation-number constraints provide local error syndromes at no additional hardware cost and give rise to a strictly nonlinear code. To correct bit-flip errors, we introduce the precisely tailored Cluster decoder, which exploits these local constraints and the underlying gadget structure. Numerically, this approach achieves an error threshold of $p_{\text{th}}\gtrsim26 \%$ across different layout geometries. Experimentally, on the QuEra Aquila QPU, the Cluster decoder recovers exact optimization solutions with an average success rate of $\gtrsim8\%$ for systems of up to 107 atoms, even when bare hardware and standard greedy post-processing find none. A Rydberg atom array embedding is therefore not merely a hardware mapping of an optimization problem, but a co-designed architecture for quantum optimization in which embedding, computation, and error correction emerge from the same physical structure.

</details>


### [127] [Optimized Dynamical Decoupling for Spin Ensembles with Large Inhomogeneities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39693)
*Rajan Paul,Genko Genov,Oliver Schmid,Shinobu Onoda,Yutaka Kobayashi,Junichi Isoya,Fedor Jelezko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统比较多种动力学去耦合（DD）方案在环境条件下负电荷NV色心系综中的相干保护与量子传感性能，发现结合快速绝热通过（RAP）脉冲与相位变化的最优序列，相比标准矩形脉冲XY8，可实现信号对比度提升及存储时间一个数量级的增加，并用于弱振荡场灵敏探测；该方案对具有幅度/频率非均匀性的自旋系综具普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋系综在高效量子传感与量子技术中前景广阔，但保持其相干性困难。常用DD序列依赖快速脉冲抑制环境相互作用，然而在系综中因幅度与频率非均匀性及控制误差而性能下降，亟需对比筛选更鲁棒的DD方案。

Method: 在常温下负电荷NV色心系综上，对比大量DD协议在相干保护与量子传感中的表现；识别出以对幅度与频率非均匀性鲁棒的快速绝热通过（RAP）脉冲并结合其相位变化的最优方案；将其与使用标准矩形脉冲的常用XY8序列及用于弱振荡场传感的场景进行比较。

Result: 最优序列相对矩形脉冲XY8展现出更高的信号对比度与约一个数量级的存储时间提升；该方案亦成功应用于弱振荡场的改进传感。

Conclusion: 结合RAP脉冲与相位变化的DD方案能有效克服系综中幅度/频率非均匀性与控制误差，显著提升相干保护与传感性能；虽以金刚石NV色心演示，该优化协议可推广至其他自旋系综及具高幅度/频率非均匀性的系统。

Abstract: Spin ensembles show increasing promise for highly efficient quantum sensing and quantum technologies. However, protecting their coherent properties remains a significant challenge. Dynamical decoupling (DD) typically employs a sequence of fast pulses that aim to reduce the effect of unwanted system-environment interactions. However, the performance of the DD sequences typically suffers in spin ensembles, e.g., due to amplitude and frequency inhomogeneities and control errors. In this work, we compare the performance of an extensive number of DD protocols for coherence protection and quantum sensing in an ensemble of negatively charged NV centers under ambient conditions. We identify the optimal approach that uses rapid adiabatic passage pulses, which are robust to amplitude and frequency inhomogeneity, in combination with the variation of their phases. We experimentally demonstrate an improved signal contrast and an order-of-magnitude increase in memory time with the optimal sequence compared to the widely used XY8 with standard rectangular pulses. We also apply our approach for improved sensing of a weak oscillating field. While our particular demonstration is with NV centers in diamond, the optimized protocol is generally applicable to other spin ensembles and to systems with high amplitude and/or frequency inhomogeneities.

</details>


### [128] [Quantum Query Complexity of Tournament Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39702)
*Pragya Arora,Udit Kumar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了锦标赛解（Copeland集、uncovered集、top cycle等）的量子查询复杂度，证明top cycle可达Õ(N)、Condorcet winner达Õ(√N)、强king为Θ(N^{3/2})，并给出极大出度顶点Θ(N^{3/2})的独立新证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典情形下这些锦标赛解均需Θ(N²)次查询，画面"统一"而缺乏区分；作者希望在量子查询模型下揭示不同锦标赛解之间的复杂度差异，并刻画其量子加速的极限。

Method: 将锦标赛问题归约为计数问题，用Ambainis对手方法（adversary method）证下界；对top cycle采用构造性算法：先找Hamiltonian路径，再用分治定位构成top cycle的前缀。

Result: top cycle为Õ(N)；Condorcet winner为Õ(√N)；强king与最大出度顶点均为Θ(N^{3/2})；并给出最大出度顶点Θ(N^{3/2})下界的自包含替代证明。

Conclusion: 量子查询模型下各锦标赛解复杂度显著分化（由Õ(√N)到Θ(N^{3/2})），打破了经典Θ(N²)的一致性；对手方法下界与Hamiltonian路径+分治的上界构成本质紧的结果。

Abstract: A tournament solution records a set of winners from a tournament, that is, from the outcomes of all pairwise comparisons among $N$ alternatives. We study the quantum query complexity of computing tournament solutions when the outcome of each comparison has to be learned by a query. Classically, the picture is uniform: the Copeland set, the uncovered set and the top cycle all require $Θ(N^2)$ queries, and so does finding a single vertex of maximum out-degree. We show that the quantum query complexity of the top cycle can be computed with $\tildeΘ(N)$ queries, and a Condorcet winner can be found with $\tilde Θ(\sqrt N)$ queries. Finding a strong king requires $Θ(N^{3/2})$ queries, the same as finding a vertex of maximum out-degree. We also give a self-contained alternative proof of the known $Θ(N^{3/2})$ bound for finding a vertex of maximum degree, established by Gilani, Mande, Patro, and Raja. Our lower bounds reduce counting problems to tournament problems using Ambainis's adversary method. For the top cycle, we find a Hamiltonian path and locate the prefix forming the top cycle by divide and conquer.

</details>


### [129] [Non-Asymptotic Bounds via the Nussbaum-Szkoła Mapping for Quantum Hypothesis Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39784)
*Jorge Lizarribar-Carrillo,Gonzalo Vazquez-Vilar,Tobias Koch*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 基于Nussbaum-Szkoła映射提出非对称量子假设检验的新上下界，统一恢复大偏差、中偏差、小偏差等渐近区的可达性与逆定理结果，并在短块长下精确逼近最优量子误差权衡函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子假设检验界或依赖技术复杂的信息谱论证，或具有固定预因子、在非渐近情形适用性受限，缺乏统一且紧致的框架。

Method: 利用Nussbaum-Szkoła映射将量子态二元检验问题转化为经典概率分布问题，由此构造统一的上下界，并允许在部分情形直接借用经典结果。

Result: 所得界在短块长下能准确近似最优量子误差权衡函数；与基于保真度和信息谱方法的现有界相比，数值上更紧致。

Conclusion: 该框架统一且紧致，适用从小偏差到中偏差、大偏差的多种渐近区，为非渐近量子假设检验提供了更实用的分析工具。

Abstract: Quantum hypothesis testing concerns the discrimination between quantum states. This paper introduces novel lower and upper bounds for asymmetric quantum hypothesis testing based on the Nussbaum-Szkoła mapping. Together, these bounds provide a unified recovery of both converse and achievability results across all major asymptotic regimes, including large-, moderate-, and small-deviations. Unlike existing bounds, which either rely on technically involved information-spectrum arguments or suffer from fixed prefactors and limited applicability in the non-asymptotic regime, the proposed bounds arise from a unified framework and enable, in some cases, the direct use of classical results. It is further demonstrated that these bounds provide accurate approximations to the optimal quantum error trade-off function at small blocklengths. Numerical comparisons with existing bounds, including those based on fidelity and information spectrum methods, highlight their improved tightness.

</details>


### [130] [Digital-Analog Quantum Neural Network with Long-Range Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39847)
*Zongyuan Ge,Daili Li,Zewen Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文以数字-模拟量子神经网络（DA-QNN）为代表性框架，证明引入幂律长程Ising相互作用可提升混合数字-模拟算法的效率：在监督图像分类与多体相检测任务中，长程拟设以更少层数或更短演化时间达到短程拟设的性能；在无监督相检测中两者互补重建完整相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单比特数字操作与模拟量子演化相结合的协议可规避大量两比特纠缠门，是变分算法与混合量子模拟的有前景方案；但此类混合算法的效率与拟设能力如何受相互作用形式（尤其相互作用程）影响尚缺乏系统研究，本文旨在考察长程相互作用能否作为降低资源开销的有效资源。
