Method: 以DA-QNN为代表框架，在模拟区块中采用带幂律相互作用的Ising模型产生纠缠；系统改变横向场强度与演化时间，并以相互作用程为核心变量，在监督（图像分类）与无监督（多体相检测）任务上对协议进行基准测试，分析相互作用程对拟设可表达性与可训练性的影响。

Result: 监督图像分类与多体相检测中，长程拟设在更少DA-QNN层数或更短演化时间下即可匹配短程对应方案，原因是非局域耦合带来系统内信息快速传播；该资源缩减减轻了经典优化开销并简化实验实现。无监督相检测中，长程拟设并非在所有区域都优于短程拟设，但两者给出互补特征，联合可重建完整相图。

Conclusion: 长程相互作用平台可作为推进实用化、大规模数字-模拟量子计算的重要资源；通过相互作用程调控可表达性与可训练性，是实现资源高效混合量子算法的可行途径。

Abstract: Protocols combining single-qubit digital operations with analog quantum evolutions bypass large numbers of two-qubit entangling gates, offering a promising approach to variational algorithms and hybrid quantum simulation. Focusing on the digital-analog quantum neural network (DA-QNN) as a representative framework, this work demonstrates that incorporating long-range interactions enhances the efficiency of such hybrid algorithms. Within the analog blocks, an Ising model with power-law interactions is employed to generate entanglement, and this protocol is benchmarked across supervised and unsupervised learning tasks. Beyond discussing the effects of the transverse field and evolution time, the primary analysis focuses on how the interaction range governs the expressibility and trainability of the ansatz. For supervised image classification and many-body phase detection, long-range ansatzes match the performance of their short-range counterparts with fewer DA-QNN layers or shorter evolution times, owing to the rapid information propagation across the system enabled by nonlocal couplings. This resource reduction alleviates the classical optimization overhead and eases experimental implementation. In unsupervised phase detection, although the long-range ansatz does not outperform its short-range counterpart in every regime, the two ansatzes provide complementary signatures that jointly reconstruct the complete phase diagram. These findings establish long-range interacting platforms as a compelling resource for advancing practical, large-scale digital-analog quantum computing.

</details>


### [131] [Distributed Inner Product Estimation of Continuous-Variable Quantum States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39912)
*Kun Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对能量受限的连续变量量子态提出三种CV-DIPE协议，用于比较异地制备的未知量子态的内积，并给出截断误差界与最坏情况样本复杂度保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CV-DIPE用于比较远距离玻色与量子光学系统制备的未知态，且需在缺乏高效经典描述或联合测量的条件下实现基准测试，因此需要可在分布式、仅局部测量数据下认证的内积估计方法。

Method: 面向自然的能量受限连续变量量子态类（含纠缠与非高斯态、任意多模输入），构建三种协议：协同位移宇称测量、协同Haar测量、独立零差阴影（homodyne shadows）。对精确内积建立截断误差界与最坏情况样本复杂度保证。

Result: 三类协议的界的主导项表明协同Haar测量最优，但其全局实现实验难度较大；三者在仅用局部测量数据的情况下均可给出可认证的内积估计，并包含截断误差与样本复杂度保证。

Conclusion: 三种CV-DIPE协议为分布式连续变量系统提供可认证的内积估计，兼顾理论保证与实验可行性，在性能最优性与实现难度之间存在权衡。

Abstract: Continuous-variable distributed inner product estimation (CV-DIPE) compares unknown states prepared by distant bosonic and quantum optical systems. It supports benchmarking without efficient classical descriptions or joint measurements. We develop three CV-DIPE protocols for the natural class of energy-constrained continuous-variable quantum states, based on coordinated displaced parity, coordinated Haar measurements, and independent homodyne shadows. For arbitrary multimode inputs in this class, including entangled and non-Gaussian states, we establish truncation error bounds and worst-case sample complexity guarantees for the exact inner product. The leading terms of these bounds favor coordinated Haar measurements, whose global implementation is experimentally challenging. The three protocols provide certified inner product estimates for distributed continuous-variable systems from local measurement data.

</details>


### [132] [Bath-state (In)dependence of Non-Markovianity Measures in Quadratic Open Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39970)
*Mirko Consiglio,Ilia Khomchenko,Tony J. G. Apollaro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究费米型二次开放量子系统，证明BLP与LPP非马尔可夫性度量与浴态无关，而基于纠缠、可分性及熵的RHP、MSV度量则对浴的温度与化学势敏感；模型数值结果验证了上述结论，且BLP/LPP仅在束缚态阈值处严格为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当环境与系统时间尺度接近时，马尔可夫近似失效，需用多种非马尔可夫性度量刻画系统行为；这些度量预期随环境参数变化，但其对浴态（温度、化学势）的依赖关系尚不明确，本文旨在厘清不同度量的浴态依赖性。

Method: 考虑一个费米型二次开放量子系统，将浴建模为自由费米子链，并针对与其耦合的单个费米格点推导出精确的时间局域主方程；在此基础上解析地考察五种非马尔可夫性度量（BLP、LPP、RHP、MSV）对浴态参数（温度与化学势）的依赖，并进行数值验证。

Result: BLP与LPP度量对浴态无关（bath-state independence）；基于纠缠与可分性的RHP度量以及熵型MSV度量则通过温度与化学势对储库态保持敏感。数值结果与解析结论一致，并显示BLP和LPP仅在束缚态阈值处恒为零，而此时对浴敏感的度量仍保持有限值。

Conclusion: 不同非马尔可夫性度量对浴态依赖性的差异表明，度量的选择会影响对环境参数的物理判读；BLP/LPP适合给出浴无关的非马尔可夫性刻画，而RHP与MSV在束缚态阈值附近可提供额外的、对浴态敏感的信息。

Abstract: Open quantum system dynamics fail to fulfill the conditions of the Markov approximation when the environment and system timescales are close to each other. Such behavior of these systems can be characterized by several non-Markovianity measures, which are expected to vary with the environment parameters. In this work, we consider a fermionic quadratic open quantum system, establishing bath-state independence of the Breuer--Laine--Piilo and the Lorenzo--Plastina--Paternostro non-Markovianity measures. However, our analytical results reveal that the entanglement-based and divisibility-based Rivas--Huelga--Plenio measures, as well as the entropic Megier--Smirne--Vacchini measure, remain sensitive to the reservoir state, through both its temperature and its chemical potential. Modeling the reservoir as a free-fermionic chain, we derive an exact time-local master equation for a single fermionic site in contact with it. Our numerical results for this model confirm these findings and show that the BLP and LPP measures vanish identically only on the bound-state threshold, where the bath-sensitive measures nevertheless remain finite.

</details>


### [133] [Identifying Logical Operations from Syndrome Histories for qLDPC Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40052)
*Yizhuo Tan,Shifan Xu,Yongshan Ding,Jakub Szefer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究仅凭综合症历史（syndrome histories）识别容错量子计算中所执行的逻辑操作，提出精确推断方法与可扩展近似方法，并在[[18,4,2]]与[[72,12,6]]双变量自行车码上验证，单条逻辑指令识别准确率超94%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 综合症历史通常用于错误检测与纠正，但其中隐含了计算过程的信息。作者希望揭示综合症数据可泄露所执行逻辑操作的信息，从而将综合症的作用从错误解码扩展到逻辑操作与电路的识别，并提示这种访问可能造成计算信息的非预期泄露。

Method: 在考虑未知电路故障与理想测量随机性的前提下，建立精确推断方法及可扩展近似推断方法；在[[18,4,2]]和[[72,12,6]]双变量自行车（BB）码上，针对自同构、逻辑Pauli测量、T注入及其组合电路，在多种噪声模型与错误率下评估识别性能，并分析由测量信息不足或不可区分、故障时序、近似推断导致的识别歧义。

Result: 在电路级噪声、错误率介于10^-5至10^-3时，单条逻辑指令识别准确率超过94%；基于自同构的逻辑电路识别准确率超过99%，组合三类指令的逻辑电路达78.32%。

Conclusion: 综合症历史可用于识别容错量子计算中执行的逻辑操作与电路，拓展了综合症在错误解码之外的用途；同时表明对综合症历史及相关经典测量结果的访问可能无意间泄露所执行计算的信息。

Abstract: Syndrome histories are normally used to detect and correct errors in fault-tolerant quantum computation, but they can also reveal information about the logical operations executed during computation. We study logical-operation identification using syndrome histories alone or together with other classical measurement outcomes. We develop an exact inference method and a scalable approximation that account for unknown circuit faults and ideal measurement randomness. We evaluate automorphisms, logical Pauli measurements, T injections, and logical circuits combining these instructions on \([[18,4,2]]\) and \([[72,12,6]]\) bivariate bicycle (BB) codes under multiple noise models and error rates. A single logical instruction can be identified with over 94\% accuracy under circuit-level noise with error rates between \(10^ {-5} \) and \(10^{-3}\). We also achieve over 99\% accuracy for automorphism-based logical circuits and 78.32\% for logical circuits combining all three instructions. We further analyze identification ambiguities arising from insufficient or indistinguishable measurement information, fault timing, and approximation inference. Together, these results extend the role of syndromes beyond error decoding to identifying executed logical operations and circuits during fault-tolerant quantum computation. They also suggest that access to syndrome histories and related classical measurement outcomes may unintentionally reveal information about the executed computation.

</details>


### [134] [Distributed Inner-Product Estimation for Continuous-Variable Quantum States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40141)
*Jun Tang,Congcong Zheng,Xutao Yu,Zaichen Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出仅用共享相位设置下的局域零差测量与经典通信的多模连续变量分布式内积估计（CV-DIPE）协议，在每模平均能量有界条件下给出有限样本保证与充分拷贝数，并刻画截断偏差与统计误差的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式内积估计是量子信息的基础任务，用于估计分布在不同平台上的两个未知量子态之间的内积；多模CV系统的无限维Hilbert空间带来额外困难，而现有方法难以在此情形下给出可操作的测量方案与有限样本保证。

Method: 设计CV-DIPE协议：各端仅在共享相位设置下做局域零差测量并辅以经典通信；在每模平均能量上界已知的假设下，推导有限样本精度与置信度的显式保证及所需拷贝数；利用能量约束得到截断偏差的平方反比界，并借助高斯结构将尾部控制强化为指数衰减、改进精度标度；以高斯与非高斯态数值仿真验证。

Result: 得到显式的有限样本误差保证与充分拷贝数；能量约束给出平方反比型截断偏差上界，高斯结构将尾界强化为指数衰减并改善精度标度；仿真展示了截断偏差与统计误差之间的平衡及其对模数与能量的依赖。

Conclusion: 仅局域零差测量加经典通信即可实现多模CV分布式内积估计，使相互独立的CV平台能在双方态均无易处理经典描述时互相基准化其态制备。

Abstract: Distributed inner product estimation (DIPE) is a fundamental task in quantum information, aiming to estimate the inner product between two unknown quantum states prepared on distributed quantum platforms. For multimode continuous-variable (CV) systems, the infinite-dimensional Hilbert space poses an additional challenge. In this work, we develop a CV-DIPE protocol using only local homodyne measurements at shared phase settings and classical communication. Under a known bound on the mean energy per mode, we derive explicit finite-sample guarantees and sufficient copy counts for estimating the full inner product to prescribed accuracy and confidence. The energy constraint yields an inverse-square truncation-bias bound, while Gaussian structure strengthens the tail control to exponential decay and improves the resulting accuracy scaling. Numerical simulations with Gaussian and non-Gaussian states illustrate the balance between truncation bias and statistical error and its dependence on mode number and energy. Our results enable independent CV platforms to benchmark their state preparations against one another, even when neither state admits a tractable classical description.

</details>


### [135] [Reversible photorefractive attack in continuous-variable quantum key distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40155)
*Junxuan Liu,Yuchao Liu,Qingquan Peng,Piao Tan,Tao Wang,Peng Huang,Anqi Huang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文首次在系统层面实验证明，局部本振CV-QKD系统中铌酸锂IQ调制器的可逆光折变响应可被用于攻击调制标定，导致信道透过率与额外噪声估计失真，从而使实际密钥率非正时仍被误报为正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QKD虽具信息论安全性，但器件缺陷带来实现层面漏洞；针对CV-QKD发射端漏洞的安全后果缺乏实验证据，尤其缺少对可逆性效应被利用的系统级验证。

Method: 在local-local-oscillator CV-QKD系统中开展可逆光折变攻击实验：用适度蓝光照射铌酸锂IQ调制器，观测调制方差变化与恢复过程，阐明物理机制，并构造利用照射—恢复过程破坏调制标定的攻击，通过接收端测量与密钥率计算评估后果。

Result: 蓝光照射使调制方差降至稳定值而不过度抬升估计额外噪声，撤除照射后方差恢复；由此产生的标定失配使接收端高估信道透过率、低估额外噪声，按实测参数计算得到正密钥率，而正确标定下密钥率为非正；恢复过程还使先前操纵更难通过事后检查识别。

Conclusion: 可逆光折变响应可被实际利用以破坏CV-QKD调制标定，构成实用安全威胁；需在安全分析与防护策略中同时考虑光扰动及其后续恢复过程。

Abstract: Quantum key distribution (QKD) offers information-theoretic security, but device imperfections introduce implementation vulnerabilities. Experimental evidence of the security consequences of transmitter vulnerabilities in continuous-variable QKD (CV-QKD) remains limited. Here, we experimentally investigate a reversible photorefractive attack in a local-local-oscillator CV-QKD system. Moderate blue-light illumination reduces the modulation variance of a lithium-niobate IQ modulator to a stable value without appreciably increasing estimated excess noise. The variance recovers after illumination is removed. We elucidate the underlying physical mechanisms and develop an attack that exploits recovery to compromise modulation calibration. Receiver measurements confirm that the resulting calibration mismatch leads to overestimated channel transmittance and underestimated excess noise. Key rate calculations based on measured parameters yield a positive estimated rate when the correctly calibrated rate is nonpositive. Recovery can also make the preceding manipulation harder to identify through later inspection. This work provides the first system-level experimental evidence that a reversible photorefractive response can be exploited to compromise modulation calibration in CV-QKD. These findings highlight reversible changes in quantum transmitters as a practical security concern, motivating security analyses and protection strategies that account for both optical perturbations and subsequent recovery.

</details>


### [136] [Inevitability of the Petz transpose map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40158)
*Jacopo Surace,Chenxu Li,Francesco Buscemi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：对有限维全矩阵代数间的量子信道，若一个对所有先验与信道定义的“回溯”赋值满足对合性、序列与并行复合相容性且与酉逆一致，则它必然就是 Petz 转置映射；去掉对合性则得到双旋转 Petz 映射族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典统计推断中 Bayes 法则由先验与随机模型唯一决定回溯条件概率，而量子理论中是否存在同样一般的回溯法则、且是否唯一，长期是非平凡问题；Petz 转置映射在量子理论多个看似无关的领域反复出现，缺乏统一的公理化解释。

Method: 以有限维全矩阵代数间的信道为对象，输入输出空间限制在参考态的支撑上；对定义于所有先验与信道的回溯赋值施加四条一致性要求：(1) 对合性，(2) 与序列复合相容，(3) 与并行复合相容，(4) 与酉逆一致；由此进行唯一性证明，并分析去掉对合性后的映射族。

Result: 满足上述四条要求的回溯赋值唯一等于 Petz 转置映射；概率混合下的仿射性以及对参考态的特征性平方根依赖并非额外公设，而是由这些要求推出；若放弃对合性，其余要求刻画出一族双旋转 Petz 映射。

Conclusion: Petz 转置映射是唯一与这些一致性要求相容的量子 Bayes 法则类比物，从而统一解释了它在量子理论不同领域的普遍出现，并给出其公理化刻画。

Abstract: Bayes' rule is central to classical statistical inference: given a prior and a stochastic model, it determines the corresponding retrodictive conditional probabilities. In quantum theory, however, the question of whether an equally general rule of retrodiction exists and, if so, whether it is uniquely determined remains nontrivial. Here we prove that a retrodiction assignment defined for all priors and channels, which takes each predicted output state back to its prior, must coincide with the Petz transpose map if it is involutive, respects sequential and parallel composition, and agrees with unitary inversion. The theorem holds for channels between finite-dimensional full matrix algebras, with the input and output spaces restricted to the supports of the corresponding reference states. In particular, affinity under probabilistic mixtures and the characteristic square-root dependence on the reference states follow from these requirements rather than being postulated. Without involutivity, the remaining requirements characterize a family of doubly rotated Petz maps. Our result thus explains why the Petz transpose map arises in seemingly different areas of quantum theory: it is the unique quantum analogue of Bayes' rule compatible with these consistency requirements.

</details>


### [137] [Detuning-assisted cavity enhancement in spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40216)
*Subhasish Guha,Sourav Das,Francesco Albarelli,Aiman Khan,Elnaz Darsheshdar,Adam D. Wright,Animesh Datta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将腔频从跃迁共振处失谐，可把偶极矩估计精度再提高最多两个数量级；该增益源于极化激元间失谐可控的信息再分配，且可用常规探测手段实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腔增强光谱是常用实验技术，通常认为腔频与目标跃迁共振最优；作者质疑这一直觉，考察失谐是否能进一步提升偶极矩估计精度。

Method: 在经典连续波与脉冲单光子两种探针情形下，改变腔频与跃迁的失谐量，并在含损耗条件下评估偶极矩估计精度；通过光-物质类极化激元的信息分布分析增益来源。

Result: 失谐带来的精度提升可比共振情形最多高两个数量级；该增强在经典CW与脉冲单光子探针、以及存在损耗时均成立；损耗决定最优的极化激元分支；增益可用常规光电探测与时间相关单光子计数观测。

Conclusion: 腔失谐是提升偶极矩估计精度的有效自由度，超越“共振最优”的传统认知，且在现有探测技术下即可实验验证。

Abstract: Cavity-enhanced spectroscopy is a widely used experimental technique. We show that detuning the cavity frequency from the transition of interest can enhance the precision for dipole moment estimation by up to two orders of magnitude beyond the improvement when the cavity is resonant with the transition. We show this for both classical continuous-wave (CW) and pulsed single-photon probes, even in the presence of losses. We trace this enhancement to a detuning-controlled redistribution of information between the light- and matter-like polaritons, with losses determining the optimal branch. We show that this enhancement remains accessible using conventional photodetection and time-correlated single-photon counting.

</details>


### [138] [Bounds for General $(r,δ)$-Quantum Locally Recoverable Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40273)
*Shuowei Ma,Fei Shi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究一般(r,δ)-量子局部可恢复码（无纯度/稳定子假设、恢复块可任意重叠），建立类似Singleton的上界并进行余项修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有qLRC界多依赖纯码或稳定子结构、恢复块不重叠或分块假设，缺乏对任意量子子空间、任意重叠恢复块的一般性单例型界。

Method: 利用多重擦除恢复的选择引理推导一般量子子空间的Singleton型上界；通过整数优化得到显式余项修正（δ=2时退化为Golowich–Guruswami单擦除界）；对纯码用局部解耦与熵论证推广Galindo等人的参数界，并去除不相交恢复块情形的分块假设、优化预算获得进一步余项修正。

Result: 得到收紧最多δ-2的一般界与推广后的纯码界；分析三类达到界的码族：重叠恢复块且d=δ的纯码（纯码界严格改进且同时取等）、不相交恢复块达到一般界的非纯码、以及重叠恢复块且d>δ同时达到上述所有界的已有纯码族。

Conclusion: 在无纯度与稳定子假设下给出了qLRC的统一Singleton型界及余项修正，推广并收紧了已有结果，并通过三族码的取等分析说明界的紧性。

Abstract: We study general $(r,δ)$-quantum locally recoverable codes (qLRCs) without assuming purity or a stabilizer structure, and with arbitrarily overlapping recovery blocks. Using a selection lemma for multiple-erasure recovery, we establish a Singleton-like upper bound for general quantum subspaces. When $δ=2$, this bound reduces to the general single-erasure bound of Golowich and Guruswami. An integer optimization yields an explicit remainder correction that tightens the bound by up to $δ-2$. For pure codes, local decoupling and entropy arguments extend the parameter bound of Galindo et al., originally obtained for pure stabilizer codes constructed from Euclidean or Hermitian dual-containing classical codes, to arbitrary pure quantum subspaces. We also remove the partition assumption from several existing pure-code bounds for disjoint recovery blocks and obtain a further remainder correction by optimizing the budget. Finally, we analyze three families attaining these bounds: pure codes with overlapping recovery blocks and $d=δ$, which exhibit both a strict improvement of the pure-code bound and simultaneous equality in the pure-code and general bounds; impure codes with disjoint recovery blocks attaining the general bound; and an existing family of pure codes with overlapping recovery blocks and $d>δ$, which simultaneously attains all these bounds.

</details>


### [139] [QDMin: Coherent Quantum Minimum Finding for Nuclear Deformation Landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40320)
*Abhishek,P. Arumugam*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: QDMin 是一种量子搜索框架：用相干集体坐标寄存器参数化哈密顿量族，配合按需基态能量阈值预言机，直接实现量子最小搜索，无需逐点构建核集体势能面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核集体势能面能量极小点的定位需大量相互作用多体计算；逐点扫描构建能量景观代价高，希望以查询式阈值预言机替代逐点评估。

Method: 以集体坐标寄存器相干编码参数化哈密顿量族，构建按需基态能量阈值预言机提供最小搜索所需的判定谓词；在示意性 pairing-plus-multipole 模型中，用线路生成的能量估计执行搜索。算例包括四点（单 Grover 轮）与 4×4 四极–八极场双坐标完整线路。

Result: 四点计算中一轮 Grover 将真实最小值的概率由 0.25 提升至 0.921；4×4 双坐标计算实现完整线路，但其最小值在零 Grover 迭代轮即被首次采样；最差分支制备保真度为 0.744，低于既定目标。

Conclusion: 相干基态访问（分支制备保真度不足）是当前主要瓶颈；在非结构化模型下 QDMin 具有预期 O(√N_q) 候选查询复杂度，总体优势取决于多体预言机开销，尚待确立。

Abstract: Locating the minimum of a nuclear collective landscape can require many interacting many-body calculations. QDMin (Quantum Deformation Minimum Finding) searches a parameterized Hamiltonian family with a coherent collective-coordinate register and an on-demand ground-energy threshold oracle. The oracle supplies the predicate required for quantum minimum-finding without first constructing the energy landscape point by point. We implement it for a schematic pairing-plus-multipole model using circuit-generated energy estimates during the search. In a four-point calculation, one Grover round raises the probability of the true minimum from 0.25 to 0.921. A $4\times4$ quadrupole--octupole-field calculation extends the complete circuit to two coordinates, although its minimum is first sampled in a round with zero Grover iterations. Its worst-case branch-preparation fidelity of 0.744 falls below the declared target, identifying coherent ground-state access as the current bottleneck. QDMin inherits expected $O(\sqrt{N_q})$ candidate-query scaling in the unstructured model; total advantage depends on the many-body oracle cost and remains unestablished.

</details>


### [140] [Subsystem Spacetime Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40357)
*Esther Xiaozhen Fu,Aranya Chakraborty,Daniel Gottesman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将时空码推广为子系统时空码，用以刻画Clifford线路的故障距离：其规范群可由瞬时稳定子群的掩蔽性质显式构造与分类，恰为对线路无害的故障集合；据此可定位动态码距离损失之处，并分析线路由小组件复合时故障距离的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错码为分析与设计容错Clifford线路提供了有力框架，但缺乏一种能精确刻画Clifford线路（包括动态码）故障距离的工具，也难以判断线路在何处损失距离以及复合线路的距离如何变化。

Method: 构造子系统时空码（subsystem spacetime code）来表征Clifford线路的距离；利用瞬时稳定子群（instantaneous stabilizer groups）的掩蔽（masking）性质显式构造并分类其规范群（gauge group），并证明该规范群恰对应于对线路无害的故障；利用该框架定位动态码的距离损失，并分析由较小部件复合而成的线路其故障距离的演化。

Result: 给出了子系统时空码规范群的显式构造与分类，证明其精确对应无害故障；识别出动态码距离损失的位置，并刻画了线路复合时故障距离的变化规律。

Conclusion: 子系统时空码为分析与设计容错线路提供了通用工具，并有望用于构造新的动态码。

Abstract: Quantum error-correcting codes provide a powerful framework for analyzing and designing fault-tolerant Clifford circuits. In this work, we construct the subsystem spacetime code and use it to characterize the distances of Clifford circuits. We show that its gauge group can be explicitly constructed and classified using the masking properties of the instantaneous stabilizer groups and consists precisely of faults that are harmless to the circuit. We use this framework to identify where a dynamical code loses distance and to analyze how the fault distance changes when circuits are composed from smaller components. Our results provide a versatile tool for analyzing and designing fault-tolerant circuits, with potential applications to the construction of new dynamical codes.

</details>


### [141] [Quantum communication: a discrimination approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40358)
*Hao-Chung Cheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从纠缠恢复与状态区分的操作性对偶出发，构造了以Petz恢复映射为解码器的纠缠生成编码，所有信道使用次数下均成立、以Petz-Rényi相干信息表达的错误界，且对乘积源达到低于相干信息速率的指数衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究无辅助量子通信（unassisted quantum communication）在有噪声量子信道中的可靠传输问题，希望利用纠缠恢复与状态区分之间的对偶关系，获得对任意有限信道使用次数都严格成立、且以相干信息度量的可计算错误界。

Method: 采用状态区分（state discrimination）方法，基于纠缠恢复与状态区分之间的操作性对偶性，构造纠缠生成编码；解码器显式地由Petz恢复映射（Petz recovery maps）给出。

Result: 所得错误界对每一次信道使用次数（每个有限块长）均成立，并以Petz-Rényi相干信息（Petz-Rényi coherent information）表述；对乘积源，错误随块长指数衰减，其速率低于相干信息。

Conclusion: 该框架为无辅助量子通信提供了有限块长的严格性能保证，并将纠缠恢复与状态区分统一起来；Petz-Rényi相干信息可作为刻画指数衰减速率的有效量。

Abstract: We develop a state discrimination approach to unassisted quantum communication through a noisy quantum channel. Starting from the operational duality between entanglement recovery and state discrimination, we construct entanglement-generation codes with explicit decoders constructed from Petz recovery maps. The resulting error bounds hold for every number of channel uses and are expressed in terms of Petz-Rényi coherent information. For product sources, they yield exponential error decay at rates below coherent information.

</details>


### [142] [Rapid mixing for Gibbs samplers for quantum spin systems with all-to-all interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40361)
*Nicola Bertoni,Giacomo De Palma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究任意相互作用图（含全连接）上两体局域量子哈密顿量的吉布斯态制备，利用满足KMS细致平衡的Lindblad动力学，基于量子Dobrushin条件和一阶量子Wasserstein距离推导高温区域的收缩估计，得到关于系统规模与误差容限均对数、且对所有初态一致成立的混合时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子多体系统何时快速趋于热平衡是量子统计力学核心问题。空间局域性为有几何结构的系统提供有力工具，但全连接模型需处理任意自旋对之间的相互作用，现有方法难以适用。作者旨在给出不依赖空间几何或图增长的统一混合时间估计。

Method: 针对任意相互作用图上的两体局域量子哈密顿量，采用满足KMS细致平衡的Lindblad动力学；引入经典Dobrushin条件的量子类比以控制局域扰动对系统其余部分的影响；借助一阶量子Wasserstein距离刻画扰动在动力学下的传播，并在由每格点最大总相互作用强度控制的高温区域内推导收缩估计。

Result: 在无需空间几何或图增长假设的前提下，获得收敛收缩估计，导出混合时间随系统规模与逆误差容限均呈对数增长，且对所有初始态一致成立。

Conclusion: 量子Dobrushin条件结合量子Wasserstein距离可有效分析任意图（含全连接）量子多体系统的热化速率，表明高温下吉布斯态制备可快速完成，为无几何结构约束的混合时间分析提供通用框架。

Abstract: Understanding when quantum many-body systems rapidly converge to thermal equilibrium is a central problem in quantum statistical mechanics. While spatial locality provides powerful tools for systems with an underlying geometry, fully connected models require estimates that accommodate interactions between every pair of spins. We study Gibbs-state preparation for two-local quantum Hamiltonians on arbitrary interaction graphs, including fully connected systems, via Lindblad dynamics that satisfies Kubo--Martin--Schwinger (KMS) detailed balance. Our approach is based on a quantum analogue of the classical Dobrushin condition, which controls the influence of local perturbations on the rest of the system. Using the quantum Wasserstein distance of order $1$, we quantify how these perturbations propagate under the dynamics and derive contraction estimates in a high-temperature regime controlled by the maximum total interaction strength per site. These estimates require no assumptions on spatial geometry or graph growth and yield mixing times logarithmic in the system size and the inverse error tolerance, uniformly over all initial states.

</details>


### [143] [Quantum Computing through Computational Imaging: Representations, Measurement, and Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40393)
*George Kourousias,Sergio Carrato,Roberto Pugliese,Panagiotis Dimitrakis,Alberto Carini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出四个区分（输入幅值编码 vs. 完整候选标注编码、复场干涉与未知相位推断、联合幅值矩阵化与Schmidt秩、目标函数→代价算符+采样电路），阐明量子成像中“代数对应≠计算方法”，并以四像素分割与六变量边界决策实验说明各表示所能支持的估计/决策及其资源开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成像模型与量子态之间的代数对应并不规定计算方法；输入访问方式、测量与解码方式决定了能返回什么。作者旨在为计算成像与工程读者澄清这类对应关系的计算与资源含义，避免把“幅值存储”误当作“整阵列读出”。

Method: 概念性阐释与可复现数值示例：区分输入取值的幅值编码与完整候选标注的编码；用复场干涉说明互补测量可消除局部Fisher信息无法检测的相位模糊；将联合幅值重塑为矩阵，把奇异值分解与纯态纠缠相联系，并区分Schmidt秩与高效经典可模拟条件；在四像素分割中将目标转化为代价算符与采样电路，用单层QAOA与均匀采样对比；另用存档的IBM与IQM测量演示六变量局部边界决策；并给出低秩态重构与若干量子算法的端到端资源核算。全文仅需复线性代数与概率论基础。

Result: 四像素分割中，单层QAOA将平均能量由均匀采样的1.675降至0.686，但最优标注概率仅0.365，低于某个非最优标注的0.484——证明能量下降不等于最优解被最可能采样。六变量局部边界决策由存档硬件测量佐证。配套Seeing Amplitudes浏览器实验室支持交互探索。

Conclusion: 量子-成像的代数对应必须配以明确的输入访问、测量与解码协议才具有计算意义；每种表示应与其允许的估计或决策及所需资源一并评估，低秩重构与所选量子算法可纳入统一的端到端资源账目。

Abstract: An algebraic correspondence between an imaging model and a quantum state does not specify a computational method: input access, measurement and decoding determine what can be returned. This exposition develops four distinctions for computational-imaging and engineering readers. It separates amplitude encodings of input values from encodings of complete candidate labelings, explaining why amplitude storage does not provide full-array readout. Complex-field interference motivates unknown-phase inference: complementary measurements resolve ambiguities that local Fisher information alone does not detect. Reshaping joint amplitudes into a matrix connects singular-value decomposition to pure-state entanglement, while distinguishing Schmidt rank from conditions for efficient classical simulation. In a four-pixel segmentation, an objective becomes a cost operator and a sampling circuit. One layer of the quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA) lowers mean energy from $1.675$ to $0.686$ relative to uniform sampling, yet makes a nonoptimal labeling more probable than the optimum 0.484 versus 0.365. Archived IBM and IQM measurements illustrate a separate six-variable local boundary decision. Low-rank state reconstruction and selected quantum algorithms connect these distinctions to an end-to-end resource account. The derivations and reproducible numerical references assume complex linear algebra and probability, without prior quantum mechanics. The accompanying Seeing Amplitudes browser laboratory supports interactive exploration; the article remains self-contained and connects each representation to the estimate or decision it permits and the resources that output requires.

</details>


### [144] [Fault-Tolerant Heisenberg-Limited Quantum Metrology with Bosonic Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40405)
*Daniel G. Li,Lorcán O Conlon,Yu-Xin Wang,Erfan Abbasgholinejad,Twesh Upadhyaya,Alexey V. Gorshkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于玻色探针的容错量子传感协议，在退相干噪声下估计位移，通过对偶校验测量与重置去除噪声，使Fisher信息在约γ_A^{-(d+1)/2}的探测时间内保持NT^2标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠与相干演化可使估计误差达到Heisenberg极限标度，但环境噪声与硬件误差破坏灵敏量子态的相干性。已有容错传感方案多针对量子比特系统，而许多实用传感器（如玻色模式探针）需要玻色探针，因此需构建玻色容错传感协议。

Method: 在平均光子数约束下构造最优误差校正码；用受控位移将探针与重复编码的量子比特寄存器耦合；通过重复的玻色奇偶测量与重置消除探针噪声而不抹除已存储的逻辑相位；分析电路级比特翻转噪声下距离d码的逻辑失效抑制。

Result: 距离d码将逻辑失效抑制到p^{(d+1)/2}量级；在衰减率γ_A、总传感时间T与平均光子数预算N下，对足够小的残余信号，Fisher信息在约γ_A^{-(d+1)/2}量级的探测时间内按NT^2标度增长。

Conclusion: 该玻色容错传感协议显著优于非容错方案——后者仅在总时间O(γ_A^{-1})内实现Heisenberg标度；本方案将Heisenberg极限维持时间延长至γ_A^{-(d+1)/2}量级，提升了噪声环境下的实用传感性能。

Abstract: Quantum sensors have been shown to enable more precise measurements than classical sensors. By using entanglement and coherent evolution, the estimation error can be made to scale better in interrogation time, photon count, and other resources, achieving Heisenberg-limited scaling. However, environmental noise and hardware errors typically destroy the coherence of the sensitive quantum states. This motivates the use of fault-tolerant techniques to create more robust quantum sensing protocols. Such fault-tolerant quantum sensing approaches have been shown to enhance sensitivity for qubit systems, but many practical sensors require bosonic probes. In this paper, we propose a fault-tolerant protocol using bosons to estimate displacements in the presence of dephasing noise. We construct an optimal error-correcting code under a mean photon-number constraint. Controlled displacements couple the probe to a repetition-encoded qubit register, while repeated boson-parity measurements and resets remove probe noise without erasing the stored logical phase. Under circuit-level bit-flip noise, a distance-$d$ code suppresses logical failures to order $p^{(d+1)/2}$. We show that, for a decay rate $γ_A$, a total sensing time $T$ and a mean photon number budget $N$, and for sufficiently small residual signals, the Fisher information scales as $NT^2$ over interrogation times of order $γ_A^{-(d+1)/2}$. This is a significant improvement over the non-fault-tolerant approach that achieves Heisenberg scaling only for a total time $\mathcal O(γ_A^{-1})$.

</details>


### [145] [Structural Bottlenecks in QAOA Mixer Design: Transport and Causal Reach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40424)
*Jasjyot Singh Gulati,Tharrmashastha SAPV*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出浅层QAOA混合器的两个互补结构瓶颈——构型空间输运与有限深度因果可达性，并分别在第一部分（微扰界、非微扰上包络）与第二部分（k-hop局域化构造）中加以刻画与验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合器的选择是QAOA的核心设计决策，但其不同结构局限无法由单一指标刻画；作者希望用两个互补的结构视角为浅层QAOA的混合器设计提供理论依据。

Method: Part I：推导小β与小γ互补区制下的一阶、混合器依赖的目标界，利用图-Laplacian恒等式证明连通正则混合器图上两式精确等价并约化为代价加权Dirichlet能量；再由逐层ℓ1扩散导出非微扰上包络及达成目标近似比所需的扩散必要条件。Part II：在MaxCut中将横向场混合器与问题图对齐的二局部相互作用增强，构造因果半径不超过(k+1)p的k-hop架构，并在k=1时数值研究环形、圆梯与高围长正则二部图。

Result: 连通正则混合器下两个领头阶表达式精确等价并归为同一代价加权Dirichlet能量；ℓ1扩散给出代价分布依赖的扩散必要条件。数值上，X+XX与X+YY在等深度下优于标准X混合器；在可达性匹配的情形中，深度p的X+YY与深度2p的纯X相差约一个近似比百分点；等可达的XX与YY表现不等。

Conclusion: 满足某一结构能力本身并不保证性能（XX与YY的对比即为例证）；两类瓶颈分析为混合器设计提供互补的结构性洞见。

Abstract: The choice of mixer is a central design decision in the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA), yet different structural limitations of a mixer need not be captured by a single metric. We identify two complementary structural bottlenecks for shallow QAOA: configuration-space transport, which limits how strongly probability can concentrate on high-quality solutions, and finite-depth causal reach, which limits which parts of a problem instance can influence a local objective term. In Part I, we derive first-order mixer-dependent objective bounds in complementary small-$β$ and small-$γ$ regimes. For connected regular mixer graphs, the two leading-order expressions are exactly equivalent through a graph-Laplacian identity and reduce to the same cost-weighted Dirichlet energy. We then derive a non-perturbative upper envelope from the layer-wise $\ell_1$ spreading of the mixer unitary, yielding a cost-distribution-dependent necessary spreading condition for attaining a target approximation ratio. In Part II, we study the locality bottleneck for MaxCut by augmenting the transverse-field mixer with problem-graph-aligned two-local interactions. The resulting $k$-hop construction has causal radius at most $(k+1)p$ at depth $p$; for the $k=1$ architectures studied numerically, this gives radius $2p$. Across cycles, circular ladders, and high-girth regular bipartite graphs, $X{+}XX$ and $X{+}YY$ improve the best-found approximation ratios over the standard $X$ mixer at equal depth, while $X{+}YY$ at depth $p$ comes within about one approximation-ratio point of plain $X$ at depth $2p$ in the reach-matched cases examined. The unequal performance of the equal-reach $XX$ and $YY$ augmentations shows that satisfying a structural capability does not by itself guarantee performance. Together, the results provide complementary structural insights for mixer design.

</details>


### [146] [A universal gauge structure in the photon content of light-matter systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40446)
*Arka Dutta,Marcus Kollar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 截断光-物质模型希尔伯特空间后，谱对规范变换不变，但基态光子数依赖规范。本文证明当规范变换表现为场的位移时，基态光子数严格满足关于规范参数λ的二次多项式规律 ⟨a†a⟩_λ = ⟨a†a⟩_0 + Cλ + Qλ²，其中 C=⟨Gp⟩、Q=⟨G²⟩；其最小值本身规范不变。对量子Rabi模型该最小值仅由基态能量决定，且在含能级劈裂的Rabi与Dicke模型中严格为正，意味着任何规范下都存在残余光子；弱耦合下最小光子数规范为Jaynes-Cummings规范，Dicke模型在热力学极限下所有耦合强度均如此。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单模光-物质模型的数值计算通常需截断物质希尔伯特空间，此时谱保持规范不变，而基态光子数却随规范变化，其物理意义长期存在争议。作者希望厘清这种规范依赖的结构，给出其精确规律，并据此定义规范不变的物理量，以解决"截断后光子数是否有意义"的问题。

Method: 将规范变换刻画为耦合到物质算符G的场的位移，利用位移算符的代数性质对基态光子数做关于规范参数λ的展开，得到⟨a†a⟩_λ = ⟨a†a⟩_0 + Cλ + Qλ²，系数为关联函数C=⟨Gp⟩和Q=⟨G²⟩；对标量取λ的最小值构造规范不变量，并在量子Rabi模型与Dicke模型（含能级劈裂）上解析求解该最小值，包括弱耦合与热力学极限下的比较。

Result: 基态光子数对规范参数的依赖严格为二次多项式，系数由G与场动量p的关联函数及⟨G²⟩给出；对λ取最小值后得到规范无关量，在量子Rabi模型中该最小值完全由基态能量确定。该最小值在含能级劈裂的Rabi与Dicke模型中严格为正，因此任何规范下都无法消除残余光子。弱耦合极限下最小光子数对应的规范为Jaynes-Cummings规范；Dicke模型在热力学极限下，该结论对所有耦合强度成立。

Conclusion: 截断空间下基态光子数的规范依赖并非任意，而服从精确的代数规律，其规范不变的最小值可作为有意义的物理量。该最小值严格为正，说明残余光子内容无法通过选择规范消除；最小光子数规范在弱耦合下是Jaynes-Cummings规范，并在Dicke模型热力学极限下普遍适用。这为光-物质模型中光子数可观测量的定义与截断方案的评估提供了明确判据。

Abstract: After truncating the matter Hilbert space of a single-mode light-matter model, gauge transformations leave the spectrum invariant whereas the ground-state photon number is gauge dependent, hence, its meaning remains disputed. We show that this gauge dependence obeys an exact algebraic law whenever the gauge transformation acts as a displacement of the field coupled to a matter operator $G$. Namely, the ground-state photon number is a quadratic polynomial in the gauge parameter $λ$, $\langle a^{\dagger} a\rangle_λ$ $=$ $\langle a^{\dagger} a\rangle_{0}$ $+$ $Cλ$ $+$ $Qλ^2$. Here the coefficients are the correlator $C=\langle G\,p\rangle$ of $G$ and the field momentum $p$, and $Q=\langle G^2\rangle$. Its minimum over all gauges is naturally gauge-invariant, and for the quantum Rabi model it is determined by the ground-state energy alone. We show that it is strictly positive for the quantum Rabi and Dicke models with level splitting, so that a residual photon content survives in every gauge. At weak coupling the gauge of minimal photon number is the Jaynes-Cummings gauge, and for the Dicke model it remains so at all couplings in the thermodynamic limit.

</details>


### [147] [Process matrices on generalised theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40448)
*Matt Wilson,James Hefford,Timothée Hoffreumon*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将过程矩阵框架推广到一大类广义物理理论，用"对经典探针的自然性"重新公理化时空语境，刻画可被过程矩阵表示的理论，并给出超映射与过程矩阵两种高阶过程视角一致的精确条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过程矩阵框架原本用于定义不定因果序，但局限于量子理论；需要弄清在多大的理论范围内该框架依然成立，以及超映射视角与过程矩阵视角何时等价。

Method: 采用范畴式/复合性（compositional）视角，以经典探针上的自然性（naturality）对时空语境做简洁的重公理化，精确捕捉非语境赋值以及与凸性和粗粒化的相容性；据此刻画哪些理论中的抽象时空语境容许过程矩阵表示。

Result: 给出了抽象时空语境可被过程矩阵表示的理论的清晰刻画，并确定超映射与过程矩阵两种高阶过程视角相一致的精确条件；经典理论、量子理论以及类Boxworld理论均满足这些条件。

Conclusion: 过程矩阵对不定因果序的定义可在广义概率理论层面得到公理化推广，其与超映射表述的等价性由可明确给出的条件决定，该条件在经典、量子与类Boxworld理论中均成立。

Abstract: We extend the process-matrix framework for defining indefinite causal orders to a broad class of generalised physical theories. We give a simple re-axiomatisation of spacetime contexts in terms of naturality over classical probes, which precisely captures non-contextual assignment together with compatibility with convexity and coarse-graining within local laboratories. Using this compositional perspective, we give a clean characterisation of theories in which abstract spacetime contexts admit a process-matrix representation, and we pin down the precise conditions (satisfied by classical theory, quantum theory, and a Boxworld-like theory) under which the supermap and process-matrix perspectives on higher-order processes coincide.

</details>


### [148] [Trajectory sharpening under quantum reference frame transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40452)
*Matteo Fadel,Giulia Gubitosi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将负质量参考系下的“轨迹锐化”效应诠释为一次量子参考系（QRF）变换，用带符号质量的自由粒子玩具模型把EPR型可观测量与一对对易的关系型可观测量对应起来，并给出QRF不变的锐度度量与不可同时锐化的“一夫一妻”关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QRF变换把物理系统的描述联系到不同量子观察者，但其与具体实验现象的联系尚不清楚；同时，通过与有效负质量参考系统关联所得的轨迹锐化效应缺乏操作性解释。

Method: 构建带符号质量的自由粒子最小玩具模型；将导致轨迹锐化的EPR型可观测量识别为在瞬时QRF变换下映射到不同子系统局域算符的一对可对易关系型可观测量；进一步用显含时间依赖的动力学QRF变换复现对应关系轨迹；并定义关系轨迹的QRF不变锐度度量，推导单配性（monogamy）关系；最后考察由两个物理系统共同编码的集体参考模式。

Result: 瞬时与动力学QRF变换均能重现关系轨迹（变换后关系位置显含时间）；证明一条轨迹不能同时对两个不同负质量参考系任意锐化（单配性关系）；表明锐度可分布在由两系统编码的集体参考模式中，即使任一子系统单独都不能提供任意锐的参考。

Conclusion: 为连续变量系统的QRF变换提供了可操作、与实验相关的解释：轨迹锐化本质上是到负质量系统的参考系变换，锐度是可分配而非局限于单一组分参考系的资源。

Abstract: Quantum reference frame (QRF) transformations relate the description of a physical system to different quantum observers, but their connection to concrete experimental phenomena remains largely unclear. Here we show that the trajectory-sharpening effect obtained by correlating a probed system with an effective negative-mass reference admits a natural interpretation as a QRF transformation to the frame of a negative-mass system. Using a minimal toy model of free particles with signed masses, we identify the Einstein-Podolsky-Rosen-type observables responsible for this effect with a pair of commuting relational observables that, under the instantaneous QRF transformation, are mapped to operators local to distinct subsystems. We then show that the corresponding relational trajectory is reproduced by the dynamical QRF transformation, where the transformed relational position acquires an explicit time dependence. We introduce QRF-invariant sharpness measures for relational trajectories and derive monogamy relations showing that a trajectory cannot be made simultaneously arbitrarily sharp with respect to two distinct negative-mass frames. We further show that trajectory sharpness can instead reside in a collective reference mode encoded across two physical systems, even when neither constituent alone provides an arbitrarily sharp reference, so that sharpness can be distributed rather than localized in a single constituent frame. These results provide an operational, experimentally relevant interpretation of QRF transformations for continuous-variable systems.

</details>


### [149] [Quantum-Walk Inspired Spectral Metrics for Evaluating QAOA Mixers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40459)
*Jasjyot Singh Gulati,Tharrmashastha SAPV*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出两个与图拓扑相关的谱启发式指标——混合哈密顿量的归一化最小特征值间隔，以及混合幺正的长时平均最小特征值间隔——用于刻画QAOA混合器的谱结构，并与经验性能进行对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QAOA混合器决定了指数级解空间的探索能力，但其选择通常依赖惯例、实现或电路约束，而非基于图拓扑的判据；作者希望建立与拓扑相关的成本无关谱指标来指导混合器选择。

Method: 受量子行走混合理论启发，定义两个在混合器哈密顿量正数缩放下的不变量：归一化最小特征值间隔与长时平均最小特征值间隔；对完全图、超立方体、环和平衡二维环面推导精确表达式与渐近界，并与其上的经验QAOA性能进行比较。

Result: 所研究拓扑类之间存在较宽的谱分离；对随机置换不变成本哈密顿量，经验性能排序与谱刻画大体一致；对结构化的1维和2维Ising QUBO，该排序失效，说明混合器-成本对齐可能变得重要。

Conclusion: 拓扑级、成本无关的谱启发式对QAOA混合器选择既有价值也有局限：它在随机成本下有效，但在结构化成本下不足，需考虑混合器与成本的相互作用。

Abstract: QAOA mixers play an important role in enabling exploration of the exponentially large solution space of binary optimization problems. However, the choice of mixer Hamiltonian is typically guided by convention and implementation or circuit constraints, rather than by a graph-topology-aware criterion. In this work, inspired by quantum-walk mixing theory, we propose two complementary spectral heuristics for characterizing QAOA mixer topologies. The first is the normalized minimum eigenvalue separation of the mixer Hamiltonian $H_M$, while the second is the long-time averaged minimum eigenvalue separation of the mixer unitary $U_M(β)=e^{-iβH_M}$. Both quantities are invariant under any positive rescaling of the mixer Hamiltonian and provide Hamiltonian- and unitary-level characterizations of the mixer's spectral structure. We use them as heuristic indicators of the mixer dynamics relevant to amplitude redistribution during QAOA evolution.
  We derive exact expressions and asymptotic bounds for the complete graph, hypercube, cycle, and balanced 2-D torus, revealing a broad spectral separation between the studied topology classes. We then compare these separations with empirical QAOA performance. For random permutation-invariant cost Hamiltonians, the observed performance ordering is broadly consistent with the spectral characterization. In contrast, for structured 1-D and 2-D Ising QUBOs, this ordering breaks down, indicating that mixer-cost alignment can become important. These results illustrate both the usefulness and the limitations of topology-level, cost-agnostic spectral heuristics for QAOA mixer selection.

</details>


### [150] [Lattice moments and quantum designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40481)
*Jonathan Conrad,Kristian Holm*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明幺模 GKP（Gottesman–Kitaev–Preskill）分布集合不构成 rigged 量子 3-设计，并量化了导致该失败的 replica 缺陷；结论通过自洽地重新推导辛格上的二阶矩公式得到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GKP 态及其分布是连续变量量子信息中构造随机性与设计结构的重要候选，但缺乏对其能否达到高阶设计（尤其是 rigged 3-设计）的严格判定与偏差度量。

Method: 以辛格（symplectic lattice）为对象，自洽地重新推导其二阶矩公式，并据此分析幺模 GKP 分布集合的高阶矩行为；引入 replica 缺陷作为刻画偏离 rigged 量子 3-设计的定量指标。

Result: 幺模 GKP 分布集合不满足 rigged 量子 3-设计的条件；该失败可由显式的 replica 缺陷量化。

Conclusion: 幺模 GKP 分布不能作为 rigged 量子 3-设计的实现，其二阶矩公式与 replica 缺陷共同给出了失败程度的精确刻画。

Abstract: We show that the ensemble of unimodular Gottesman$-$Kitaev$-$Preskill distributions fails to form a rigged quantum $3$-design and quantify the replica defect that determines this failure. This is established by rederiving a second moment formula for symplectic lattices in a self-contained manner.

</details>


### [151] [Fast and accurate neural triage decoding of ultra-high-rate quantum LDPC codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40490)
*Hong-Ye Hu,Zhenlai Wang,Binzhao Luo,Tao Xiang,Chen Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出“神经分诊解码”（neural triage decoding）框架，利用综合症一致性自适应分配神经计算量，使超高速率qLDPC码（LP码、Kasai码）在保持接近最优算法解码器精度的同时，将解码延迟降低约1–2个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速率qLDPC码可降低容错量子计算的物理量子比特开销（编码率>1/2、约千个数据比特），但其实际落地需要兼顾高精度与低延迟的解码器；现有高精度解码器延迟大，而神经网络解码器此前尚未在高码率qLDPC码上得到验证。

Method: 设计三阶段自适应解码流程：①局部神经消息传递网络快速预测纠错；②按需追加迭代以消除残余综合症违规；③极少触发的多样化阶段搜索一致性纠错。方法源于对精确逐比特边缘解码器的严格分析，揭示了与码距无关的综合症不一致误码平台。

Result: Triage解码器可解码编码数百个逻辑量子比特的超高码率qLDPC码，精度与近最优算法解码器相当（state-of-the-art），解码延迟降低约1–2个数量级；并证明神经网络解码器对误差先验失配具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 自适应神经解码是实现近期快速、高精度、可扩展量子纠错的有前景路径。

Abstract: Quantum error correction is essential for fault-tolerant quantum computing. High-rate quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes have emerged as a promising approach to reducing the substantial physical qubit overhead of quantum error correction, enabling encoding rates exceeding $1/2$ in blocks of approximately one thousand physical data qubits. However, their practical implementation requires decoders that combine high accuracy with low latency to keep pace with quantum hardware. Existing decoders of high-rate qLDPC codes can achieve high accuracy but often incur substantial computational latency, whereas neural network decoders have yet to be demonstrated for high-rate qLDPC codes. Here, we introduce \emph{neural triage decoding}, a framework that uses syndrome consistency to adaptively allocate neural computation. A local neural message-passing network rapidly predicts error corrections, additional iterations reduce residual syndrome violations, and a rarely invoked diversification stage searches for consistent corrections to the remaining shots. We demonstrate that the \texttt{Triage} decoder can decode ultra-high-rate qLDPC codes encoding a few hundred logical qubits, including lifted-product and Kasai codes, achieving state-of-the-art accuracy comparable to the near optimal algorithmic decoders while reducing decoding latency by approximately one to two orders of magnitude. Our framework is inspired by a rigorous analysis of the exact bitwise marginal decoder, revealing a distance-independent syndrome-inconsistent error floor. In addition, we show the robustness of neural network decoders to mismatched error priors. Our results establish adaptive neural decoding as a promising route towards fast, accurate, and scalable quantum error correction in the near term.

</details>


### [152] [Using domain walls to benchmark fast quantum anneals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40504)
*Shushmi Chowdhury,Asa Hopkins,Nick Chancellor,Viv Kendon*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究反平行边界条件下受挫一维Ising自旋链基态中畴壁的分布：理想链在量子情形下应呈正弦平方（粒子在盒中）分布、经典情形下应呈均匀分布；含随机缺陷的链则出现源于随机场模型与热平均的U形分布。利用D-Wave新型快速退火可在相干时间内观测到正弦平方分布；在最短退火时间下动力学尚未达到该形状，而呈现中部不同的U形，据此提出以畴壁分布尾部范围作为与平台无关的指标，可同时反映相干时间与噪声水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机：厘清受挫一维Ising自旋链中畴壁分布的物理来源，区分理想量子（正弦平方）、理想经典（均匀）与随机缺陷链（U形）行为，并利用快速退火进入相干时间尺度，检验实际量子硬件是否表现出预期的相干量子行为，同时寻找可跨平台刻画相干时间与噪声水平的诊断量。

Method: 方法：考虑反平行边界自旋的受挫一维Ising自旋链；对比理想链条的量子预期（正弦平方分布）与经典预期（均匀分布）；以带随机场的经典模型解释随机缺陷链的U形分布（随机场平均与热平均共同作用）；使用D-Wave较新的快速退火功能，使退火时间短于相干时间，直接观测相干量子行为；并对最短可用退火时间下的动力学演化未达稳态的情形进行分析。

Result: 结果：在短于相干时间的退火条件下，观测到符合预期的相干量子行为，畴壁分布呈正弦平方形状；对于最短可用退火时间，动力学尚未达到正弦平方形状，而是在链的中部呈现不同的U形分布；该区间内畴壁分布的尾部范围可作为平台无关的度量。

Conclusion: 结论：畴壁分布的形状（正弦平方、均匀或U形）可作为判别量子相干、经典热化与噪声效应的有效指纹；快速退火使硬件进入相干区，验证了量子预期；畴壁分布尾部范围是一种与平台无关的指标，能够同时给出相干时间与噪声水平的特征信号。

Abstract: We consider the distribution of domain walls arising in ground states of frustrated 1-dimensional Ising spin chains with anti-parallel boundary spins. For ideal chains, a sine-squared particle-in-a-box distribution of domain walls is expected in the quantum setting, and a uniform distribution expected classically. However, for chains with random imperfections, prior work [Chancellor et al., npj Quantum Inf. 8, 73, 2022] found that the distribution in a physical implementation corresponded to neither. Instead, the domain wall distribution forms a U-shaped profile arising from a classical model with random fields, where the combination of averaging over these fields and thermal averaging produces the characteristic U-shape. With D-Wave's newer fast anneal feature, we access anneal times shorter than the coherence time and observe the expected coherent quantum behavior in the form of a sine-squared distribution. For the shortest available anneal time, the dynamics have not yet reached the sine-squared shape and instead display a different U-shape in the middle of the chains. This motivates using the extent of the domain wall distribution tail in this regime as a platform independent metric that provides a signature of both coherence time and noise levels.

</details>


### [153] [Grow, Compress, and Perturb: A Hybrid Quantum-Classical Pathway to Scalable Quantum Chemistry on Quantum Computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40561)
*Hugo D. Nogueira,Siwar Badreddine,Olivier Adjoua,Diata Traore,Guillaume Michel,Jean-Philip Piquemal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出无哈密顿量梯度的VULCAN分层VQE框架，通过VDBF动态生成算符、线性规模最小完备池与整体分层追加，消除梯度测量，以5-20%测量成本达化学精度，并在32量子比特C3H6上结合GPU加速随机ENPT2实现化学精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VQE变体多依赖基于梯度的算符选择，需大量哈密顿量梯度测量，限制了其在电子结构计算中的可扩展性。

Method: VULCAN框架：用变分双括号流(VDBF)方程动态生成ansatz算符；构建线性缩放的最小完备池（比QEB池小4-12倍）；整层同时追加以完全消除哈密顿量梯度测量；采用"先增长后压缩"方案并以高效态重叠测量控制线路深度；将哈密顿量投影到主导行列式子空间以降低量子比特需求。对32量子比特C3H6另加GPU加速随机Epstein-Nesbet (ENPT2)微扰修正作为后处理。

Result: 在最高32逻辑量子比特的高性能模拟中，对氢链、BeH2和H2O仅用批处理ADAPT-VQE测量成本的5-20%即达到化学精度；对32量子比特C3H6，批处理ADAPT-VQE无法达标，而单层VULCAN加随机ENPT2修正可达化学精度。

Conclusion: VULCAN通过无梯度分层构建与随机ENPT2后处理，显著降低测量开销并提升可扩展性；ENPT2支持在达到化学精度时提前终止，为大规模量子化学模拟提供了高效路径。

Abstract: While Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE) variants are among leading candidates for electronic structure calculations on quantum computers, gradient-based operator selection limits their scalability. To solve this, we introduce the Hamiltonian-gradient-free VULCAN (Variational Upbuilding, Layered Compression, Adaptive N-body perturbation) layered VQE framework. Our method dynamically generates ansatz operators using Variational Double Bracket Flow (VDBF) equations. Combined with a linearly scaling minimal complete pool, it creates a pool 4-12 times smaller than QEB (Qubit-Excitation-Based) pools. By appending entire layers simultaneously, Hamiltonian gradient measurements are completely eliminated. Circuit depth is managed via a grow-then-compress scheme using efficient state-overlap measurements, and qubit requirements are reduced by projecting the Hamiltonian onto a dominant-determinant subspace. Using high-performance emulation up to 32 logical qubits, we achieve chemical accuracy at just 5-20% of the measurement cost of batched ADAPT-VQE for hydrogen chains, BeH2 and H2O. For the 32-qubit C3H6, where batched ADAPT-VQE does not reach chemical accuracy, a single VULCAN layer combined with a GPU-accelerated stochastic Epstein-Nesbet (ENPT2) correction does. This stochastic ENPT2 post-processing step enables early termination at chemical accuracy.

</details>


### [154] [On the Quantum Chromatic Gap II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40571)
*Lorenzo Ciardo,Antoine Mottet*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证实了第一作者在SODA'26提出的猜想：对Khot的d-to-1博弈存在强形式伪心灵感应——任意d≥4、ε>0、k∈ℕ，都存在经典值<ε但具备完美有限维量子策略（测量上下文半径≥k）的d-to-1标签覆盖博弈；由此得到量子色数χ_q=3而经典色数χ任意大的图，这是自BCT'99以来量子色数间隙的首次改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自Brassard–Cleve–Tapp（1999）以来，图量子色数与经典色数的间隙一直未被改进（其构造中χ在χ_q上指数增长）。作者希望证明更强形式的伪心灵感应，使在经典值任意小的同时量子策略完好，从而在固定χ_q=3下把经典色数推到任意大，改进量子色数间隙。

Method: 构造分多步：(1) 从Mermin–Peres魔方出发，做"拉伸"操作——拉开其方程间距并用额外长"方程导线"连接，增大初始测量上下文半径且保持经典不可满足性；(2) 施加并行重复以压低经典值；(3) 用Dinur–Guruswami–Khot–Regev(2003)的PCP构造将其转为伪心灵感应的多层Kneser超图；(4) 将该超图输入Fei–Minzer–Wang由超图着色到4-to-1博弈的归约，证明该归约的量子完备性并证明其保持上下文半径。关键在于各步骤对"上下文半径"这一参数的追踪与保持。

Result: 对每个d≥4、ε>0、k∈ℕ，构造出经典值<ε却拥有使用有限维共享纠缠、测量上下文半径≥k的完美量子策略的d-to-1标签覆盖博弈；并导出χ_q=3而χ任意大的图族。

Conclusion: 证实了关于Khot d-to-1博弈强伪心灵感应的猜想，首次在BCT'99之后改进量子色数间隙；核心贡献还包括证明Fei–Minzer–Wang超图着色→4-to-1博弈归约的量子完备性及其半径保持性质。

Abstract: We confirm a conjecture of the first author [SODA'26] on a strong form of pseudo-telepathy for Khot's $d$-to-$1$ games: for every $d\geq 4$, every $ε>0$, and every $k\in\mathbb N$, there is a $d$-to-$1$ label-cover game of classical value less than $ε$ that admits a perfect quantum strategy using finite-dimensional shared entanglement with measurement contexts of radius at least $k$. As a consequence, there exist graphs whose quantum chromatic number $χ_q$ is $3$ and whose classical chromatic number $χ$ is arbitrarily large. This is the first improvement on the quantum chromatic gap since the work of Brassard, Cleve, and Tapp [Phys. Rev. Lett.'99], which produced graphs for which $χ$ grows exponentially in $χ_q$.
  To prove the conjecture, we proceed as follows. We start with a ``stretched'' version of the Mermin--Peres magic square, obtained by spacing out its equations and linking them with additional long equation wires. This operation increases the radius of the initial measurement contexts of a perfect quantum strategy for the game, while preserving classical unsatisfiability. We then apply parallel repetition to make the classical value small, and we turn the system into a pseudo-telepathic multi-layered Kneser hypergraph via a PCP construction of Dinur, Guruswami, Khot, and Regev [STOC'03]. Finally, we feed the resulting hypergraph into the reduction of Fei, Minzer, and Wang from hypergraph colouring to $4$-to-$1$ games, we establish quantum completeness of this reduction, and we show that it preserves context radius.

</details>


### [155] [What Can Be Computed with Indefinite Causal Structure?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40573)
*Omri Shmueli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在过程矩阵形式化框架下，若允许量子操作不遵循确定因果序，计算能力可超越 BQP：一般过程矩阵计算可在多项式时间内求解 NP 内所有问题，纯过程矩阵则可多项式时间求解唯一汇点定向问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨"不遵循确定因果序的量子操作"是否能带来超越标准量子计算（BQP）的计算能力，并在过程矩阵形式化（Oreshkov 等，2012）这一严格框架下给出可证明的复杂度结论。

Method: 采用过程矩阵形式化；计算模型中量子线路简洁地描述一个过程矩阵，算法访问其生成的通道，代价以生成线路的规模衡量。作者构造两类多项式时间算法：(1) 一般过程矩阵计算，所用有效过程满足 Araújo 等的 purification postulate，生成通道为酉的（尽管生成线路非酉）；(2) 要求过程矩阵本身为纯的，给出唯一汇点定向（unique-sink orientation）的多项式时间算法，酉生成器显式且高效构造。

Result: 一般过程矩阵计算足以在多项式时间内解决 NP 中的每个问题；纯过程矩阵情形下可在多项式时间内求解唯一汇点定向这一涵盖若干长期优化问题的通用问题，且构造出的酉生成器显式高效。

Conclusion: 若唯一汇点定向不存在量子多项式时间算法，则表明在过程矩阵形式化中，不定因果序即使在保持酉性的条件下也能提供超越 BQP 的计算能力。

Abstract: Can quantum computation become more powerful when operations do not need to follow a definite causal order? We study this question in the process matrix formalism of Oreshkov et al. (Nature Communications, 2012), which allows quantum operations to be connected without a fixed global order. In the computational model, a quantum circuit succinctly describes a process matrix, and the algorithm is given access to its generated channel, with computational cost measured by the size of the generating circuit.
  We give polynomial-time algorithms in two settings. First, general process matrix computation suffices to solve every problem in $\textbf{NP}$. The valid processes used by our algorithm satisfy the purification postulate of Araújo et al. (Quantum, 2017), and their generated channels are unitary, although their generating circuits are not. Second, requiring the process matrices themselves to be pure, we give a polynomial-time algorithm for unique-sink orientation, a general problem capturing several longstanding computational problems in optimization. Our unitary generators are constructed explicitly and efficiently. Assuming that unique-sink orientation has no quantum polynomial-time algorithm, our result shows that indefinite causal order can provide computational power beyond $\textbf{BQP}$ within the process matrix formalism, even while preserving unitarity.

</details>


### [156] [Classical communication over quantum channels: does entanglement help in nature?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40585)
*Peixue Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一般的Schur加权块熵不等式，证明所有相位协变信道（含多能级振幅阻尼、广义退相位、阻尼-退相位信道）不存在纠缠辅助的经典通信优势，并顺带恢复"所有qubit信道无纠缠优势"的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠码字能否提升独立使用量子信道时的经典通信率是一个长期问题；旧猜想认为无优势，Hastings等虽反证但所需维度极大，暗示大量物理信道或所有低维信道可能无纠缠优势。受广义振幅阻尼信道进展启发，作者希望给出更广泛、更构造性的无优势判定。

Method: 证明一个一般的"Schur加权块熵不等式"，并将其应用于相位协变信道类：多能级振幅阻尼信道、广义退相位信道、阻尼-退相位信道，从而确立这些信道的经典容量满足可加性（无纠缠辅助增益）。

Result: 所有相位协变信道均无纠缠优势；作为副产品，重新得到"所有qubit信道无纠缠优势"这一已有结论。

Conclusion: 相位协变这一对称性结构是排除纠缠辅助经典通信优势的关键；Schur加权块熵不等式可作为统一工具，将无优势结果从qubit扩展到多能级与阻尼类物理信道。

Abstract: Can entangling codewords across independent uses of a quantum channel improve the rate of classical communication? An old conjecture states that there is no such entanglement advantage for all channels. Although Hastings's result and follow-up works disproved this conjecture, it turns out that enormous dimension is required to construct a channel where entanglement enhances classical communication. This indicates that there might be no entanglement advantage for many physical channels, or for all low dimensional channels. Motivated by recent progress on (generalized) amplitude damping channels, we propose a general Schur-weighted block entropy inequality, and show that there is no entanglement advantage for all phase-covariant channels including multi-level amplitude damping channels, generalized dephasing channels, and damping-dephasing channels. As a by-product, we also recover the recently established result that there is no entanglement advantage for all qubit channels.

</details>


### [157] [Optimal detuning and gate time for strongly driven single-qubit gates beyond the rotating-wave approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40595)
*Alejandro D. Bendersky,Sergio S. Gomez,Rodolfo H. Romero*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 超越旋转波近似(RWA)，用二阶Magnus-Taylor有效哈密顿量理论分析单量子比特门的失真度，并通过幅度依赖的Bloch-Siegert型失谐与时间重标度修正RWA门参数，使门失真度降低数个量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RWA仅适用于弱耦合，可给出闭式门参数；但加快动力学需要更大驱动幅度，此时被忽略的反旋项不可忽略并引起共振移动（Bloch-Siegert shift），导致按RWA设计的门不准确，故需研究超越RWA的门失真度并恢复精确门条件。

Method: 理论上以驱动频率倒数的二阶展开构建有效Magnus-Taylor哈密顿量；对驱动频率施加幅度依赖的Bloch-Siegert型失谐并对操作时间做重标度，从而以修正参数恢复RWA门条件；再用薛定谔方程数值解验证。

Result: 数值求解薛定谔方程表明，这些修正可将门失真度降低数个量级，并扩展可用驱动幅度范围。

Conclusion: 二阶Magnus-Taylor修正（Bloch-Siegert失谐加时间重标度）能有效补偿反旋项影响，在更大驱动幅度下保持高保真单量子比特门，为高速量子门设计提供可行方案。

Abstract: Single-qubit quantum gates are implemented as the time-controlled unitary evolution of a two-level system under harmonic driving. The rotating-wave approximation (RWA) provides closed-form gate parameters for weak coupling. Larger amplitudes, desirable to speed up the dynamics, make the RWA-designed gates inaccurate because of the neglected counter-rotating term, which also shifts the resonance (Bloch-Siegert shift). Here we study theoretically the infidelity of gates beyond the RWA, using the effective Magnus-Taylor Hamiltonian through second order in the inverse of the driving frequency. By detuning the driving frequency by an amplitude-dependent shift of Bloch-Siegert type and rescaling the operation time, the RWA gate conditions are recovered with corrected parameters. Numerical solutions of the Schrodinger equation show that these corrections lower the gate infidelity by several orders of magnitude, extending the range of usable amplitudes

</details>


### [158] [Optimal Diamond Distance Estimation for Unitary Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40623)
*Jinge Bao,Qisheng Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出一种量子算法，用 $O(\sqrt{d}/\varepsilon)$ 次对未知 $d$ 维酉信道及其逆信道的查询，将两酉信道间钻石距离估计到加性误差 $\varepsilon$，且查询复杂度达到最优（匹配 Jeon–Oh 的下界）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两量子信道间的钻石距离刻画了单次使用下区分它们的最大成功概率，是信道区分的核心度量。然而对未知酉信道的钻石距离进行估计的查询复杂度此前缺乏最优算法，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出量子算法：以对两个未知 $d$ 维酉信道 $U$、$V$ 及其逆信道 $U^\dagger$、$V^\dagger$ 的黑盒查询为模型，通过相干控制/信道干涉类技术估计钻石距离，查询次数为 $O(\sqrt{d}/\varepsilon)$。

Result: 算法以加性误差 $\varepsilon$ 估计钻石距离，查询复杂度 $O(\sqrt{d}/\varepsilon)$，与 Jeon 与 Oh（npj Quantum Inf. 2026）所给下界吻合，故在查询复杂度意义下最优。

Conclusion: 未知酉信道的钻石距离估计具有 $\Theta(\sqrt{d}/\varepsilon)$ 的紧查询复杂度；该结果确立了两酉信道区分问题在查询模型下的最优样本效率。

Abstract: The diamond distance between two quantum channels determines the optimal probability of distinguishing them from a single use. We give a quantum algorithm that estimates the diamond distance between two unknown $d$-dimensional unitary channels to within additive error $\varepsilon$, using $O(\sqrt{d}/\varepsilon)$ queries to the unitary channels and their inverses. The query complexity is optimal, as it matches the lower bound due to Jeon and Oh (npj Quantum Inf. 2026).

</details>


### [159] [Left-Handed Transmission Lines for the Dynamical Casimir Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40624)
*Valentin E. Salari,Fernando C. Lombardo,Paula I. Villar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究左手超导传输线（LH）中经磁通调制SQUID实现的动力学卡西米尔效应，并与右手线（RH）及共面波导对比，揭示LH谱与模式依赖耦合如何决定被占据模式及其增长率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LH线具有非零下带边与高频稀疏模式，其谱结构与常规RH线截然不同，而动力学卡西米尔效应中模式选择与耦合强度受谱结构支配，尚缺乏系统比较研究。

Method: 构建由串联电容与并联电感组成的LH超导传输线，终端接磁通调制SQUID；推导量子哈密顿量，证明两模式耦合可因式分解为各模式在SQUID处的振幅；用同一组元件构建RH线，并与同样SQUID终端的共面波导对照，分析泵浦频率、模式占据与增长率。

Result: 等元件条件下LH耦合随频率增长，在由单元电感与电容决定的交叉泵浦频率之上超过RH耦合；仅LH线在RH带边以上存在共振。同时泵浦两个模式时，弱耦合模式可借用强耦合模式的耦合；共振线交叉可占据三或四个模式而不提高增长率。相较共面波导，LH耦合相近但能将产生的粒子保持在目标模式，而近等距波导会将其分散到多对模式并需更高泵浦能量。

Conclusion: LH谱结构与耦合的模式依赖共同决定动力学卡西米尔效应的模式选择与速率，LH线在频率选择性、带外共振与目标模式保真度上优于RH线与共面波导，是有望用于定向粒子产生与模式工程的可控平台。

Abstract: Left-handed (LH) superconducting transmission lines, built with series capacitors and shunt inductors instead of the series inductors and shunt capacitors of a conventional right-handed (RH) line, have a spectrum with a nonzero lower band edge and modes that become sparse at high frequencies. We study the dynamical Casimir effect in a LH line terminated by a flux-modulated SQUID and compare it with an RH line built from the same elements and with a long coplanar waveguide terminated by the same SQUID. We derive the quantum Hamiltonian and show that the coupling between two modes factorizes into their amplitudes at the SQUID. Together, the LH spectrum and the mode dependence of this coupling determine which modes are populated and how fast. With equal elements, the LH coupling grows with frequency and exceeds the RH one above a crossover pump frequency set by the cell inductance and capacitance, and only the LH line has resonances above the RH band edge. Pumping two modes at once lets a weakly coupled mode borrow coupling from a strongly coupled one, while crossings of resonance lines populate three or four modes without increasing the growth rate. Compared with the coplanar waveguide, the LH line has a similar coupling but keeps the created particles in the target mode, whereas the nearly equidistant waveguide spreads them over many pairs and needs more pump energy.

</details>


### [160] [Comparison of entangling strategies for unitary synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40642)
*Jonas Wunder,Hendrik Weimer,Ricardo Costa de Almeida*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文以酉算子合成作为与具体应用无关的基准，构建了一套通用工具集，用于评估由原生Mølmer–Sørensen多比特门构成的门的性能，并识别多比特门真正具有优势所需的硬件条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 囚禁离子平台具备长相干时间、近乎全连接以及潜在多比特门等优势，但增加工程复杂度与在量子算法中采用更灵活门集所带来的实际收益之间存在权衡，这一权衡尚缺乏系统评估。

Method: 开发通用的工具集来评估由原生Mølmer–Sørensen多比特门组成的门集性能；采用酉算子合成（unitary synthesis）作为应用无关的基准目标，从而刻画多比特门优势的具体硬件要求。

Result: 识别出使多比特门具备优势所需的具体硬件条件（如多比特门保真度、可扩展性等门槛要求）；给出工程复杂度与门集灵活性之间的定量权衡关系。

Conclusion: 多比特门带来的优势依赖于具体硬件条件；所提供的工具集可为囚禁离子量子计算中门集设计与工程投入的取舍提供评估依据。

Abstract: Trapped-ion quantum computers are promising as a platform for realizing quantum computational advantage. They promise long coherence times, near all-to-all connectivity, and potential multiqubit gates as distinguishing advantages compared to competing platforms. Regarding these advantages, it is crucial to understand the trade-offs between increasing engineering complexity and actual improvements in running quantum algorithms with a more flexible gate set. In this work, we investigate this question and develop a versatile toolset to evaluate the performance of gate sets composed of native Mølmer--Sørensen multiqubit gates. To this end, we employ unitary synthesis as an application-agnostic benchmark target and identify specific hardware requirements for multiqubit gates to be advantageous.

</details>


### [161] [Reconstructing hidden observables via quantum snapshot thermometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40652)
*Jie Ren,Andrew Hallam,Luheng Zhao,Huanqian Loh,Zlatko Papić*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出"量子快照测温法"：利用局部平衡与独立标定的平衡态物态方程，从固定基下的投影快照中反演出原本不可见的守恒密度分布，从而获得能量前锋、扩散常数等定量流体力学信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投影快照是量子模拟器的标准读出工具，但只能直接探测在测量基下对角的可观测量的信息，横向或非对角的守恒密度信息被隐藏，传统上需切换多个测量基或做量子态层析，成本高。

Method: 基于局部平衡假设和独立标定的平衡态物态方程，将固定基快照测得的对角可观测量映射为守恒密度；数值上验证该方法可恢复能量前锋与扩散常数、区分自旋输运与能量传播，并识别由对称性导致的读出歧义、用空间结构先验加以消解。

Result: 数值实验表明该方法能恢复能量前锋、提取扩散常数、区分自旋输运与能量扩散，并揭示初始测量中不可见的守恒荷分布；同时明确了对称性带来的读出模糊性及用先验空间结构消解的方式。

Conclusion: 固定基快照测温法无需额外测量基或量子态层析，即可给出超越输运指数的定量流体力学信息，为量子模拟器的读出提供低成本、可扩展的方案。

Abstract: Projective snapshots are a standard readout tool for quantum simulators, but they directly probe only observables that are diagonal in the measurement basis. We show how local equilibration and an independently calibrated equilibrium equation of state can reveal otherwise hidden conserved-density profiles. We demonstrate numerically that such ``quantum snapshot thermometry'' can recover energy fronts and a diffusion constant, distinguish spin transport from energy spreading, and reveal conserved-charge profiles invisible in the initial measurements. We also identify symmetry-imposed ambiguities in the readout and show how prior knowledge of the spatial structure can resolve them. Fixed-basis snapshot thermometry thus provides quantitative hydrodynamic information beyond transport exponents, bypassing additional measurement bases and quantum state tomography.

</details>

### [162] [Large connectivity expansion of Ising Spin Glasses on random graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38724)
*Luca Maria Del Bono,Flavio Nicoletti,Federico Ricci-Tersenghi*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该论文提出稀疏随机图上的Ising自旋玻璃的高连通性解析展开，通过围绕全连接平均场极限展开腔方程，得到平均场有限连通系统de Almeida-Thouless线的封闭解析方程，避免数值迭代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏随机图上Ising自旋玻璃的精确腔解只能通过数值迭代获得，缺乏解析描述；作者希望获得计算优势并理解有限连通相对全连接平均场的偏差来源。

Method: 以全连接平均场极限为中心展开腔方程，识别三类修正来源：(i) 腔场非高斯性，(ii) 腔场与其涨落间的统计相关性，(iii) 腔滤波函数的修正；并采用非均匀展开（三者均在平均场解附近展开）与均匀展开（仅展开统计量、保留腔滤波函数）两种方案，并推广至T=0极限。

Result: 非均匀展开可直接与Sherrington-Kirkpatrick模型比较，但低温失效，因de Almeida-Thouless线在零温极限下发散；均匀展开能较好精确描述系统临界行为，并可扩展至T=0。

Conclusion: 该解析展开在T–H平面给出有限连通平均场系统AT线的封闭方程，具显著计算优势；均匀展开是描述临界行为的更优方案，并适用于零温极限。

Abstract: We compute a high-connectivity analytical expansion for Ising spin glasses on sparse random graphs. We expand the cavity equations around the fully-connected mean-field limit, focusing on the critical behavior of the system: our approach allows us to compute the de Almeida-Thouless line of mean-field finitely-connected systems in the temperature $T$--magnetic field $H$ plane, by means of closed analytical equations. The exact cavity solution of such systems can only be obtained by solving numerically iterative equations, so our approach brings a notable computational advantage. On the theoretical side, we are able to identify three sources of corrections to the fully-connected mean-field solution: i) the non-Gaussianity of the cavity fields, ii) the statistical correlation between the cavity fields and their fluctuations, and iii) the corrections to the cavity filter functions. These corrections are treated using two different expansions: a non-uniform expansion, where we expand all three contributions around the mean-field solution, and a uniform expansion, where we only expand the statistics but leave the cavity filters untouched. The former allows for a direct comparison with the Sherrington-Kirkpatrick model but fails at low temperatures, as a consequence of the de Almeida-Thouless line diverging in the zero-temperature limit. On the other hand, the uniform expansion describes with good accuracy the critical behavior of the system. We also extend these results to the $T = 0$ limit.

</details>


### [163] [Spectral theory of large data with power-law latent features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39013)
*Philipp Fleig*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可含测量噪声的生成式潜特征模型，用于描述从神经群体到蛋白质等多领域中协方差矩阵的幂律特征值谱，并建立其谱理论：给出Stieltjes变换的闭式自洽方程以获取体谱密度、相应的变分原理，以及孤立顶部特征值的位置与涨落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实证数据估计的协方差矩阵普遍呈现幂律特征值谱（如神经群体、蛋白质等），但缺乏统一的可解释生成模型及其严格的谱理论刻画，尤其在有测量噪声时体谱与少数顶部离群谱的行为难以解析预测。

Method: 构建生成式潜特征模型（潜特征加噪观测）；推导协方差矩阵Stieltjes变换的领先阶自洽方程，由此得到体特征值密度；为该自洽方程建立变分原理；并解析计算孤立顶部特征值的位置与涨落。

Result: 得到体谱密度的闭式（领先阶）自洽描述与变分刻画；解析给出孤立顶部特征值的位置及其涨落；理论在已知代数极限下与既有结果一致，并通过数值模拟验证。

Conclusion: 该工作为幂律协方差谱提供了可生成的建模框架与系统的谱理论工具，统一解释了体谱与顶部离群谱，可作为噪声条件下潜结构推断与谱分析的理论基础。

Abstract: Power-law eigenvalue spectra of covariance matrices estimated from data appear in diverse domains ranging from neural populations to proteins. We introduce a generative latent feature model for such data, optionally corrupted by measurement noise, and develop its spectral theory. We derive closed leading-order self-consistent equations for the Stieltjes transform of the covariance matrix from which the bulk eigenvalue density is obtained and provide a variational principle for the self-consistent equations. We also compute the positions and fluctuations of isolated top eigenvalues. The theory is validated in known algebraic limits and tested against simulations.

</details>


### [164] [Tunable periodic cascade of delocalization transitions in non-Hermitian IAAF model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39514)
*Yi Xue,Yunhua Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 在非厄米插值Aubry-André-Fibonacci模型中引入虚相位移动，实现了可调的周期性退局域化转变级联，其周期仅由虚相位移动决定，可单参数连续调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 退局域化转变级联是实现多步退局域化控制的途径，但如何实现可编程的周期性级联仍是挑战。作者希望在准周期体系中构造一种周期可调的级联退局域化转变。

Method: 构建非厄米插值Aubry-André-Fibonacci（IAAF）模型，在准周期势中引入虚相位移动；通过相图上的静态局域化刻画（局域态与扩展态的交替条纹图样）以及静态与动力学诊断进行数值研究；并解析推导级联周期的表达式。

Result: 相图中出现局域态与扩展态交替的规则条纹图样；解析得到级联周期仅依赖虚相位移动；静态与动力学诊断一致确认了该周期性退局域化转变级联；可通过单一控制参数连续调节级联周期。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了一类具有可调级联周期的新型退局域化转变，物理机制为动能促退局域化与增益-损耗调控局域化之间的竞争，为实验上可编程的退局域化控制提供了途径。

Abstract: Cascade of delocalization transitions provides a route to multi-step control of delocalization, yet achieving a programmable periodic cascade remains a challenge. Here, we demonstrate a tunable periodic cascade of delocalization transitions in a non-Hermitian interpolating Aubry-André-Fibonacci (IAAF) model by introducing an imaginary phase shift into the quasiperiodic potential. Such a periodic cascade of delocalization transitions arises from the competition between kinetic-energy-facilitated delocalization and gain-loss-regulated localization. In numerical static localization characterizations, successive regular stripe patterns for localized and extended states emerge in phase diagrams. We also derive an analytical expression for the cascade period and find that it relies solely on the imaginary phase shift. This provides a direct way of continuously tuning the cascade period via a single control parameter. Complementary static and dynamical diagnostics consistently confirm this periodic cascade of delocalization transitions in the non-Hermitian IAAF system. Our findings identify a novel class of delocalization transitions with tunable cascading periods, opening a route toward experimentally programmable control of delocalization.

</details>


### [165] [Microscopic Mechanism and Control of Reentrant Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39961)
*Pei-Jie Chang,Quanfeng Lu,Wentao Yang,Dong Ruan,Gui-Lu Long*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文通过解析傅里叶通道间散射，揭示了准周期二聚体链中同一调制既能诱导局域化又能恢复扩展态的微观机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准周期调制增强时局域态可重新变为扩展态，但同一调制为何能同时驱动局域化与恢复缺乏微观解释，本文旨在解答这一问题。

Method: 在准周期二聚体链中分辨傅里叶通道间的散射过程；引入中性辅助共振确定局域化边界；分析散射进入相邻通道并返回所导致的光谱边移动；并构造精确的子晶格映射。

Result: 光谱边相对于由中性辅助共振确定的局域化边界发生移动，其与边界的相继穿越使具有固定积分态密度（IDS）标签的同一光谱包先后进入局域化和扩展区；该恢复过程在两类开隙族的有限参数范围内均存在，包括相对相位失谐；子晶格映射可实现恢复窗口的补偿调控，并预测单个IDS标签的恢复阈值。

Conclusion: 准周期二聚体链中的局域化—恢复循环源于通道间散射引起的光谱边与局域化边界的两次穿越，且该机制可由子晶格映射精确刻画并预测恢复阈值。

Abstract: Increasing quasiperiodic modulation can restore extended states after localization, raising the question of how the same modulation drives both localization and recovery. We identify a microscopic mechanism in quasiperiodic dimer chains by resolving scattering between Fourier channels. Specifically, scattering into neighboring channels and back shifts the spectral edge relative to a localization boundary determined by a neutral auxiliary resonance. Their successive crossings drive the same spectral packet with fixed integrated-density-of-states (IDS) labels into localization and back into an extended regime. This recovery persists across finite parameter ranges in both gap-opening families, including relative-phase detuning. Furthermore, an exact sublattice mapping enables compensated control of the recovery window and predicts recovery thresholds for individual IDS labels.

</details>


### [166] [Dynamical mean-field theory for associative memory with block-correlated patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40060)
*Andrea Callegaris,Yoshiyuki Kabashima*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 将生成泛函动力学平均场理论（DMFT）从独立模式推广到固定有限尺寸的相关二值模式独立分块，用以刻画初始状态如何在单个记忆检索与对称混合态之间做出选择，并揭示重入式检索区及其边界性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存储相关模式的联想记忆中，单个记忆的检索会与对称混合态竞争，仅靠平衡态分析无法判断初始状态最终选择哪种结果，因此需要动力学描述来刻画吸引域的几何与检索轨迹。

Method: 把面向独立模式的生成泛函DMFT推广到由固定有限尺寸相关二值模式构成的独立分块；所得有效单神经元过程具有自洽的交叉噪声协方差和自反馈系数，二者由分块协方差的本征值决定；并与直接网络仿真对照，分析层次相关模式在非单调传递函数下的情形。

Result: DMFT复现了直接仿真中的检索轨迹与吸引域几何；对层次相关模式，随载荷增大可出现从"不完美初始状态仍能检索"的重入区，之后再增大载荷则检索失效；在该重入区下边界附近，直接网络显示存在分隔共存混合态与目标检索态的驻定鞍点；DMFT轨迹的参数敏感性可预测该边界的邻近移动；在上边界处，检索仍可从目标重叠更大的初始状态进入，表明吸引域可达性发生改变。

Conclusion: 动力学DMFT为相关模式的联想记忆提供了统一的非平衡描述，能够定量预测检索动力学、吸引域结构与重入相界的移动；在不动点收敛假设下，该理论退化为广义自洽信噪比分析（SCSNA）的方程。

Abstract: In associative memories storing correlated patterns, retrieval of individual memories can compete with symmetric mixed states. A dynamical description is needed to determine how the initial state selects between these outcomes. We extend the generating-functional dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT) for independent patterns to independent blocks of correlated binary patterns of fixed finite size. The resulting effective single-neuron process has a self-consistent cross-talk noise covariance and self-feedback coefficients that depend on the block-covariance eigenvalues. For hierarchically correlated patterns with nonmonotonic transfer, increasing the loading can permit retrieval from an imperfect initial state before further loading prevents it. DMFT reproduces the retrieval trajectories and basin geometry found in direct simulations. Near the lower boundary of this re-entrant region, direct networks reveal stationary saddles separating coexisting mixed and target-retrieval states. Parameter sensitivities of DMFT trajectories predict nearby shifts of this boundary. At the tested upper boundary, retrieval remains accessible from initial states with larger target overlap, indicating a change in basin access. Under fixed-point convergence assumptions, the theory recovers the equations of generalized self-consistent signal-to-noise analysis.

</details>


### [167] [Structural signatures of primary and secondary relaxation during physical aging of a metallic glass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40298)
*Beatrice Baraldi,Stefano Marchesin,Davide Marchesi,Lara Piemontese,Francesco Dallari,Søren S. Sørensen,Eloi Pineda,Marco Di Michiel,Giulio Monaco,Peihao Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 利用快扫量热与原位X射线全散射联用装置，在Pd42.5Cu30Ni7.5P20金属玻璃中跨越六个数量级时间、从Tg以上到Tg以下150 K以上的温度范围追踪物理老化，发现第一衍射峰变化（中程有序）主导近/高于Tg的焓弛豫，低温下则出现与第二衍射峰（短程重排、Pd–Pd关联增强）相关的更快次级过程，表明主级与次级老化对应不同结构长度尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理老化使玻璃趋近平衡并改变其热力学与力学性质，但伴随的微观结构演化长期缺乏清晰认识；现有手段难以在宽时间与宽温度窗口内同时获得热力学与结构信息。

Method: 采用新开发的低背景实验装置，将快速扫描量热法（FSC）与原位X射线全散射（total scattering）结合，对Pd42.5Cu30Ni7.5P20金属玻璃进行物理老化研究；测量时间跨度达六个数量级，温度从Tg以上延伸至Tg以下150 K以上；重点分析结构因子第一峰（FSDP）与第二峰附近的结构变化，并与焓弛豫对比。

Result: 结构变化主要集中于结构因子第一、第二峰附近。近Tg及Tg以上，FSDP变化与焓弛豫高度一致，主要归因于中程有序度提高；在更低温度，仅用该贡献无法描述焓演化，需引入一个更快的额外弛豫过程，该过程与第二衍射峰附近变化相关，对应短程结构重排（包括Pd–Pd关联增强）。

Conclusion: 热力学弛豫与结构演化之间存在直接对应关系，主级与次级老化过程分别关联于不同的结构长度尺度（中程 vs. 短程）。

Abstract: Physical aging drives glasses toward equilibrium and changes their thermodynamic and mechanical properties, but the accompanying microscopic structural evolution remains poorly understood. Here, using our recently developed low-background setup combining fast scanning calorimetry with \emph{in-situ} X-ray total scattering, we investigate microscopic structural changes during physical aging in Pd$_{42.5}$Cu$_{30}$Ni$_{7.5}$P$_{20}$ metallic glass. The measurements span an unprecedented six orders of magnitude in time and a temperature range extending from above the glass transition temperature to more than 150\,K below it. We observe dominant structural changes near the first and second peaks of the structure factor. Near and above the glass transition temperature, changes in the first sharp diffraction peak closely follow enthalpy relaxation and are associated predominantly with increasing degrees of medium-range ordering. At lower temperatures, the enthalpy evolution can no longer be described by this contribution alone, revealing an additional, faster relaxation process that correlates with changes near the second diffraction peak and thus short-range structural rearrangements, including increased Pd--Pd correlations. These results establish a direct connection between thermodynamic relaxation and structural evolution, providing strong evidence that primary and secondary aging processes are associated with distinct structural length scales.

</details>

### [168] [Tan's Contact for Trapped Spin-Orbit-Coupled Fermi Gases at Finite Temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38512)
*Tianhao Wu,Yuanbang Wei,Rui Tian,Dongdong Pu,Beizhi Chen,Qihang Ying,Hongyan Shi,Yue Zhang,Shuai Li,Bo Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文用路径积分蒙特卡洛（PIMC）方法研究有限温度下自旋轨道耦合（SOC）费米气体的Tan接触，发现热效应从根本上改变Tan接触与SOC强度的标度关系：零温下的线性标度被显著削弱，并转变为非单调依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有关于自旋轨道耦合费米气体Tan接触的研究几乎全部局限于零温，而多数实验条件下不可忽略的热效应此前尚未被探讨。

Method: 采用路径积分蒙特卡洛（PIMC）数值方法，在有限温度下计算强相互作用自旋轨道耦合费米气体的Tan接触，并分析其随SOC强度的变化。

Result: 零温下Tan接触随SOC强度线性增长的规律被热效应显著削弱；其机理为相互作用范围内相反自旋费米子对的平均占据数与能量重加权占据数之间的竞争，使线性标度变为非单调依赖。

Conclusion: 热效应从根本上改变Tan接触与SOC的标度关系，所揭示的占据数竞争机制可为今后强相互作用自旋轨道耦合费米气体中Tan接触热效应的实验研究提供参考。

Abstract: Tan's contact, bridging microscopic features of many-body quantum systems to two-body contact interactions, is pivotal in the description of quantum gases. However, past studies of Tan's contact of the spin-orbit coupled Fermi gases have been exclusively discussed at zero temperature. The thermal effects on that, which cannot be neglected in most experimental conditions, has not been explored so far. Here, through a path integral Monte Carlo (PIMC) study, we show that thermal effects fundamentally alter the scaling relation between spin-orbit coupling (SOC) and Tan's contact. The linear scaling of Tan's contact with respect to the strength of SOC found at zero temperature is drastically undermined by the thermal effects. And a new mechanism based on the competition between the mean occupancy and the energy-reweighted occupancy of the opposite spin fermion pairs within the interaction range has been unveiled, which radically changes the above linear scaling to a non-monotonic dependence. Our work should be useful for future experiments to explore the preternatural thermal effects on Tan's contact of the strongly interacting spin-orbit-coupled Fermi gas.

</details>


### [169] [Nonreciprocal Floquet tunneling dynamics of spin-orbit-coupled bosonic atoms in a non-Hermitian double-well potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38597)
*Limei Ju,Yingying Zhang,Jilei Liang,Shoukang Chang,Zunlue Zhu,Lu Qin,Wuming Liu,Xingdong Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该文研究增益-损耗双势阱中自旋轨道耦合玻色原子在非互易耦合下的稳定自旋翻转隧穿，通过Floquet准能谱分析给出PT对称相图，并揭示非互易强度、增益-损耗强度与周期驱动的协同调控机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米量子系统中的稳定隧穿动力学是量子输运研究的基本问题，而非互易耦合作为非厄米性的重要表现对器件稳定性和自旋隧穿动力学有显著调控作用，现有研究对此缺乏系统分析。

Method: 将自旋轨道耦合玻色原子置于带增益与损耗的双势阱中，通过对周期驱动下Floquet态与准能谱的解析分析，获得系统的PT对称相图，并系统考察非互易耦合强度、增益-损耗导致的非厄米强度及周期驱动对稳定自旋翻转隧穿的协同调控。

Result: 精确调控非互易性与增益-损耗可显著扩大稳定参数区域；在增益-损耗平衡的非互易系统中，稳定参数区的连续性或离散性由驱动频率比Ω/ω的奇偶性决定，自旋翻转隧穿可通过参数λ与2ε/ω精确控制；在增益-损耗非平衡情形下，进一步给出不同Ω/ω奇偶性下实现稳定自旋翻转隧穿的平衡条件。

Conclusion: 研究为实现鲁棒自旋翻转隧穿提供了新的理论途径，对非厄米超冷原子系统中量子态输运研究具有指导意义，并为新型自旋量子器件的实验设计提供参考。

Abstract: The stable tunneling dynamics in non-Hermitian quantum systems is one of the fundamental issues in the study of quantum transport. Nonreciprocal coupling, as another crucial manifestation of non-Hermiticity, exerts significant regulatory effects on the stability of quantum devices and on spin tunneling dynamics. Here we place spin-orbit-coupled bosonic atoms in a double-well potential with gain and loss, and investigate the effect of nonreciprocal coupling on the system dynamics. Through an analytical analysis of the Floquet states and the quasi-energy spectrum under periodic driving, we obtain the PT-symmetry phase diagram of the system. We further explore the synergistic regulation mechanism of several factors---including the strength of nonreciprocal coupling, the non-Hermitian strength arising from gain-loss, and the periodic driving---on stable spin-flip tunneling. The results show that precise manipulation of nonreciprocity and gain-loss can substantially enlarge the stable parameter region.
