In nonreciprocal systems with balanced gain and loss, the continuity or discreteness of the stable parameter region is determined by the parity of the driving-frequency ratio $Ω/ω$, and the spin-flip tunneling can be precisely controlled through the parameters $λ$ and $2\varepsilon/ω$. For the unbalanced gain-loss scenario, the equilibrium conditions for achieving stable spin-flip tunneling under different parities of $Ω/ω$ are further provided. These findings offer a new theoretical approach for realizing robust spin-flip tunneling, provide guiding significance for the study of quantum-state transport in non-Hermitian ultracold atomic systems, and also serve as a reference for the experimental design of novel spin-based quantum devices.

</details>


### [170] [Transverse momentum transport and collective-mode localization in dipolar tube arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38801)
*Camilla Polvara,Nishchhal Verma,Sarang Gopalakrishnan,Vadim Oganesyan*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究无粒子隧穿、仅由偶极相互作用耦合的一维玻色气体阵列中，纵向动量的横向输运。借助零温Bogoliubov理论刻画振幅与强度两个关联量，揭示弹道传播、t^{-3}退相干衰减、无序局域化以及r^{-7}等时关联衰减，并给出管间交错密度失稳阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对耦合一维玻色气体阵列的研究多依赖粒子隧穿耦合，而仅靠偶极相互作用（无隧穿）实现的横向输运与关联性质缺乏系统刻画；同时亟需厘清密度不平衡这类淬火无序对横向集体模式的影响，以及相互作用程对等时关联的调控作用。

Method: 采用零温Bogoliubov理论，以管间动量关联函数及其模平方（分别称振幅与强度）为观测量，研究偶极耦合的一维玻色气体阵列；将管间密度不平衡视为淬火无序，分别分析均匀阵列与无序阵列中横向模式的传播、退相干与局域化，并比较偶极耦合与最近邻耦合下的等时关联，最后做稳定性分析。

Result: 均匀阵列中Bogoliubov准粒子对在管间弹道传播，退相干导致长波振幅按t^{-3}衰减；密度不平衡作为淬火无序使横向集体模式局域化，在固定纵向动量下强度剖面的增长被抑制，其时间平均空间衰减给出局域化长度；等时关联对无序不敏感，主要反映相互作用程：偶极耦合下随管间距按r^{-7}衰减，最近邻耦合则为指数衰减；足够强的管间排斥使纵向均匀态失稳，形成相邻管间交错的密度调制，且阈值与密度不平衡无关。

Conclusion: 无隧穿的偶极耦合一维玻色气体阵列可实现横向动量输运，其动力学由准粒子弹道传播与退相干主导，并可被密度不平衡引起的无序局域化；等时关联主要由相互作用程决定，且系统存在与无序无关的管间交错密度失稳阈值。

Abstract: We study transverse transport of longitudinal momentum in an array of one-dimensional Bose gases coupled by dipolar interactions without particle tunneling. Within zero-temperature Bogoliubov theory, we characterize this transport using the inter-tube momentum correlator and its squared magnitude, termed amplitude and intensity. In a uniform array, pairs of Bogoliubov quasiparticles propagate ballistically across the tubes, while their dephasing produces a $t^{-3}$ decay of the long-wavelength amplitude. Tube-to-tube density imbalance acts as quenched disorder and localizes the transverse collective modes. At fixed longitudinal momentum, this arrests the growth of the intensity profile, whose time-averaged spatial decay measures the localization length. Equal-time correlations are much less sensitive to disorder and primarily reflect the interaction range: in the uniform array they decay with tube separation as $r^{-7}$ for dipolar coupling, compared with exponential decay for nearest-neighbor coupling. Finally, sufficiently strong inter-tube repulsion destabilizes the longitudinally uniform state toward density modulations staggered between neighboring tubes, at a threshold independent of density imbalance.

</details>


### [171] [Metastability, collective excitations, and traveling states of one-dimensional spin-orbit-coupled quantum droplets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39361)
*Harsimranjit Kaur,Ritu,Rajat,Kuldeep Suthar,Sandeep Gautam*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 研究含Lee-Huang-Yang修正的自旋-轨道耦合一维Bose-Bose混合体系中的静态与运动液滴相，揭示条纹液滴到零动量液滴的一阶相变及运动液滴的纵向磁化与稳定相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究自旋-轨道耦合与量子涨落（LHY修正）共同作用下，一维Bose-Bose混合体系中液滴的基态相结构及其运动行为，厘清条纹相与零动量相的竞争与转变机制。

Method: 对无限延展混合体系采用变分分析求基态；对热力学极限液滴及"平顶区"有限尺寸液滴进行数值求解验证；计算有限尺寸条纹液滴的激发谱判断稳定性；在液滴共动系中求解基态相图，结合空间固定系的实时演化与共动系Bogoliubov分析考察运动液滴稳定性。

Result: 静态情形下存在两种基态液滴相：条纹液滴相在临界Rabi耦合强度处经一阶相变转为零动量液滴相；变分结果与数值解吻合良好。有限尺寸条纹液滴可存在多种具有不同条纹峰数的定态，激发谱证实其稳定。共动系中液滴获得有限纵向磁化，相图呈现运动条纹液滴与平面波液滴两相，实时演化与Bogoliubov分析表明其稳定。

Conclusion: 自旋-轨道耦合一维Bose-Bose混合液滴的基态由条纹相与零动量相构成，二者间为一阶相变；有限尺寸下多重条纹定态稳定存在；运动液滴因纵向磁化而呈现运动条纹相与平面波相，且保持动力学稳定。

Abstract: We study the static and traveling droplet phases of spin-orbit coupled, one-dimensional Bose-Bose mixtures with a Lee-Huang-Yang correction. For the static droplets, employing a variational analysis for an infinitely extended mixture, we show that the system can feature two ground-state droplet phases: a stripe droplet phase up to a critical Rabi coupling strength, at which it undergoes a first-order transition to a zero-momentum droplet phase. The variational results for the ground-state properties are shown to be in good agreement with numerical solutions for droplets in the thermodynamic limit as well as finite-size droplets in the "flat-top regime". For finite-size droplets, the stripe phase can feature multiple stationary states with different numbers of peaks in their stripe density patterns. The calculation of the excitation spectrum for these stationary states confirms their stability. To study the moving droplets, we investigate the ground-state phases of the system in the droplet's comoving frame. We show that in the comoving frame, droplets acquire a finite longitudinal magnetization. As a consequence, the phase diagram features two distinct phases: a traveling stripe droplet and a plane-wave droplet. The real-time evolution of these droplets in the space-fixed frame, complemented by the Bogoliubov analysis in the comoving frame, illustrates the stability of these traveling droplet phases.

</details>


### [172] [Kelvin-Wave Multiplication by Sound Emission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39909)
*Weican Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 亚声速的Kelvin量子可通过发射一个声子并分裂为两个能量更低的量子，实现Kelvin数增长而总能量下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Kelvin波（量子涡旋上的螺旋扰动）在超流体系中的非线性衰变/分裂机制及其与声子（声波）的耦合，明确该过程是否受对称性与色散关系约束。

Method: 利用软声子极限下的旋转不变性，将声子轴向动量反演下为奇的领头声学剪切振幅与释放能量相联系；并结合微观计算检验该色散依赖性；最后在含相干声场与Kelvin种子的反射腔中进行Gross–Pitaevskii演化模拟。

Result: 解析关系与微观计算一致，支持上述色散依赖；GP数值演化显示Kelvin模增益、均方位移增大、均方斜率减小，且初始声相位平移π可使这些变化反向。

Conclusion: 亚声速Kelvin量子的声子发射型分裂由对称性支配，其效应依赖于声场相位；相干声场可作为控制Kelvin动力学（增益与几何形变）的可调手段。

Abstract: A subsonic Kelvin quantum can emit a phonon and split into two softer quanta, increasing Kelvin population while losing energy. In the soft-phonon limit, rotational invariance relates the leading acoustic shear amplitude, odd under reversal of phonon axial momentum, to the energy released. Microscopic calculations support this dispersion dependence. In a reflecting cavity with coherent sound and Kelvin seeds, Gross--Pitaevskii evolution shows Kelvin gain, larger mean-square displacement and smaller mean-square slope; a $π$ shift of the initial sound phase reverses these changes.

</details>


### [173] [Single-atom billiards in an ultracold bath with energy-tunable collisions and threshold-sensitive detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40098)
*Silvia Hiebel,Sabrina Burgardt,Julian Feß,Artur Widera*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 通过将单个铯原子以可控速度穿过超冷铷浴，实现能量可调的单原子对撞机，把微观碰撞能量转移转化为单原子二值观测，连接单次散射事件与耗散输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微观尺度输运过程（尤其在不规则热介质中）与宏观行为差异显著，需要高可控、可直接观测的实验平台来研究此类输运。

Method: 在光晶格中以恒定速度输运单个铯原子穿过超冷铷浴；晶格速度设定碰撞能量分布中的定向贡献，晶格深度提供碰撞诱导输运中断的能量阈值；独立改变速度、深度、浴密度与温度，分离碰撞事件数与中断概率。

Result: 在每次穿过预期仅数次碰撞的区域内，推断出的单次碰撞弹射概率随碰撞能量超过晶格束缚尺度而从小值上升至接近1。

Conclusion: 实验将微观碰撞能量转移转化为二值单原子可观测信号，为单次散射事件与涌现耗散输运之间提供可控联系。

Abstract: Transport processes at the microscopic scale can differ strongly from their macroscopic counterparts, particularly when the environment is not a well-defined surface but an unordered thermal medium. Ultracold quantum gases offer a high degree of controllability and directly observable dynamics, thereby providing a versatile platform to study such transport processes under precisely controllable conditions. Here, we realize an energy-tunable single-atom collider by transporting individual cesium atoms at controlled velocity through an ultracold rubidium bath. The constant lattice velocity sets the directed contribution to the collision-energy distribution, while the lattice depth provides a tunable energy threshold for collision-induced transport disruption. By independently varying these parameters together with bath density and temperature, we separate the number of collision events from their probability to disrupt transport. Across a regime with only a few expected collisions per passage, the inferred single-collision ejection probability rises from small values to near unity as the collision energy exceeds the lattice confinement scale. The experiment thus converts microscopic collisional energy transfer into a binary single-atom observable and provides a controlled link between individual scattering events and emergent dissipative transport.

</details>


### [174] [Multidimensional revival dynamics in a bosonic many-body system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40353)
*Denis Ullmo,Steven Tomsovic,Gabriel M. Lando,Peter Schlagheck*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究对称双格点Bose-Hubbard模型在强自囚禁区从相干初态出发的非平衡动力学，系统推导期望值、相干性与自关联函数中的坍缩-复苏行为，并用半经典理论统一解释五个新的复苏"谜题"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往量子复苏研究多局限于薛定谔力学框架下的准一维体系，且主要依赖自关联函数作为观测量。玻色多体情形维度更高、可观测量更丰富，复苏问题更为复杂，亟需在最简单的非一维玻色多体系统中系统考察。

Method: 以对称双格点Bose-Hubbard模型（可积二维系统）为对象，制备单格点相干初态，在强自囚禁区求解动力学；理论上推导期望值、相干性及自关联函数中的复苏行为，展示纠缠演化，并基于底层经典（平均场）相空间结构构建完整半经典理论，与半经典预言及量子微扰计算进行定量比较。

Result: 观察到五个在薛定谔语境中未知的新特征，包括同步复苏（synchronized resurgence）行为、复苏时间依赖具体可观测量、以及源于平均场动力学的进动（precession）表现。半经典预言与量子微扰计算整体吻合极佳。

Conclusion: 这些真正量子的多体干涉现象可完全由基于经典相空间结构的完整半经典理论理解，表明半经典理论对解释此类多体干涉现象至关重要。

Abstract: The collapse and revival of localized states in many-body systems is a quintessential example of a quantum interference phenomenon that cannot be explained purely within mean-field theories. Historically, the majority of previous work on quantum revivals has been in a Schrödinger mechanics context, typically for effectively 1D systems with a focus on the autocorrelation function as the key detection observable. The general revival subject acquires a far greater complexity in the bosonic many-body context that offers a larger variety of observables and intrinsically implies high dimensionality. Here, the focus is on the simplest nontrivial (i.e., not 1D) bosonic many-body system, namely a symmetric two-site Bose-Hubbard model. The non-equilibrium situation of a coherent initial state prepared on one of the two sites of this integrable 2D system in the strongly self-trapping regime is considered. The revival behaviors emerging from the resulting dynamics within expectation values, coherences, and the autocorrelation function are theoretically derived, and the entanglement behavior is illustrated. Several new features, unknown so far in the Schrödinger context and identified here in terms of five ``puzzles'', are encountered and discussed, among them, a synchronized resurgence behavior, differing revivals times depending on the specific observable, and a manifestation of a precession that appears in the underlying mean field dynamics. These new phenomena can be entirely understood through a comprehensive semiclassical theory, building upon the underlying classical (mean-field) phase-space structure. A quantitative comparison of the latter with semiclassical predictions and quantum perturbative calculations yields an overall excellent agreement and confirms that a full semiclassical theory is pivotal for the understanding of these genuinely quantum many-body interference phenomena.

</details>


### [175] [Static dipolar impurity in a Fermi gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40460)
*Eugen Dizer,Xin Chen,Richard Schmidt,Arthur Christianen*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 研究静态偶极杂质浸入费米气体中偶极相互作用对少体散射与极化子物理的影响，发现基态同时占据偶/奇波束缚态，且长程偶极作用削弱Anderson正交灾变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究各向异性长程偶极相互作用如何同时塑造少体散射性质与由此产生的极化子物理，并检验常用近似方法的适用性。

Method: 基于Fumi定理将各向异性相移与基态能量直接联系；采用泛函行列式方法精确计算杂质谱；并对比仅含单个粒子-空穴激发的变分拟设。

Result: 精确谱表现为独立偶波与奇波贡献的卷积，基态同时布居偶波与奇波束缚态，其间距可由偶极长度调节；单粒子-空穴变分拟设无法描述该双占据基态；长程偶极作用削弱Anderson正交灾变，减缓准粒子权重的幂律衰减。

Conclusion: 偶极相互作用导致双重占据的极化子基态与减弱的正交灾变，可通过微波屏蔽下相互作用可独立调控的超冷极性分子进行实验验证。

Abstract: We consider a static dipolar impurity immersed in a Fermi gas and show how dipolar interactions shape both the few-body scattering properties and the resulting polaron physics. Using Fumi's theorem, we connect the anisotropic phase shifts directly to the ground-state energy, and compute the exact impurity spectrum via the functional determinant approach. The resulting spectrum is a convolution of independent even- and odd-wave contributions, giving rise to a ground state with simultaneously occupied even- and odd-wave bound states whose separation is tunable via the dipole length. A variational ansatz limited to one particle-hole excitation fails to capture this doubly occupied ground state. We further find that long-range dipolar interactions weaken Anderson's orthogonality catastrophe, reducing the power-law decay of the quasiparticle weight. These effects can be tested with ultracold polar molecules, where microwave shielding and independent tunability of interactions enable experimental access to the predicted regimes.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [Approaching Landauer's limit in a single superconducting vortex memory bit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37753)
*Nofar Fridman,Itai Keren,Avia Noah,Yishay Zur,Martin E. Huber,Nadav Katz,Roland Willa,Hadar Steinberg,Yonathan Anahory*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作利用纳米双势阱钉扎势中的超导磁通涡旋作为存储比特，研究其操控能耗：实验上施加正弦力并分析驱动力与频率的影响，观察到弱驱动下的随机共振与强低频驱动下的绝热操控；数值分析表明擦除所需功在4.2 K下仅比Landauer极限(k_BT ln2)高一个数量级以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算产业能耗持续攀升，亟需低能耗存储元件；超导涡旋比特有望与低温量子电路集成或作为拓扑量子计算比特，但其操控能耗的下限尚不明确。

Method: 将单个超导涡旋限制在纳米尺度双势阱钉扎势中构成双稳比特；实验对多种双势阱体系施加正弦驱动力，系统改变驱动幅度与频率；并结合数值模拟评估比特擦除操作所需功，与Landauer极限对比。

Result: 弱驱动下呈现典型随机共振行为，强低频驱动下表现为绝热操控；尽管实验方法所需的稳态正弦驱动与Landauer擦除协议不相容，数值分析显示4.2 K下比特擦除所需功处于k_BT ln2的一个数量级以内。

Conclusion: 超导涡旋双势阱比特的操控能耗可接近Landauer极限，为低功耗低温存储元件及与量子电路/拓扑量子比特的集成提供了可行性依据，有助于缓解计算产业的能耗压力。

Abstract: This work investigates the energy required to manipulate a memory bit using a superconducting vortex confined in a nanoscale double-well pinning potential. In the experiments, various double-well systems subjected to sinusoidal forces is explored, and the effects of both driving force and frequency is analyzed. Results reveal characteristic stochastic resonance behavior for weak drives and adiabatic manipulation at strong, low-frequency drives. Although our experimental method requires a steady sinusoidal drive incompatible with Landauer's erasure protocol, our numerical analysis demonstrates that the work required for such bit manipulation (erasure) is within an order of magnitude of Landauer's limit ($k_B T \ln 2$) at 4.2 K. These findings open possibilities for creating highly efficient memory elements that could be integrated with cryogenic quantum circuits or function as topological quantum computing qubits; thereby contributing to memory devices with lower energy consumption and mitigating the escalating global energy demand in the computing industry.

</details>


### [177] [Finite-step statistical mechanics of scale-invariant learning: spectral equations of state and curvature transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38012)
*Alexander Itin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文论证随机梯度动力学中的熵标度关系可由尺度对称性（而非热平衡）导出，给出精确的曲率输运恒等式，将谐波涨落关系的修正分解为曲率变化、有限步余项、梯度记忆关联与有限窗漂移项，并构造时间箭头诊断量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平稳学习过程只有在严格噪声假设下才服从理想气体式描述，而神经网络的梯度噪声偏离这些假设。作者希望区分"源于尺度对称性的类热力学关系"与"源于平衡假设的关系"，并量化对理想气体描述的偏离程度，针对带记忆的离散随机梯度动力学建立可测诊断量。

Method: (1) 通过轨迹的精确重标度解释熵关系，不依赖热平衡；(2) 在局部谐波近似下，由自适应权重范数导出谱状态方程，其维里系数由曲率谱矩决定，并指出耦合参数组可存在平稳概率流；(3) 核心结果为精确的曲率输运恒等式，结合有限步损失平衡，将谐波涨落关系的修正分解为可测曲率变化、有限步余项、梯度记忆关联与有限窗漂移项；(4) 以位移加权曲率变化构造时间箭头诊断量，其均值与二阶矩给出正/反向状态对相对熵的下界；(5) 解析计算高斯输入下整流教师-学生网络的激活边界曲率贡献。

Result: 在球面模型、教师-学生网络以及 CIFAR-10/CIFAR-100 上的卷积网络上验证了输运描述，并界定了谐波近似与三次余项近似的适用范围与失效情形；位移加权曲率变化的均值与二阶矩给出相对熵下界，可作为非平衡性诊断。

Conclusion: 研究将基于对称性的关系与平衡假设分离，为学习动力学中的非理想涨落提供了定量诊断工具；曲率输运恒等式构成超越谐波近似的精确框架。

Abstract: Stationary learning admits an ideal-gas description under restrictive noise assumptions. In neural networks whose gradient noise departs from these assumptions, however, an entropy-scaling relation resembling a Maxwell relation remains approximately valid. We ask which thermodynamic-looking relations follow from scale symmetry rather than equilibrium, and how departures from the ideal-gas description can be quantified, for discrete stochastic gradient dynamics with memory. An exact rescaling of trajectories explains the entropy relation without assuming thermal equilibrium. Within a local harmonic approximation, self-adjusting weight norms yield a spectral equation of state whose virial coefficients are set by moments of the curvature spectrum, and coupled parameter groups can sustain stationary probability currents. Our central result beyond this approximation is an exact curvature-transport identity. Together with a finite-step loss balance, it decomposes corrections to the harmonic fluctuation relation into measurable curvature changes, finite-step remainders, gradient-memory correlations and finite-window drift terms. A related displacement-weighted curvature change provides a diagnostic of the arrow of time; its mean and second moment give a lower bound on the relative entropy between forward and reversed state pairs. We also calculate the activation-boundary contribution to curvature for rectified teacher-student networks with Gaussian inputs. Tests in sphere models, teacher-student networks and convolutional networks on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 support the transport description and identify limits of the harmonic and cubic-remainder approximations. These results separate symmetry-based relations from equilibrium assumptions and provide quantitative diagnostics of nonideal fluctuations in learning dynamics.

</details>


### [178] [Far Tails of the Positional Distribution in random walk: A Saddle-Point Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38492)
*Chen Zhou,YuDa Cheng,Yangji Li,Jing Xu,Wanli Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文用鞍点法研究连续时间随机游走在普通与平衡初始条件下的位置分布稀有事件，揭示远尾行为对等待时间分布在最小值附近展开形式的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀有事件与大偏差理论在随机游走研究中广受关注，但连续时间随机游走位置分布的远尾稀有事件统计及其对初始条件、等待时间分布细节的依赖性尚缺乏系统刻画，本文旨在填补该空白。

Method: 采用鞍点法（saddle point method）解析求解连续时间随机游走位置分布的远尾大偏差行为，分别在普通初始条件与平衡初始条件下进行分析；并考察最小等待时间附近等待时间分布的不同展开形式（一般幂级数展开与在最小值处截断）以及到达给定位移所需最可能更新次数及相应速率函数。

Result: 远尾行为对等待时间分布在最小值附近的展开高度敏感：一般幂级数展开下位置分布远尾近似呈指数衰减；而当等待时间分布在最小值处存在截断时，位置分布呈现意外的Gaussian衰减，这与更新计数分布的远尾行为形成鲜明对比。此外，到达给定位移的最可能更新次数及相应速率函数对初始条件不敏感，但位置分布的远尾仍保留对初始条件的依赖性。

Conclusion: 连续时间随机游走位置分布的远尾统计由等待时间分布在最小值附近的解析性质决定，可表现为指数或Gaussian衰减；初始条件影响远尾而基本不影响最可能更新次数和速率函数。研究为随机游走的稀有事件统计与大偏差提供了新见解。

Abstract: Rare events and large-deviation theory have attracted considerable interest in the study of random walks. Here, we investigate the rare events of the positional distribution of continuous-time random walks under ordinary and equilibrium initial conditions using the saddle point method. We show that the far-tail behavior is sensitive to the expansion of the waiting-time distribution near its minimum value. For a general power-series expansion near the minimum waiting time, the positional distribution exhibits an approximately exponential decay in its far tails. In contrast, when the waiting-time distribution has a cutoff at its minimum value, the positional distribution exhibits an unexpected Gaussian decay, in contrast to the far-tail behavior of the renewal-count distribution. We further find that the most likely number of renewals required to reach a given position, as well as the associated rate functions, is insensitive to the initial conditions, whereas the far tails of the positional distribution retain a dependence on the initial conditions. Our results provide new insights into the rare-event statistics and large deviations of random walks.

</details>


### [179] [Swimming versus Screening: How Activity Reshapes the Ionic Cloud of a Charged Particle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38594)
*Tanmoy Chakraborty,Tomer Markovich,Ram M. Adar*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 游动者的持续运动会定性改变稳态离子电荷密度分布：低活性下恢复Debye-Hückel屏蔽，高活性下出现幂律、拉伸指数与更长屏蔽长度（λ>λ_D）的"欠屏蔽"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活性物质中的核心问题之一是粒子间相互作用如何被活性（activity）所改变。作者针对库仑相互作用提出该问题：将一个带电游动者置于电解质中，考察其自身持续运动如何影响静电屏蔽。

Method: 理论分析：将带电游动者浸入电解质，比较静态电荷被Debye长度λ_D衰减的离子云屏蔽，推导游动者持续运动下稳态离子电荷密度剖面的变化。按活性强弱分区间讨论：低活性（离子视角准静态）与高活性（弹道—扩散动力学），并按与游动者距离划分不同空间区域。

Result: 低活性：恢复常规Debye-Hückel屏蔽。较高活性：弹道—扩散动力学在中距离先给出幂律衰减，随后过渡为拉伸指数衰减。足够远处恢复指数屏蔽，但屏蔽长度增大（λ>λ_D），即活性诱导的欠屏蔽（underscreening）。

Conclusion: 活性运动同时改变静电屏蔽的空间形式与作用范围。结果为研究由带正、负电荷游动者组成的"活性电解质"提供了动机。

Abstract: A major question in active matter is how interactions between particles are modified by activity. Here, we address this question for Coulomb interactions by considering a charged swimmer immersed in an electrolyte. A static charge is screened by an ionic cloud that decays over the Debye length $λ_D$. We show that the persistent motion of the swimmer can qualitatively modify the steady-state ionic charge-density profile. At low activity, the swimmer appears effectively quasistatic to the ions, and conventional Debye-Hückel screening is recovered. At higher activity, the ballistic-to-diffusive dynamics of the swimmer are reflected in distinct spatial regimes. A power-law decay is followed by a stretched-exponential decay at intermediate distances. At sufficiently large distances, exponential screening is restored, but with an increased screening length, $λ>λ_D$, revealing activity-induced underscreening. Thus, active motion modifies both the spatial form and the range of electrostatic screening. Our results motivate the study of active electrolytes composed of positively and negatively charged swimmers.

</details>


### [180] [Information-Theoretic Reliability--Safety Bounds and Finite-Alignment Dynamics in AI Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38651)
*Miao-Miao Yi,Zhen-Yu Cai,Y -M. Du,C. P. Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文建立了AI可靠性与安全性权衡的信息论界限，并刻画了知识蒸馏下该权衡的动力学，利用重整化群方法得出多代蒸馏的精确递推关系与临界行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI的可靠性与安全性及其在知识蒸馏中的迁移与演化日益受到关注，但缺乏与模型形式无关的理论界限，以及蒸馏过程中权衡关系如何变化的定量描述。

Method: 先证明与模型形式无关的普适权衡不等式，其边界由量化隐藏层区分良性/危险输入能力的单参数Kh决定；随后构建AI响应的正则Boltzmann机模型，导出可靠性与安全性的两个精确关系（曲线由危险判别能力K决定，整体回答倾向控制曲线上的工作点）；再以重整化群方法处理多代蒸馏，对两种代表性直接跨代蒸馏协议在任意师生对齐强度下推导精确递推关系与重整化群流。

Result: 所得权衡关系与现有实验数据吻合良好；蒸馏中教师响应引起学生模型权衡参数的重整化；递推流揭示不动点结构、长期动力学与临界行为，并对能力损失与稳定传递之间的临界转变给出可检验预测。

Conclusion: 可靠性与安全性存在由判别能力单参数刻画的普适权衡，蒸馏等价于参数重整化，多代蒸馏呈现类重整化群的不动点与临界现象，为理解模型能力传递与安全退化提供理论框架与可检验预言。

Abstract: The reliability and safety of artificial intelligence (AI), together with their transfer and evolution through knowledge distillation, have attracted growing attention. We establish an information-theoretic bound on the reliability--safety trade-off and characterize the dynamics of this trade-off under knowledge distillation. We first prove a universal trade-off inequality that is independent of the specific model form. Its boundary is determined solely by a single parameter Kh, which quantifies the hidden layer's ability to distinguish benign from hazardous inputs. We then develop a canonical Boltzmann-machine model of AI response behavior and derive two exact relations between reliability and safety. The trade-off curve is determined by the hazard discrimination capacity K, while the overall answer tendency controls the operating point along the curve. These relations agree well with existing experimental data. In knowledge distillation, we find that teacher responses induce a renormalization of the student model's trade-off parameters. We further use a renormalization-group approach to study multigenerational distillation, deriving exact recursion relations and the associated renormalization-group-like flows for two representative direct intergenerational distillation protocols at arbitrary teacher--student alignment strength. These flows reveal fixed-point structures, long-term dynamics, and critical behavior, yielding testable predictions for the critical transition between capability loss and stable transmission.

</details>


### [181] [Finite-time minimum-work protocols for underdamped traps driven by active forces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38653)
*Kangcan Yao,Ruicheng Bao,Zhonghuai Hou*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文针对欠阻尼活性粒子在平移谐振势阱中的功提取问题，设计了最小功协议，发现最小平均功及其涨落与质量无关，并揭示了惯性与活性对最优协议的共同影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活性系统可提供非平衡能量，理论上可通过控制协议提取，但惯性与活性如何共同影响最优功提取协议、最大可提取功及其涨落、协议可实现性尚不清楚。

Method: 为平移谐振势阱中的欠阻尼活性粒子设计最小功协议（在可提取时最大化功提取）；应用于欠阻尼活性Ornstein-Uhlenbeck粒子（AOUP），构建显式功提取判据，并分析提取功对旋转噪声和初始条件的依赖。

Result: 最小平均功及其涨落均与质量无关，尽管惯性会改变协议形式；惯性强烈影响最优协议的实施难度，在欠阻尼与过阻尼最优协议之间产生类相变式转变。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了微观尺度功提取机制，可为可靠的能量收集微型器件与机器人设计提供指导。

Abstract: Active systems provide non-equilibrium energy that can, in principle, be harvested using control protocols, making the optimization and reliability of work extraction a fundamental problem in microscopic thermodynamics. Yet how inertia and activity jointly modify the optimal work extraction protocol, the maximum extractable work its fluctuations, and implementability of protocols remains unclear. Here, we design minimum-work protocols, which maximize work extraction whenever extraction is possible, for an underdamped active particle in a translated harmonic trap. Remarkably, both the minimum mean work and its fluctuations are independent of mass, although inertia modifies the protocol. The results are then applied to an underdamped active Ornstein-Uhlenbeck particle (AOUP), designing an explicit work-extraction criterion and revealing extracted work's dependence on rotational noise and initial conditions. We further show that inertia strongly affects the difficulty of implementing the optimal protocol, producing a transition-like change between underdamped and overdamped optimal protocols. These findings uncover work extraction mechanisms at the microscale and guide the design of reliable energy-harvesting microscopic devices and robots.

</details>


### [182] [Thermodynamic cost of locally maintaining a steady state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38925)
*Sarang Gopalakrishnan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 局部哈密顿量的Gibbs态具有短程条件关联，但存在严格局域、满足细致平衡的高斯过程，其唯一稳态却具有任意长的条件关联；若用局域二次哈密顿量耦合局域快速弛豫热浴来实现，则必须产生熵，且每格点每弛豫时间的熵产生满足 σ̂ ≥ c ξ²。离散态跳跃过程在不可约假设下则须破坏细致平衡，作者猜测快速混合的局域跳跃过程无法稳定长程CMI稳态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gibbs态的条件关联是短程的，直觉上要保持长程条件关联的稳态，随机过程须在非局域跃迁或熵产生上"付出代价"。作者检验这一直觉是否成立，并量化其中的代价。

Method: 构造严格局域、满足细致平衡的高斯过程，使其唯一稳态具有任意长程条件互信息（CMI）；研究由局域二次哈密顿量与局域耦合的快速弛豫热浴实现时的热力学代价；以CMI的指数衰减长度（Markov长度ξ）刻画关联范围，推导熵产生下界；并给出耦合范围与熵产生之间的权衡界。同时与离散态空间跳跃过程对比。

Result: 存在局域、细致平衡的高斯过程，其唯一稳态CMI随距离的衰减长度ξ可任意大，突破了"Gibbs态必短程"的直觉。但用局域二次哈密顿量加局域快速弛豫热浴实现时必然产生熵，且大ξ时 σ̂ ≥ c ξ²（c为实现相关常数），并给出耦合范围与熵产生的权衡界。离散态跳跃过程在不可约性假设下，长程CMI稳态必须违反细致平衡。

Conclusion: 长程条件关联的稳态在局域高斯细致平衡过程中是可实现的，但热力学上以熵产生（随ξ平方增长）为代价；耦合范围与熵产生可权衡。对离散跳跃过程，作者猜想快速混合的局域过程无法稳定长程CMI稳态，并给出支持证据。

Abstract: The Gibbs states of local Hamiltonians have short-range conditional correlations. To maintain a steady state with long-range conditional correlations, one might expect that a stochastic process must ``pay'' either in nonlocal moves or entropy production. Surprisingly, we find strictly local Gaussian processes that obey detailed balance and have unique steady states with arbitrarily long-range conditional correlations. We show that such processes must still produce entropy, if implemented using a local quadratic Hamiltonian coupled locally to equilibrium, rapidly relaxing baths. We lower-bound this entropy production in terms of the steady state's conditional mutual information (CMI); if the CMI decays exponentially with distance, the associated length-scale is called the Markov length $ξ$. The entropy production per site per relaxation time obeys $\hatσ\ge c ξ^2$ for large $ξ$, and with $c$ an implementation-dependent constant. We also bound the tradeoff between coupling range and entropy production. We contrast these Gaussian processes with jump processes on discrete state spaces, for which, under certain irreducibility assumptions, steady states with long-range CMI are only possible if detailed balance is violated. We conjecture (and present evidence) that rapidly mixing local jump processes cannot stabilize steady states with long-range CMI.

</details>


### [183] [Two faces of symmetry match in gear-like coupled rotary motors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39121)
*Sara Iranbakhsh,David A. Sivak*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 旋转对称性在耦合的随机无势垒旋转马达中产生基本权衡：在紧耦合下最大化输出功率的齿轮比，同时也是需要最强耦合才能保持锁定的比值；削弱耦合会使齿轮效应反转，将功率极大变为局部极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转分子马达常耦合不同旋转对称性的组件，但这种"齿轮传动"如何控制能量转换尚不清楚，本文旨在揭示对称性与耦合强度对能量转导的控制机制。

Method: 建立耦合的随机无势垒旋转马达模型，理论分析对称性与齿轮比、耦合强度的关系；并对有限能量势垒的转子数值求解Fokker-Planck动力学，分别考虑按每转与按每亚基固定驱动强度的情形。

Result: 理论预测：紧耦合下功率最优的齿轮比恰是最易滑移、需最强耦合保持锁定的比值；弱化耦合可反转齿轮效应。强驱动下，功率最优比与最易滑移比均趋近对称性匹配。Fokker-Planck数值解复现这些预测，并揭示驱动强度定义方式（每转/每亚基）改变行为。

Conclusion: 耦合强度是决定匹配旋转对称性增强还是抑制能量转导的关键因素，为耦合分子马达提供了通用设计原理；以细菌鞭毛马达为动机。

Abstract: Rotary molecular motors commonly couple components with different rotational symmetries, but how this gearing controls energy transduction remains unclear. Here we show that symmetry can produce a fundamental tradeoff in coupled stochastic barrierless rotary motors: the gear ratio that maximizes output power under tight coupling is also the ratio that requires the strongest coupling to remain locked. Consequently, weakening the coupling can invert the effect of gearing, transforming a power maximum into a local minimum. At strong driving, both the power-optimal and most slip-susceptible ratios approach symmetry match. Numerical solutions of the Fokker-Planck dynamics for rotors with finite energy barriers reproduce these predictions and reveal how the behavior changes when driving strength is fixed per rotation or per subunit. Motivated by the bacterial flagellar motor, these results identify coupling strength as a key determinant of whether matched rotational symmetries enhance or suppress energy transduction, providing a general design principle for coupled molecular motors.

</details>


### [184] [The Constructive Cavity Method for the Ising Perceptron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39141)
*Simone Franchini,Francesco Concetti,Giampiero Bardella*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 将构造性空腔方法（CCM）应用于Ising感知机，借助正软通道的辅助表示把感知机改写为稠密二分高斯模型，得到两个构造性方差剖面与带三个通道项的标量空腔泛函，并证明其与ROSt变量一致、经Parisi递归平均化后可复现Gardner公式与Krauth–Mézard泛函。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ising感知机（含硬通道的二值感知机）的空腔/副本分析多为启发式或缺乏构造性基础，本文旨在用CCM给出严格的构造性框架，统一软通道与硬通道情形。

Method: 从正软通道 U(x)=∫e^{tx}ρ(dt) 出发（辅助测度具有限指数二次矩），将感知机重写为Ising层与连续辅助层构成的稠密二分高斯模型；用CCM blanket几何定义Ising侧方差剖面Q_ℓ与辅助侧Γ_ℓ，以二分壳层尺度 Q_{ℓ+1}Γ_{ℓ+1}−Q_ℓΓ_ℓ 刻画，并用高斯核心比较在blanket细化下消去核心项ΔQ_ℓΔΓ_ℓ；构造三通道标量空腔泛函A_perc(ν)，将构造性空腔变量等同于Aizenman–Sims–Starr随机重叠结构（ROSt）变量，再经Parisi递归平均化得到G_perc^CCM。

Result: 得到显式自由能表达式 G_perc^CCM(ζ,Q,Γ)=X_0(0)+αY_0(0)−½∑_{ℓ=0}^{R}ζ_ℓ(Q_{ℓ+1}Γ_{ℓ+1}−Q_ℓΓ_ℓ)；其副本对称特化给出广义Ising感知机的Gardner公式，将约束终端特化为硬通道则给出二值感知机的Krauth–Mézard泛函。

Conclusion: CCM为Ising感知机提供了构造性、可验证的空腔推导，涵盖软/硬通道，并与经典副本结果（Gardner、Krauth–Mézard）一致，表明ROSt结构与Parisi递归是感知机空腔泛函的自然表述。

Abstract: We apply the Constructive Cavity Method (CCM) to the Ising perceptron. The process starts from positive soft channels admitting an auxiliary representation $$ U(x)=\int_{\mathbb{R}} e^{tx}ρ(\mathrm{d}t),\qquad ρ\ge0, $$ with an auxiliary measure having a finite exponential quadratic moment. This rewrites the perceptron as a dense, bipartite Gaussian model with an Ising layer and a continuous auxiliary layer. The CCM blanket geometry then gives two constructive variance profiles, $Q_\ell$ on the Ising side and $Γ_\ell$ on the auxiliary side. The bipartite shell scale is $$ Q_{\ell+1}Γ_{\ell+1}-Q_\ellΓ_\ell, $$ and Gaussian core comparison removes the core contribution $ΔQ_\ellΔΓ_\ell$ under blanket refinement. The scalar cavity functional has three terms, $$ \mathcal{A}_{\mathrm{perc}}(ν)=\mathbb{E}\left[\log\langle2\cosh x\rangle_ν+α\log\langle U(s)\rangle_ν-\log\langle e^y\rangle_ν\right], $$ where $x$ is the new-spin field, $s$ is the new-constraint field, and $y$ is the cavity correction. CCM identifies the constructive cavity variables with the Aizenman--Sims--Starr random overlap structure (ROSt) variables. Parisi's recursive averaging then gives $$ \mathcal{G}_{\mathrm{perc}}^{\mathrm{CCM}}(ζ,Q,Γ)=X_0(0)+αY_0(0)-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\ell=0}^{R}ζ_\ell\left(Q_{\ell+1}Γ_{\ell+1}-Q_\ellΓ_\ell\right). $$ The replica-symmetric specialization gives Gardner's formula for the generalized Ising perceptron. Specializing the constraint terminal to the hard channel gives the Krauth--Mézard functional for the binary perceptron.

</details>


### [185] [Ten-loop fixed-dimension renormalization-group functions of the three-dimensional O(N) model from distributed GPU-accelerated parametric integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39307)
*Paolo Ribeca*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 将三维 O(N) 模型的 β、η、ν 重整化群函数从八阶推广到十阶（β 至十一阶），并给出 N=1、N=3 的重求和结果，与共形自举对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前固定维度下三维 O(N) 模型的重整化群函数仅已知至八阶，精度受限于系数误差，希望获得更高阶系数以提高临界指数的精度并检验与共形自举的一致性。

Method: 在固定维度零动量减除方案下计算两点和四点函数至十圈（末阶 1,593,254 个图）；通过 Callan-Symanzik 方程，将各阶系数化为逐阶线性求和的有限零动量 R̄-减除参数积分（每图一个）；采用热带蒙特卡洛在 GPU 上抽样，密度按森林项自适应调整。

Result: 在三个标准差内复现二至七阶已知结果，九、十阶在较低精度下与中间方案的独立路径一致；新系数相对不确定度优于几×10⁻⁵，η 与 ν 的八阶系数精度分别提高 120 倍和 4 倍；从七圈起每增加一圈积分耗时仅增长 2.4–2.9 倍，但被积函数构造仍阶乘增长，十阶时已超过积分本身耗时。

Conclusion: 重求和后 N=1 得 ν=0.62997(51)、η=0.0333(18)、ω=0.803(14)，与共形自举值 0.62997097(12)、0.036297612(48)、0.82968(23) 相比偏差明显；N=3 时偏差更大，误差现由重求和而非系数主导。所有结果均由 GitHub 上公开的、可独立汇总的运行账本自动生成或校验，欢迎贡献算力。

Abstract: The renormalization-group functions $β$, $η$ and $ν$ of the three-dimensional $O(N)$ model in fixed dimension (massive zero-momentum scheme) were known to eighth order in the coupling. We extend them to tenth order, and $β$ to eleventh, for general $N$, which requires the two- and four-point functions to ten loops (1,593,254 graphs at the last order), and re-evaluate all lower orders. Through the Callan-Symanzik equation every coefficient follows from per-order sums that are linear in finite, zero-momentum $\bar R$-subtracted parametric integrals, one per graph, which we sample on GPUs by tropical Monte Carlo with densities adapted to the forest terms. The computation reproduces the known sums from two to seven loops within three standard deviations and agrees at ninth and tenth order, at lower precision, with an independent route through an intermediate scheme. The new coefficients have relative uncertainties of a few $10^{-5}$ or better, and the eighth-order coefficients of $η$ and $ν$ improve by factors of 120 and 4. The time needed to integrate an order to fixed relative precision grows by only 2.4-2.9 per loop from seven loops on, while the construction of the integrands still grows factorially and, at tenth order, costs more than the integration. Resummed, the tenth-order series give $ν=0.62997(51)$, $η=0.0333(18)$ and $ω=0.803(14)$ for $N=1$, against $0.62997097(12)$, $0.036297612(48)$ and $0.82968(23)$ from the conformal bootstrap; at $N=3$ the deviations grow, so that the uncertainty is now dominated by the resummation rather than by the coefficients. Every result is generated automatically from, or checked against, a public ledger of independent, exactly pooled runs, hosted on GitHub, to which anyone is welcome to contribute computing time.

</details>


### [186] [Hop rates of H and D on Pt(111) at finite coverage: the memory equation and the collective friction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39436)
*S. Miret-Artés*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文用记忆方程与最近邻Ising格气模型重新分析Pt(111)上H和D的氦散射退相位速率，将单吸附物跳跃速率从集体速率中分离，并给出覆盖度无关的集体摩擦与衬底摩擦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已发表的氦散射测得的退相位速率究竟属于"单个吸附物"还是"整个吸附层"，此前并不清楚；若用单吸附物模型直接解释，可能混淆集体效应与单粒子跳跃。作者希望在不引入势垒模型的前提下，厘清两者关系并提取真实的跳跃速率与摩擦。

Method: 以氦散射所测相干中间散射函数为核心，用记忆方程结合最近邻Ising格气描述吸附层；将记忆函数解释为吸附层对自身密度涨落的"集体摩擦"；通过除以一个相互作用因子把单吸附物跳跃速率从集体速率中分离；由覆盖度序列确定横向耦合（相邻对的自由能，含振动对熵）；并用与Chudley–Elliott形式的偏离（tilt）作为耦合的可观测指标。记忆方程本身构成吸附层的过渡态理论，无交叉点发散。

Result: 在0.1 ML时相互作用因子为0.97–1.17，故此覆盖度下已发表的单吸附物分析成立；在0.4 ML、80 K时该因子为1.2–4.7，取决于占据近邻如何修正隧穿跳跃。0.15与0.2 ML处的tilt与理论一致，但从已发表曲线中难以精确提取。结合单吸附物量子过渡态理论，反推得衬底摩擦为H: 124 ps⁻¹、D: 75 ps⁻¹，且与覆盖度无关。

Conclusion: 氦散射测得的速率本质上是吸附层的集体量，需除以相互作用因子方可得到单吸附物跳跃速率；记忆函数可视为集体摩擦，并给出无交叉发散的吸附层过渡态理论；衬底摩擦对H和D分别为124与75 ps⁻¹，与覆盖度无关，为区分集体与单粒子动力学提供了自洽框架。

Abstract: Helium scattering measures the coherent intermediate scattering function of an adlayer, the characteristic function of the separation between adparticles at two times. Published dephasing rates of H and D on Pt(111) are analysed with its memory equation and a nearest-neighbour Ising lattice gas. The measured rate belongs to the adlayer. The hop rate of a single adsorbate is extracted from it by dividing out an interaction factor, without a model of the barrier. This factor lies between $0.97$ and $1.17$ at $0.1$~ML, so that the published single-adsorbate analyses hold there. At $0.4$~ML and $80$~K it lies between $1.2$ and $4.7$, depending on how the occupied neighbours modify a tunnelling jump. The memory function is interpreted as a friction, the collective friction, which the adlayer exerts on its own density fluctuations. The lateral coupling, fixed by the coverage series, is the free energy of a neighbouring pair, with a vibrational pair entropy. The small deformation of the measured curves from the Chudley--Elliott form, the tilt, is proposed as an observable of the coupling. The tilts at $0.15$ and $0.2$~ML are consistent with it, although they are hard to extract from the published curves. The memory equation provides a transition-state theory for the adlayer, with the collective friction in the role of the friction of the substrate and without divergence at the crossover. Combined with the standard quantum transition-state theory of a single adsorbate, the extracted hop rates give the friction of the substrate, $124$ and $75$~ps$^{-1}$ for H and D, independent of the coverage.

</details>


### [187] [Itô versus Stratonovich for shot noise with state-dependent amplitude: why the answer is Marcus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39511)
*Marco Bianucci,Riccardo Mannella*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文研究状态依赖增益的更新射击噪声（Dirac尖峰序列）下Itô与Stratonovich规定的差异：每事件差异有限、且在I(x)零点附近可为定性差异；Marcus kick是dx/ds=I(x)的流映射，不能跨越I的零点，Itô kick仅为其切映射。给出阈值判据、不可重整化结论与精确流积分器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯白噪声中Itô与Stratonovich规定仅差O(dt)/步，累积为熟知的伪漂移½D·II'，可被重整化漂移吸收。作者要考察具有状态依赖增益的更新射击噪声（乘性Dirac尖峰）下两规定的差异是否仍为微小量、能否被重整化吸收，以及零点附近是否出现定性差别。

Method: 解析比较Itô与Stratonovich（Marcus）kick：将Marcus kick定义为dx/ds=I(x)的流映射，Itô kick作为其切映射；对线性增益I(x)=1+βx导出跨越零点x*=-1/β的精确阈值条件及ξβ=1时的坍缩行为；通过单算子生成性分析检验主方程归属；与Heun二阶截断kick对比并分析其矩偏差来源。

Result: 两规定每事件差异有限而非O(dt)；Marcus kick对任意跳跃与等待时间统计均不能跨越I的零点x*。对I(x)=1+βx：当且仅当ξβ>1时Itô kick跨越x*，ξβ=1时整条直线坍缩至x*，低于阈值时Itô kick本身即带重映射跳跃律的Marcus kick。有界跳跃律存在阈值，无界跳跃律对任意参数均会泄漏越过势垒。差异在最低阶之外不可被重整化漂移吸收；仅Marcus kick由单一算子生成，故Bianucci–Bologna–Mannella (2026)主方程本质上是Marcus型。数值上标准Heun kick是Marcus映射的二阶截断，仅因偶数阶而保持势垒；它缩短每次跳跃，使二阶矩产生与跳跃幅度平方及峰度成正比的偏差，并耗竭势垒附近区域。

Conclusion: 射击噪声下Itô–Stratonovich差异本质有限且不可重整化，零点处呈定性区别；Marcus kick是唯一由单算子生成且严格保势垒的规定，应作为正确离散化基准。作者给出替代Heun的精确流积分器，以消除跳跃缩短、矩偏差与势垒附近密度耗竭等系统性误差。

Abstract: For Gaussian white noise the Itô and Stratonovich prescriptions differ by $O(dt)$ per time step, which accumulates into the familiar spurious drift $\tfrac12 D\,II'$. We show that for renewal shot noise with state-dependent gain, $\dot x=-C(x)-I(x)ξ[t]$ with $ξ[t]$ a train of Dirac spikes of finite random amplitude, the two prescriptions differ by a finite amount at every event, and that where $I$ vanishes the difference can become qualitative. The Marcus kick, the jump-process counterpart of the Stratonovich prescription, is the flow map of $dx/ds=I(x)$: no kick can cross a zero $x^\ast$ of $I$, for any jump and waiting-time statistics. The Itô kick is only the tangent map of that flow. For $I(x)=1+βx$ it crosses $x^\ast=-1/β$ if and only if $ξβ>1$ and collapses the whole line onto $x^\ast$ at $ξβ=1$; below threshold it is itself a Marcus kick with a remapped jump law. Bounded jump laws thus have a threshold, while unbounded ones leak across the barrier for \emph{any} parameters. The discrepancy cannot be absorbed into a renormalised drift beyond lowest order, and only the Marcus kick is generated by a single operator, which makes the master equation of Bianucci, Bologna and Mannella (2026) intrinsically Marcus. Numerically, the standard Heun kick is a second-order truncation of the Marcus map that preserves the barrier only because its order is even; it shortens every jump, biasing second moments in proportion to the squared jump amplitude and the jump kurtosis, and depleting the region near the barrier. We give the exact-flow integrator that replaces it.

</details>


### [188] [Diffusion models for the $O(3)$ nonlinear sigma model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39568)
*Daniel Schubring*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 在二维经典Heisenberg模型（O(3)非线性sigma模型的格点表示）上训练分数扩散模型，通过三种粗粒化实现多尺度采样，并提出Stein（Schwinger-Dyson）诊断评估学习到的分数函数质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将基于分数的扩散模型应用于格点场论/S^2流形上的采样，需要解决多尺度效率、学习分数函数质量的独立检验，以及球面几何（短时球面热核、CP^1表示）带来的实现困难。

Method: (1) 在同一训练集的三种粗粒化上训练神经网络，构建最细格点直接采样之外的多尺度采样方案；(2) 引入针对两点关联函数的Stein/Schwinger-Dyson诊断，不依赖逆向扩散采样即可评估分数质量，并将其作为额外训练损失；(3) 讨论S^2上扩散模型的实现细节，包括将网络输入提升至S^3（CP^1表示）以及短时球面热核的边界层近似以扩展其在大扩散时间下的适用性。

Result: 多尺度采样在最细格点上相比直接采样略微同时提升精度与计算成本；Stein诊断可独立于逆向扩散采样评估分数函数质量，并可作为训练损失使用；边界层近似扩大了短时球面热核的有效时间范围。

Conclusion: 分数扩散模型可用于S^2上的格点场论采样，多尺度方案与Stein诊断提供了效率与质量保证；所讨论的S^3提升与球面热核近似为在球面目标空间上实现扩散模型提供了可操作的技术路径。

Abstract: A score-based diffusion model is trained on the two-dimensional classical Heisenberg model, the lattice representation of the $O(3)$ nonlinear sigma model. Neural networks are trained on three different coarse-grainings of the same training set to implement a multiscale sampling scheme that modestly improves both the accuracy and computational cost of direct sampling on the finest lattice. Stein (or Schwinger-Dyson) diagnostics for the two-point correlation function are introduced to assess the quality of the learned score without relying on reverse diffusion sampling. These diagnostics are also incorporated into an additional training loss. We discuss several technical considerations relevant to implementing diffusion models on $S^2$. These include a consideration of lifting the network input to $S^3$, as in the $CP^1$ representation, and developing a boundary-layer approximation to the short-time spherical heat kernel that extends its usefulness to larger diffusion times.

</details>


### [189] [Fractal dimensions of paths on three-dimensional random-walk traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39612)
*Qiyuan Shi,Xinyi Li,Youjin Deng*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 在无限立方格上对N=L²步简单随机游走迹图中的四条起点—终点路径进行大规模蒙特卡洛模拟，测得各自长度随L的标度指数依次为 d_{S1}=1.48005(9) < d_{S2}=1.49868(5) < d_{S3}=1.51205(5) < d_LE=1.62400(5) < d_{De}=1.7099(7)，表明方向性与时间序约束会改变标度指数本身而非仅改变幅度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究随机游走迹图上不同规则定义的路径几何标度行为，厘清"无向最短路径""有向最短路径""时间相容最短路径"以及DFS树深度等路径在标度指数上的差异，为尚无解析理论的路径几何问题提供精确数值基准。

Method: 在无限立方格上生成N=L²步简单随机游走，以访问点与遍历键构成的迹图为对象，按四种规则选取从起点到终点的路径：无向最短路径S1、有向最短路径S2、时间相容最短路径S3，以及最终点在深度优先搜索树中的深度D_e。对L最大至32768进行蒙特卡洛模拟，拟合平均长度的标度指数，并分析标准差与长度的重标度分布塌缩。

Result: 平均长度按 L^{d} 标度，得 d_{S1}=1.48005(9)、d_{S2}=1.49868(5)、d_{S3}=1.51205(5)、d_{D_e}=1.7099(7)；满足 d_{S1}<d_{S2}<d_{S3}<d_LE<d_{D_e}（d_LE=1.62400(5)）。加入遍历方向性使最短路径指数上升，再施加时间序使其进一步上升。各路径的标准差与均值同指数标度（标度不变），不同于访问点质量的行为；重标度后的长度分布塌缩到宽度有限的标度函数上。

Conclusion: 方向性约束与时间序约束改变的是标度指数而非仅振幅；标准差具有标度不变性且长度分布呈现普适塌缩。目前无解析理论可预测这些指数，所给高精度数值可作为随机游走迹图路径几何后续研究的基准。

Abstract: We study four paths that join the starting site to the final position of a simple random walk of \(N=L^2\) steps on the infinite cubic lattice. The paths are selected on the walk trace---the graph of visited sites and traversed bonds---by different rules: the undirected shortest path \(S_1\), the directed shortest path \(S_2\), the time-respecting shortest path \(S_3\), and the depth \(D_e\) of the final site in a depth-first-search tree. Monte Carlo simulations for \(L\) up to 32768 give mean lengths scaling as \(L^{d_O}\), with \(d_{S_1}=1.48005(9)\), \(d_{S_2}=1.49868(5)\), \(d_{S_3}=1.51205(5)\), and \(d_{D_e}=1.7099(7)\), so that \(d_{S_1}<d_{S_2}<d_{S_3}<d_{\rm LE}<d_{D_e}\), where $d_{\rm LE}=1.62400(5)$ is the known loop-erased-random-walk exponent. Imposing traversal directions increases the shortest-path exponent, and imposing time order increases it further; these constraints therefore change the scaling exponent, not only the amplitude. For every path, the standard deviation scales with the same exponent as the mean, so it is scale-invariant, unlike the visited-site mass; the rescaled length distributions collapse onto a finite-width scaling function. No analytical theory currently predicts these exponents; our results provide precise numerical benchmarks for future studies of path geometry on random-walk traces.

</details>


### [190] [Fluctuation-dissipation relation in branching particle systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39624)
*Tom Michoel*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文为分支粒子系统建立了涨落-耗散关系(FDR)，揭示单克隆群体涨落同时编码群体生长与单粒子动力学的耗散结构；该关系可给出谱隙上界、解释克隆记忆与零协方差变量的克隆共涨落，并预测非平衡定态下的振荡型克隆涨落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克隆群体（源自单一祖先的增殖细胞群）的涨落中蕴含何种信息尚缺乏统一理论刻画；同时需要解释实验中观察到的克隆记忆持久性，以及单粒子定态下协方差为零的变量之间仍出现的克隆共涨落现象。

Method: 针对内部状态按平稳马尔可夫过程演化的粒子，推导分支粒子系统的涨落-耗散关系；该关系在平衡与非平衡定态下均成立，并借助单粒子谱性质分析克隆涨落的渐近行为。

Result: FDR 给出受克隆涨落幅度约束的谱隙上界；表明克隆动力学渐近地由单粒子动力学最慢模式主导；在非平衡定态下预测出现振荡型克隆涨落。

Conclusion: 克隆涨落可作为探针同时读取群体生长与单粒子耗散结构；为克隆记忆的持久性和零协方差变量的克隆共涨落提供理论解释；并提出在无单细胞追踪条件下测量增殖细胞群体非平衡通量的可能途径。

Abstract: A fluctuation-dissipation relation (FDR) for branching particle systems is derived that reveals how fluctuations in clonal populations originating from a single ancestor encode both population growth and the dissipative structure of the single-particle dynamics. For particles whose internal states evolve according to a stationary Markov process, the FDR holds in both equilibrium and nonequilibrium stationary states. It yields an upper bound on the spectral gap that is constrained by the magnitude of clonal fluctuations and implies that clonal dynamics is asymptotically dominated by the slowest modes of the single-particle dynamics. This provides a theoretical explanation for the persistence of clonal memory and for experimentally observed clonal cofluctuations between variables with zero covariance in the single-particle stationary state. In nonequilibrium stationary states, the FDR predicts oscillatory clonal fluctuations, suggesting a route to measuring nonequilibrium fluxes in proliferating cell populations without single-cell tracking.

</details>


### [191] [Two-dimensional binary Lennard-Jones mixtures with ECMC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39793)
*Kush Patel,Gabriele Tartero,Werner Krauth*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 论文讨论用于长程二维二元Lennard-Jones混合物的可逆与非可逆局部Monte Carlo算法，提出适配的cell-veto策略以高效处理长程相互作用，并将非可逆事件链Monte Carlo（ECMC）与因子化Metropolis算法进行基准比较，在简单测试中实现平衡态与非平衡动力学的数量级加速，并借助ECMC给出该混合物物理概览。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程相互作用下的二元Lennard-Jones混合物模拟计算代价高，传统可逆局部Monte Carlo算法效率受限，需探索能高效处理长程力并被体系加速的采样算法，同时系统理解此类混合物在不同热力学相与耗竭极限下的物理行为。

Method: 采用因子化Metropolis滤波结合适配的cell-veto策略处理长程与二元混合物相互作用；构建非可逆事件链Monte Carlo（ECMC）算法，并与因子化Metropolis算法在简单测试体系上进行基准对比；在不同热力学相及相应极限下分析算法表现。

Result: 在简单测试案例中，ECMC在平衡态与非平衡动力学方面均相较因子化Metropolis算法取得数个数量级的加速；并利用ECMC刻画了二元Lennard-Jones混合物从无耗竭（溶质-溶剂无相互作用）到耗竭极限（溶剂粒子数发散）的物理图景。

Conclusion: 因子化Metropolis滤波配合cell-veto可高效处理长程二元混合物，而非可逆ECMC在效率上显著优于可逆算法，是研究此类体系平衡与非平衡物理的有效工具，所获物理概览为耗竭效应等问题提供了系统认识。

Abstract: We discuss reversible and non-reversible local Monte Carlo algorithms for long-range two-dimensional binary Lennard-Jones mixtures. We illustrate how the factorized Metropolis filter efficiently handles long-range interactions even for binary mixtures, using an adapted cell-veto strategy. We then benchmark the non-reversible event-chain Monte Carlo (ECMC) algorithm against the factorized Metropolis algorithm. For simple test cases, we demonstrate speedups of several orders of magnitude in the equilibrium and the out-of-equilibrium dynamics. Using the ECMC algorithm, we provide an overview of the physics of binary Lennard-Jones mixtures, from the case without depletion (no interaction between solute and solvent) all the way to the depletion limit (with a diverging number of solvent particles). We analyze the algorithms in different thermodynamic phases and in the corresponding limits.

</details>


### [192] [High-temperature expansion of the inverse-square Ising chain in a magnetic field: exact coefficients and finite-size reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39815)
*M. A. Rajabpour*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文发展了一维逆平方 Ising 链自由能的高温展开，在每一阶保留均匀磁场的依赖，并给出可由有限环精确重构无穷链系数的代数结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆平方相互作用一维 Ising 链虽有 Thouless 转变，但其完整的场依赖热力学缺乏闭式结果；作者希望在高阶获得精确、非数值外推的场依赖展开。

Method: 对自由能做保留磁场的高温展开；证明耦合重标度后每阶系数约化场平方双曲正切的有理多项式；证明有限周期环的第 n 阶系数是 L^{-2} 的至多 n 次精确多项式，其常数项即无穷链系数；结合匹配行列式与圈-Laplacian 约化推导最高两个场幂次的精确公式。

Result: 给出至 26 阶的完整场依赖展开，共 26 个多项式、377 个有理系数条目；获得对任意阶成立的高幂次系数精确公式。

Conclusion: 揭示了逆平方链微观热力学中意外的代数结构，并为磁响应提供高阶精确信息。

Abstract: The one-dimensional Ising chain with inverse-square interactions is a paradigmatic long-range system: it exhibits the unusual Thouless transition, with a discontinuity in the spontaneous magnetization, yet its full field-dependent thermodynamics remains unknown in closed form. We develop the high-temperature expansion of its free energy while retaining the dependence on a uniform magnetic field at every order. We prove that, after a natural rescaling of the coupling, each coefficient is a rational polynomial in the squared hyperbolic tangent of the reduced field. Remarkably, at each order $n$ the field-dependent free-energy coefficient of a finite, periodically repeated ring is an exact polynomial of degree at most $n$ in $L^{-2}$. The infinite-chain coefficient is its constant term and can therefore be reconstructed from $n+1$ finite rings without numerical extrapolation. Using this reconstruction, we determine the full field-dependent expansion through order 26: 26 polynomials containing 377 rational coefficient entries. We further derive exact formulas, valid at every order, for the coefficients of the two highest powers of the field variable using matching determinants and a cycle-Laplacian reduction. These results reveal an unexpected algebraic structure in the microscopic thermodynamics of the inverse-square chain and provide high-order information about its magnetic response.

</details>


### [193] [Ensemble-dependent effect of disorder on multicritical points in a system with long-range interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39929)
*Nir Schreiber,Reuven Cohen*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究两个具有长程平均场型相互作用和淬火无序的 Blume–Emery–Griffiths 模型变体，二者自由能与平均能量相同但系综相图不等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨不同哈密顿量在给出相同自由能与微正则熵的前提下，正则与微正则系综相图及多临界行为是否一致，即系综不等价性的表现。

Method: 构造两个长程、平均场型、含淬火无序的 BEG 模型变体；分别计算自由能、平均能量与微正则熵，比较两系综下的相边界与多临界行为。

Result: 两变体自由能与平均能量相同，微正则熵亦相同；但正则与微正则相边界显著不同。微正则下任意活性自旋比例均出现三临界点，正则下仅当该比例足够大时才出现，较低比例时表现为正则临界端点；并观察到熵随磁化强度出现间隙。

Conclusion: 相同热力学量（自由能、平均能量、微正则熵）并不保证系综等价；相图与多临界行为可明显不同，熵间隙进一步体现系综不等价。

Abstract: We introduce two variants of the Blume--Emery--Griffiths model with long-range, mean-field-like interactions and quenched disorder. Although the two variants are described by different Hamiltonians, they yield the same free energy. Moreover, both Hamiltonians lead to the same average energy and hence to the same microcanonical entropy. The canonical and microcanonical phase boundaries differ markedly, and so does the multicritical behaviour of the two ensembles. In the microcanonical ensemble, tricritical points appear for any fraction of active spins, whereas in the canonical ensemble they emerge only when this fraction is sufficiently large. At lower fractions, canonical critical endpoints arise instead. We also report the formation of gaps in the entropy as a function of magnetization, another manifestation of ensemble inequivalence.

</details>


### [194] [Nonequilibrium statistical thermodynamics of a small quantum system coupled to quantum reservoirs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39973)
*Jeremy Schofield,Raymond Kapral*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出一种统计力学框架，将选定量子可观测量的非平衡平均值精确表示为局部密度算符的平均，用以描述小量子系统与热库耦合时的动力学与热力学，并推导子系统对库的边界条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要对耦合于热库的小量子系统的非平衡平均（如平均内能、熵产生）给出精确而非近似的统计热力学描述，并刻画子系统对大型量子库的反作用。

Method: 采用局部密度算符表述：局部密度算符是约束变量的泛函，约束变量充当热力学力并满足一组运动方程；据此建立量子子系统、孤立子系统及库的非平衡统计热力学，并导出库方程的边界条件。

Result: 得到量子子系统平均内能演化（含外部自主驱动做功）与熵产生的精确表述；当子系统耦合大型量子库时，子系统对库的影响可通过库方程的边界条件精确捕捉，且边界条件被显式推导。

Conclusion: 该框架为耦合热库的量子子系统提供了精确的非平衡统计热力学描述，并通过两不同温度热库间的量子热输运算例得到验证。

Abstract: A statistical mechanical formulation where nonequilibrium averages of selected quantum observables are given exactly as averages over local density operators is used to describe the dynamics and thermodynamics of a small quantum system coupled to reservoirs. The local density operators are functionals of constraint variables that play the role of thermodynamic forces that satisfy a set of equations of motion. A focus of the work is on the evolution of the average internal energy of the quantum subsystem that may be subject to external autonomous driving that does work on the system, and on entropy production in the system. The nonequilibrium statistical thermodynamics the quantum subsystem coupled to reservoirs, as well as that for the isolated subsystem and reservoirs, are discussed. When the subsystem is coupled to large quantum reservoirs, the effects of the quantum subsystem on the reservoirs can be captured through boundary conditions on the reservoir equations, and these boundary conditions are derived. The formulation is illustrated by considering the heat transfer through a system comprising a quantum system coupled to two heat reservoirs with different temperatures.

</details>


### [195] [Thermodynamic Crossovers in Supercritical Matter: One, No One, and One Hundred Thousand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39986)
*Yang Xu,Matteo Baggioli*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文证明超临界区中区分类液/类气区域的交叉边界线并非由普适临界行为唯一确定：满足相同条件（过临界区三分、仅在临界点相交、遵守临界标度律）的线有无限多对；Lee-Yang形式体系也无法消除该歧义；只有额外限定尊重Z2对称性的"规则探测路径"才能在渐近意义上回到Widom与L±方案，但即便此时仍存在两种不等价方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超临界流体中类液/类气区域的划分长期缺乏统一定义，已有多种判据（如Widom线、L±交叉线）被提出；近期L±线被引入以解决旧定义与Ising模型普适临界行为不相容的问题。作者希望检验：这些条件（三分超临界区、仅在临界点汇合、满足临界标度律）是否足以唯一确定交叉边界。

Method: 以热力学与临界标度分析为框架，构造性地证明存在无限多对满足L±全部条件的交叉线，且不同热力学路径选取对应不同边界；进一步考察Lee-Yang形式体系对临界奇点的内在刻画能否在实轴上给出无歧义的边界；再分析限定为尊重临界点Z2对称性的"规则探测路径"时的渐近行为。

Result: 证明L±所依赖的条件不唯一确定边界：无限多对线满足同样要求，边界随所选热力学路径变化；Lee-Yang形式体系虽能内在刻画临界奇点，但无法消除实轴交叉边界的歧义；在规则探测路径（保持Z2对称）限制下，Widom与L±方案仅在渐近意义上被恢复，且仍有两种不等价方案并存。

Conclusion: 仅凭普适临界行为不能选出特权性的超临界热力学交叉边界；此类交界线可能对应物理上有意义的热力学、结构或输运性质变化，但其具体位置及所划区域宽度依赖于所用操作判据，属于协议选择而非系统固有属性。

Abstract: Beyond a critical point, two thermodynamic phases become continuously connected, without being separated by any thermodynamic singularity. The paradigmatic example is the liquid-gas transition, where several criteria have been proposed to distinguish liquid-like and gas-like regimes within the supercritical region. Recently, a pair of thermodynamic crossover lines, $L^\pm$, was introduced to address a proposed incompatibility of previous definitions with the universal critical behavior of the Ising model. These lines divide the supercritical region into three regimes, meet only at the critical point, and obey the expected critical scaling laws. Here, we demonstrate that these requirements do not uniquely determine the crossover boundaries. Instead, infinitely many pairs of lines satisfy the same conditions, with different choices of thermodynamic paths selecting different crossover boundaries. We further show that the Lee-Yang formalism, despite providing an intrinsic characterization of critical singularities, does not resolve this ambiguity in defining real-axis crossover boundaries. Restricting to regular probing paths that respect the $Z_2$ symmetry of the critical point recovers the Widom and $L^\pm$ prescriptions asymptotically. This restriction, however, is a choice of protocol rather than a property of the system, and even then two inequivalent prescriptions survive. Our results demonstrate that universal critical behavior alone does not select privileged supercritical thermodynamic crossover boundaries. While such lines may identify physically meaningful changes in thermodynamic, structural, or transport properties, their precise locations and the widths of the regions they delimit remain dependent on the operational criteria used to define them.

</details>


### [196] [Non-Gaussian transport in tempered fractional Laplace motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40113)
*Yiming Zhao,Wei Wang,Ralf Metzler*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文构建了一个最小随机模型——经指数调节(tempering)的分数布朗噪声相关性叠加扩散系数的伽马分布，生成"调节分数拉普拉斯运动"，能定量重现活性货物粒子输运中的瞬态超扩散与非高斯位移分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活性输运实验中普遍观察到非高斯位移概率密度与瞬态超扩散现象，但缺乏一个同时刻画二者、且参数最简的随机模型框架。

Method: 在母代分数布朗噪声的相关函数中引入指数调节，再对系综上的扩散系数赋予伽马分布，据此构造调节分数拉普拉斯运动(tempered fractional Laplace motion)，解析推导均方位移、峰度、非高斯性参数及位移概率密度函数，并用随机模拟与实验数据对比。

Result: 获得位移各统计量的解析表达式；随机模拟表明该模型可定量复现变形虫细胞毯驱动活性货物粒子输运实验中的主要观测特征。

Conclusion: 指数调节与伽马型扩散系数分布的组合足以同时描述瞬态超扩散和非高斯位移统计，为活性输运提供简约而具备解析可处理性的建模方案。

Abstract: We study a minimal stochastic model for active transport featuring a non-Gaussian displacement probability density function and transient superdiffusion. Our approach is based on a combination of an exponential tempering in the parental fractional Brownian noise correlations, augmented by a gamma-distribution of the diffusivity across the ensemble. For the resulting tempered fractional Laplace motion we derive analytical expressions for the mean-squared displacement, the kurtosis, the non-Gaussianity parameter and the displacement probability density function. We also show via stochastic simulations that the model quantitatively reproduces the main experimental observations in a recent study of active cargo particle transport by a carpet of amoeba cells.

</details>


### [197] [Seeding and Amplification of Chaos in Long-Range Interacting Spin Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40417)
*Sareen Sabra,Jeffrey G. Rau*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 在无穷温度经典Heisenberg自旋链中，弱长程相互作用尾部虽几乎不影响常规自旋动力学可观测量，却能定性改变退关联（decorrelation）的长距离传播规律：指数尾部下前缘可为弹道型，幂律尾部下则呈对数到达时间（指数式扩张前缘）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 截断弱相互作用是多体哈密顿量的常见简化，但其有效性未必在距离与时间上一致。混沌系统中，微弱长程耦合可在远离源头处播下微小扰动，随后被局域动力学指数放大，因此需要检验截断近似的适用性并厘清长程尾部如何影响传播。

Method: 构建以最近邻交换为主导、附带弱相互作用尾部的无穷温度经典Heisenberg自旋链；借助经典decorrelator（退关联函数），在数个量级的相互作用强度范围内比较指数衰减尾部与幂律衰减尾部，并用“播撒—放大”图像解释结果。

Result: 指数衰减尾部：尾部足够平缓时远距离前缘保持弹道型，速度由种子空间衰减与局域混沌增长的竞争决定；衰减足够快时，在可及时空窗口内恢复由内禀蝴蝶速度v_b定义的最近邻前缘。幂律尾部：所有研究指数下到达时间与距离呈对数关系，对应指数扩张的空间前缘，幂律指数控制该对数的系数。

Conclusion: 长程耦合在全链产生弱扰动，其增长主要由局域最近邻动力学主导；尾部总体强度决定长程前缘何时显现，其空间衰减形式决定传播规律。故弱相互作用尾部可使常规自旋动力学可观测量基本不变，却定性改变退关联的长距离传播。

Abstract: Truncating weak interactions is a natural simplification of a many-body Hamiltonian, but the validity of this approximation need not be uniform in distance and time. In a chaotic system, a weak long-range coupling can seed a small disturbance far from its source, after which the local dynamics can amplify that disturbance exponentially. We study this mechanism in an infinite-temperature classical Heisenberg spin chain with a dominant nearest-neighbor exchange and a weak interaction tail. Using a classical decorrelator, we compare exponentially and power-law decaying tails over several decades in interaction strength. For exponentially decaying interactions, the long-distance front remains ballistic when the tail is sufficiently shallow, with a velocity controlled by the competition between the spatial decay of the seed and the local chaotic growth. For sufficiently rapid exponential decay, the nearest-neighbor front, which is defined by an intrinsic butterfly velocity $v_b$, is recovered within the accessible space-time window. For power-law interactions, the arrival time is logarithmic in distance for all exponents studied, corresponding to an exponentially expanding spatial front; the power-law exponent controls the coefficient of this logarithm. We interpret both behaviors using a seeding-and-amplification picture in which long-range couplings generate weak disturbances throughout the chain whose growth is governed primarily by the local nearest-neighbor dynamics. In this picture, the overall strength of the tail primarily controls when the long-range front becomes visible, while its spatial decay controls the propagation law. Thus a weak interaction tail can leave conventional spin-dynamical observables essentially unchanged while qualitatively altering the long-distance propagation of decorrelation.

</details>


### [198] [Teleported free energy simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40428)
*Davide Carbone*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR:  escorted free energy simulation建立于沿平衡分布的连续流场之上，其固有局限是连续流无法在亚稳态盆之间转移概率。本文证明任意escort都存在一个由盆权重变化决定的方差下限，并通过以正比于驱动速率的速率引入"teleportation"（跳跃）来突破该下限；同时导出相应的非平衡恒等式、零耗散条件与所需最少跳跃数，并在精确可解模型与倾斜双势阱上展示了增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: escorted free energy simulation通过构造跟随平衡分布的流场来降低耗散，但其核心假设——连续流——在原理上无法实现亚稳态盆之间的概率转移。这一结构性缺陷意味着存在无法通过优化连续流消除的方差下限，作者希望定量刻画此下限并给出突破手段。

Method: 以盆权重（basin weights）为分析对象，证明escort的方差下限由盆权重变化量决定；在连续流之上叠加"teleportation"式跳跃（速率与驱动速率成正比），据此推导对应的非平衡恒等式、零耗散条件以及达成零耗散所需的最少跳跃次数。

Result: 得到escort的方差地板（variance floor）的严格表述，并证明teleportation可将其消除；给出非平衡恒等式、零耗散条件与最小跳跃数；在精确可解模型与倾斜双势阱（tilted double well）两个算例上验证了方差与耗散的降低。

Conclusion: 连续escort流在原理上受限于亚稳态盆间不可转移概率，因而存在不可消除的方差下限；引入按驱动速率比例发生的跳跃即可越过该下限并满足零耗散条件，所需跳跃数存在明确的最小值。该框架为亚稳态体系的低耗散自由能估计提供了可操作的理论准则。

Abstract: Escorted free energy simulations reduce dissipation with a flow field that follows the equilibrium distribution. A continuous flow, however, cannot transfer probability between metastable basins. We show that any escort then faces a variance floor set by the change in basin weights, and lift it by adding teleportation: jumps at a rate proportional to the driving rate. We derive the corresponding nonequilibrium identity, the zero-dissipation condition, and the minimal number of jumps it requires, and illustrate the gain on an exactly solvable model and a tilted double well.

</details>


### [199] [Renormalization for Robust Memory without Synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40651)
*Shashwat Chakraborty,Jong Yeon Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出时空重整化框架，证明异步更新下由竞争性局部规则维持的纠错码（异步R压缩码）具有稳定性，并构造二维、三维鲁棒过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部纠错可在同步更新下保护经典信息，但当各格点以独立随机时刻演化（异步更新）时，保护是否仍成立并不清楚；尤其当保护依赖相互竞争的局部规则的集体作用时，稳定性分析尤为困难。

Method: 建立时空重整化框架，刻画独立作用的局部更新如何在集体层面稳定记忆；给出充分条件：稳定界面的几何结构与缺陷传播的抑制共同导致大尺度下可靠的“侵蚀”（erosion）。

Result: 利用该框架证明了异步R压缩码的稳定性，并在二维与三维中构造出鲁棒过程；在足够弱的噪声下（即使噪声偏向某一相），两种编码相均能持续存在。

Conclusion: 为设计由竞争性局部随机动力学自主维持的记忆提供了判据。

Abstract: Local error correction can preserve classical information against noise, but protection under synchronized updates need not survive when sites evolve at independent random times. Establishing stability is particularly challenging when protection relies on the collective action of competing local rules. Here we develop a spacetime renormalization framework that reveals how independently acting local updates can collectively stabilize memory. We identify sufficient conditions under which the geometry of stable interfaces and suppression of defect propagation produce reliable erosion at large scales. Applying this framework, we prove stability of the asynchronous \textsf{R} squeezing code and introduce robust processes in two and three dimensions. In these systems, both encoded phases persist under sufficiently weak noise, even when the noise favors one phase. Our results provide a criterion for designing autonomous memories sustained by competing local stochastic dynamics.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [200] [Meta-Altermagnetism and Enhanced Spin-Splitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37754)
*Bogeng Wen,Hae-Young Kee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出"元交替磁性（meta-altermagnetism）"概念，证明二维体系中磁序诱导的能带重构可使自旋劈裂范霍夫奇点穿越费米面，从而产生一级交替磁相变，并在中间交替磁区获得增强的磁八极矩与自旋劈裂电导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替磁体具有零净磁化但自旋劈裂能带，然而是否存在类似一级相变的不连续跳变（元交替磁性）尚不清楚，且如何利用该机制增强自旋劈裂效应以服务自旋电子学应用是核心动机。

Method: 在双子晶格方格子上构建含各向异性跃迁积分的扩展 t-J 模型，采用平均场理论计算，通过调节化学势与各向异性跃迁强度分析相图与相变性质。

Result: 发现磁序诱导的能带重构驱动自旋劈裂 vHS 穿越费米面时出现一级元交替磁相变；该相变产生一个中间交替磁区，其交错磁化减弱但磁八极矩与时间反演奇的自旋劈裂电导显著增强；一级相变线终止于量子临界端点，形成丰富的相图。

Conclusion: 自旋劈裂范霍夫奇点是实现元交替磁性与增强自旋劈裂效应的有效途径，为自旋电子学应用提供了新机制。

Abstract: Altermagnets are collinear magnets with zero net magnetization and spin-split electronic bands. Here, we explore whether altermagnetism can exhibit a discontinuous jump, a phenomenon we refer to as meta-altermagnetism. We show that meta-altermagnetism can emerge in 2D systems when magnetic-order-induced band reconstruction drives spin-split van Hove singularities (vHSs) across the Fermi level. Using mean-field theory for an extended $t$-$J$ model on a two-sublattice square lattice including anisotropic hopping integral, we find that a first-order meta-altermagnetic transition produces an intermediate altermagnetic regime with reduced staggered magnetization but strongly enhanced magnetic octupole and time-reversal-odd spin-splitter conductivity. We further show that the first-order transition line terminates at a quantum critical endpoint, giving rise to a rich phase diagram as the chemical potential and anisotropic hopping strength are varied. Our results identify the spin split vHSs as a route to enhancing spin-splitter effect for spintronic applications.

</details>


### [201] [Field theory of competing phases in the interacting Kitaev chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37761)
*Pradip Kattel,Thierry Giamarchi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 结合玻色化与DMRG研究有限化学势下近邻相互作用Kitaev链，建立含掺杂的连续场论，给出由非公度Luttinger液体分隔拓扑超导相与公度CDW/Mott相的相图，并构造描述拓扑体的对偶连续费米子表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究多集中于可精确求解的格点极限，缺乏对远离这些极限、有限密度下掺杂非微扰改变超导配对与公度电荷序竞争的普适连续场论描述，以及一维相互作用拓扑超导体的解析刻画。

Method: 相互作用Kitaev链（近邻相互作用+有限化学势）；玻色化与DMRG数值相结合；构建有限密度一般区域的连续场论；半经典分析及自由费米子边界理论分析边缘Majorana模；构造对偶连续费米子表示。

Result: 相图包含拓扑超导相与公度CDW/Mott相之间的中间非公度Luttinger液体，以及大化学势下的平庸带绝缘区；公度–非公度边界由Pokrovsky–Talapov临界性控制，非公度–拓扑超导边界由BKT临界性控制；拓扑相在拓扑/平庸界面支持指数局域Majorana边缘模；对偶表示将拓扑体能区描述为常规对称破缺。

Conclusion: 给出了一维相互作用拓扑超导体的解析描述：从拓扑与公度性竞争、相应量子临界性到拓扑相的对偶对称破缺描述，并给出不同费米子表示间SPT–SSB对应的连续版本。

Abstract: We study the interacting Kitaev chain with nearest-neighbor interactions and finite chemical potential using a combination of bosonization and density-matrix renormalization-group calculations. Focusing away from special exactly solvable lattice limits, we develop a continuum field-theoretic description of the generic finite-density interacting regime, where doping nonperturbatively modifies the competition between superconducting pairing and commensurate charge ordering. We show that the phase diagram consists of an intermediate incommensurate Luttinger liquid between the topological superconducting and commensurate charge-density-wave Mott phases, as well as a trivial band-insulating regime at large chemical potential. The commensurate--incommensurate and incommensurate--topological-superconducting phase boundaries are governed by Pokrovsky--Talapov and Berezinskii--Kosterlitz--Thouless criticality, respectively. We analyze the interacting topological superconducting regime and its boundary excitations. At the semiclassical level, and explicitly in the free-fermion boundary theory, the topological phase supports exponentially localized Majorana edge modes at interfaces between topological and trivial regions. We also construct a dual continuum fermionic representation in which this topological bulk sector is described instead by conventional symmetry breaking. This gives a continuum version of the SPT--SSB correspondence between different fermionic representations. Overall, our results provide an analytical description of one-dimensional interacting topological superconductors, from the competition between topology and commensurability and the resulting quantum criticality to the dual symmetry-breaking description of the topological phase.

</details>


### [202] [Long-Range Toric Code: Finite-Temperature Topological Order in Two Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37765)
*Jacopo Gliozzi,Taylor L. Hughes,Kyung-Su Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出带有长程环相互作用（long-range loop interactions）的二维 toric code 模型：在保持原有拓扑序基态的同时于零温禁闭任意子（anyon）；当相互作用足够长程时，禁闭可延伸至有限温度，从而实现有限温度拓扑序、可作为自纠错量子存储并具有长程纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普通二维 toric code 在有限温度下任意子会自由增殖，导致拓扑序被破坏，无法作为自纠错量子存储。作者希望通过对原有模型添加长程环相互作用来抑制任意子激发，从而在有限温度下稳定拓扑序。

Method: 在二维 toric code 上引入额外的长程环相互作用，构造新模型；论证该相互作用不改变零温下的拓扑序基态，但在零温下禁闭任意子，并进一步分析相互作用程长对有限温度行为的影响（区分足够长程与较短程两种情形）。

Result: 1）零温下任意子被禁闭，而拓扑序基态保持不变；2）相互作用足够长程时，禁闭在有限温度下持续，任意子迁移率被强烈限制，产生有限温度拓扑序，表现为自纠错量子存储并具有长程纠缠；3）相互作用较短程时，有限温度下拓扑序特征仅能维持到指数大的长度尺度，形成部分自纠错量子存储。

Conclusion: 长程环相互作用是稳定二维有限温度拓扑序的有效机制，其自纠错能力由相互作用程长调控：足够长程可实现完全自纠错，较短程则仅能获得指数尺度上的部分自纠错。这为设计有限温度自纠错量子存储提供了新思路。

Abstract: We introduce two-dimensional toric code models with additional long-range loop interactions. Our models preserve the topologically ordered ground states of the ordinary toric code, while confining anyons at zero temperature. For sufficiently long-ranged interactions, we argue that this confinement persists at finite temperatures, strongly constraining anyon mobility and giving rise to finite-temperature topological order. This topological phase acts as a self-correcting quantum memory and exhibits long-range entanglement. For shorter-ranged interactions, signatures of topological order survive only up to an exponentially large length scale at finite temperature, yielding a partially self-correcting quantum memory.

</details>


### [203] [Spin Space Symmetry Without Spin Space Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37770)
*Jeffrey G. Rau*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文提出用自旋空间群对应的"空间群在自旋空间中的实表示"直接处理对称性约束，将磁性补偿、压磁、输运、Edelstein效应、动量空间自旋纹理等响应判定转化为空间群表示论问题，并归约为定义表示的三次外幂与点群多极表示的重叠条件，从而在点群分析之前提供波矢层面的选择定则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋空间群是相对于自旋群扩展、在非相对论极限下对磁结构进行完整对称性分类的工具，但构造和识别具体群本身繁琐。作者希望绕过显式构造群，直接以"父空间群在自旋空间中的实表示"为工作对象，简化并统一导出允许响应的对称性约束。

Method: 对给定父空间群，用其自旋空间实表示（模去纯自旋对称性）标记自旋空间群；在此语言下把各类物理响应的存在性判据表达为表示论条件，最终化为定义表示的外幂（∧¹、∧²、∧³）与点群多极表示的重叠；并利用外幂的波矢内容给出序波矢求和选择定则。以共线、共面、非共面磁序的多种材料为例进行验证。

Result: 多种物理上不同的响应归约为定义表示上的同一条件：p波自旋纹理与时间偶Edelstein效应条件相同；补偿序中的压磁与d波自旋纹理条件相同。外幂的波矢内容进一步给出层级化选择定则——均匀响应要求1、2或3个序波矢之和为倒格矢（依相关外幂而定），可在点群对称性之前排除大量响应。

Conclusion: 以实表示为核心的工作框架避免了自旋空间群的显式构造与识别，使磁性响应的对称性约束系统化、可计算化；波矢层面的选择定则具有强筛选力。作者还讨论了向磁空间群的推广、与共线序Landau理论的联系以及奇宇称磁体的刻画。

Abstract: Spin space groups provide a complete symmetry classification of magnetic structures in the non-relativistic limit and have become an increasingly useful language for organizing magnetic phases and their physical properties. Each spin space group with a given parent space group is specified, up to spin-only symmetries, by a real representation of that space group acting in spin space. We argue that working directly with this representation not only avoids the construction and identification of the group, but also organizes and simplifies the resulting symmetry constraints on allowed responses. Determining the presence or absence of magnetic compensation, piezomagnetism, spin and charge transport, the Edelstein effect, and momentum-space spin textures becomes an ordinary problem in the representation theory of space groups. The resulting conditions reduce to overlaps between the three exterior powers of the defining representation and multipole representations of the point group. Several physically distinct responses then reduce to the same condition on the defining representation. For example, a $p$-wave spin texture and the time-even Edelstein effect are allowed under the same condition, as are piezomagnetism and a $d$-wave spin texture in compensated order. The wave-vector content of the exterior powers gives a further hierarchy of selection rules: depending on the relevant power, a uniform response requires one, two, or three ordering wave vectors to sum to a reciprocal lattice vector. These rules can exclude many responses before point-group symmetry is considered. We illustrate the approach with examples drawn from a wide variety of materials hosting collinear, coplanar and non-coplanar magnetic order. Finally, we discuss extensions of these results to magnetic space groups, connections to Landau theories of collinear order, and the characterization of odd-parity magnets.

</details>


### [204] [Evidence for a charged vortex lattice in an exciton superfluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37781)
*Ryan L. Lee,Jungwoo Lee,Viliam Vaňo,Bo Zou,Cheng-Li Chiu,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Allan MacDonald,Ali Yazdani*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在双层石墨烯激子凝聚体中，用扫描隧道显微镜成像带电涡旋，首次观测到预言中的涡旋-反涡旋晶格，其周期随总过剩电荷缩放并与理论定量符合，为激子超流相位相干性提供证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涡旋晶格是旋转超流与第二类超导体的标志性特征，但激子由中性电子-空穴对构成，难以实现类似晶格；理论预言强磁场下弱带电激子超流可通过分数电荷涡旋核间的相互排斥稳定形成涡旋-反涡旋晶格，此前缺乏实验验证。

Method: 构建双层石墨烯激子凝聚体：两层石墨烯之间由薄六方氮化硼势垒隔开，置于垂直磁场中，凝聚体形成于两层朗道能级之间；利用扫描隧道显微镜对体系中的带电涡旋进行实空间成像。

Result: 观测到与理论预言一致的涡旋晶格；晶格周期随总过剩电荷变化而缩放，且对其在两层间的分布不敏感，与理论预测定量吻合。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持激子超流中涡旋-反涡旋晶格的存在，并为该激子超流体系的相位相干性提供了证据。

Abstract: Lattices of quantized vortices are a defining property of rotating superfluids and of type-II superconductors in magnetic fields. Realizing an analogous vortex lattice in an exciton superfluid-formed from bound electron-hole pairs-has remained challenging because excitons are electrically neutral. Building on previous theoretical studies of the low-energy excitations in an exciton superfluid, recent theory has predicted that in a strong magnetic field, a weakly charged exciton superfluid can stabilize a vortex-antivortex lattice arising from the mutual repulsion of fractionally charged vortex cores. Here we report evidence for this predicted vortex lattice in a bilayer exciton condensate by imaging its charged vortices with the scanning tunnelling microscope. The condensate forms between Landau levels of two graphene layers separated by a thin hexagonal boron nitride barrier in a perpendicular magnetic field. We find that the vortex lattice periodicity scales with the total excess charge and is insensitive to its distribution between the two layers, in quantitative agreement with theoretical predictions. Our observations provide evidence for phase coherence in an exciton superfluid system.

</details>


### [205] [From a Lifshitz Transition to Spin-Density-Wave Order: Sr$_2$RuO$_4$ under Uniaxial Strain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38025)
*Evgeny A. Stepanov,Sophie Beck,Maria Chatzieleftheriou,Antoine Georges*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在先进多体理论框架下，单轴应变通过各向异性Lifshitz转变增强Sr₂RuO₄超导临界温度并诱发磁有序，磁响应由γ片内与α-β片间两个竞争嵌套波矢主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验表明单轴应变使Sr₂RuO₄超导临界温度显著提升，并伴随涌现的磁有序迹象，但其微观机制（应变如何同时影响谱性质、散射与磁性）尚缺乏统一的理论解释。

Method: 采用先进的多体理论框架，研究单轴应变对Sr₂RuO₄谱学性质与磁性质的影响，重点分析应变诱导的费米面变化（Lifshitz转变）、各向异性散射率与相干费米液体行为，并考察费米面不同片层间的嵌套波矢与磁性不稳定性。

Result: 应变诱导各向异性Lifshitz转变：沿伸长方向的电子散射率增大，而垂直方向趋于相干费米液体行为，与实验吻合；计入该各向异性后可捕捉大应变下有限温磁有序不稳定性。磁响应由两个竞争波矢主导——一个来自γ片内嵌套，另一个来自α与β片之间的嵌套；两者均可稳定磁序，其竞争敏感依赖于相应费米面片层的相干性。

Conclusion: 单轴应变下Sr₂RuO₄的超导增强与磁有序可由应变诱导的各向异性Lifshitz转变及费米面片层相干性的差异统一描述，磁性不稳定性源于两个相互竞争的嵌套波矢。

Abstract: In recent years, the effects of uniaxial strain on Sr$_2$RuO$_4$ have been extensively investigated, revealing a pronounced enhancement of the superconducting critical temperature accompanied by experimental indications of an emergent magnetically ordered state. In this Letter, we study the impact of strain on the spectral and magnetic properties of this material within an advanced many-body theoretical framework. We find that strain induces an anisotropic Lifshitz transition, which enhances the electron scattering rate along the elongation axis while simultaneously promoting coherent Fermi-liquid behavior in the perpendicular direction, in close agreement with experimental observations. Taking into account this anisotropic behavior allows us to capture the magnetic instability leading to a finite-temperature ordered state at large strain values. Our analysis further reveals that the magnetic response is dominated by two competing wave vectors associated with distinct nesting vectors: one within the $γ$ sheet and the other between the $α$ and $β$ sheets of the Fermi surface. Both wave vectors can stabilize magnetic order, with their competition depending sensitively on the coherence of the respective Fermi surface sheets.

</details>


### [206] [Level ordering in the linear $E \otimes e$ dynamic Jahn-Teller model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38190)
*Naoya Iwahara*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文严格证明线性 $E\otimes e$ 动态 Jahn-Teller 模型在任意耦合强度下，对每个能级指标 $n$，固定 $j$ 子空间中的第 $n$ 个能级 $E_{nj}$ 随 $|j|$ 严格递增，故基态恒为 $j=\pm\tfrac12$ 双重态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性 $E\otimes e$ 动态 Jahn-Teller 模型的基态长期被认为（仅凭经验/数值）是振动角动量 $j=\pm\tfrac12$ 的双重态，但缺乏对任意耦合强度的严格证明；本文旨在填补这一严格性缺口，并给出能隙的定量下界。

Method: 构造一个将振动角动量 $j$ 降低 1 的幺正变换，结合变分原理，证明对任意 $n$ 有 $E_{n,j}$ 关于 $|j|$ 严格单调递增；并由此导出基态能隙 $E_{0,j+1}-E_{0j}$ 的显式下界。

Result: 证明了对任意耦合强度与任意 $n$，有 $E_{n,1/2}<E_{n,3/2}<E_{n,5/2}<\cdots$；因此基态始终是 $j=\pm\tfrac12$ 双重态。同时给出能隙 $E_{0,j+1}-E_{0j}$ 的显式下界。

Conclusion: 线性 $E\otimes e$ Jahn-Teller 模型基态为 $j=\pm\tfrac12$ 双重态这一长期假设被严格证实，且适用于所有耦合强度；所给能隙下界为进一步的定量分析提供了依据。

Abstract: The ground level of the linear $E\otimes e$ dynamic Jahn-Teller model has long been believed to be the doublet with vibronic angular momentum $j = \pm\tfrac12$. We prove that, for arbitrary coupling strength, the $n$th level $E_{nj}$ in the subspace of fixed $j$ strictly increases with $|j|$ for every $n$, $E_{n,1/2} < E_{n,3/2} < E_{n,5/2} < \cdots$; in particular, the ground level is always the $j = \pm\tfrac12$ doublet. The proof uses only a unitary transformation that lowers $j$ by one, combined with the variational principle. We also give an explicit lower bound on the gap $E_{0,j+1} - E_{0j}$.

</details>


### [207] [Gravitational anomaly spectroscopy of a Kitaev spin liquid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38704)
*Kumar Ghosh*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 论文提出手征中心荷 \(c\) 是边缘的谱学量：通过对边缘耦合施加沿线传播的弱调制，吸收谱权重满足 \(D_n=2n\langle L_0\rangle+\frac{c}{12}(n^3-n)\)，从而 \(\mathcal{C}\equiv 2D_2-4D_1=c\)，与态（热或非热）无关。该方案在 Kitaev 蜂窝自旋液体上给出 \(\mathcal{C}=0.49999\)、\(h_\sigma=0.06250\)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手征中心荷 \(c\) 度量二维拓扑相的重力反常并决定边缘热流，但既有测量手段受限：热输运受声子干扰，波函数探针需要纯基态。因此需要一种不依赖纯态、不受声子污染的谱学测量方法。

Method: 对边缘耦合施加沿边缘传播的弱调制，依据 Virasoro 代数分析吸收谱权重，并用对同一耦合静态变化的响应进行归一化；提出组合量 \(\mathcal{C}=2D_2-4D_1\) 直接提取 \(c\)。进一步利用体中有无 vison 的线权重比 \(\Lambda=4h_\sigma/c\) 无标定地确定非阿贝尔 Ising 任意子的共形自旋。数值上采用精确对角化/大规模计算（7168、96 自旋）、DMRG 处理固定磁通扇区的相互作用边缘 Majorana，以及在量子处理器上精确模拟含原生 Floquet 线路的协议。

Result: Kitaev 蜂窝自旋液体在 7168 自旋上得 \(\mathcal{C}=0.49999\)、\(h_\sigma=0.06250\)；用实测谱线自身移动作标定，96 自旋上 \(c\) 误差约 1\%，在 20\% 随机耦合误差下仍保持 1\% 以内；固定磁通扇区的相互作用边缘 Majorana（DMRG）中 \(\mathcal{C}\) 偏差在 1.5\% 内，而边缘速度变化达 \(\pm13\%\)；含原生 Floquet 线路的量子处理器协议精确模拟在 96–600 自旋上给出 \(c\) 误差 3.5\% 以内。

Conclusion: 中心荷虽从激发能隙中抵消，却显现在跃迁权重中，可作为边缘的谱学可观测量被稳健提取；该框架对相互作用、耦合无序、有限尺寸及量子线路实现均具鲁棒性，并可直接给出非阿贝尔任意子的共形自旋。
