Abstract: The chiral central charge $c$ measures the gravitational anomaly of a two-dimensional topological phase. It fixes the heat current carried along the edge. So far, it has been accessed through thermal transport, where phonons complicate the signal, or through wave-function probes that need a pure ground state. Here we show that $c$ is a spectroscopic quantity of the edge. A weak modulation of the edge couplings that travel along the edge is absorbed according to the Virasoro algebra. Normalized by the response to a static change of the same couplings, the absorbed weights obey $D_n=2n\langle L_0\rangle+\frac{c}{12}(n^3-n)$, so that $\mathcal{C}\equiv2D_2-4D_1=c$ in any state of the ideal edge theory, thermal or not. The central charge thus appears in transition weights, although it cancels from excitation gaps. The ratio of line weights with and without a vison in the bulk, $Λ=4h_σ/c$, needs no calibration and fixes the conformal spin $h_σ$ of the non-Abelian Ising anyon. For the Kitaev honeycomb spin liquid we find $\mathcal{C}=0.49999$ and $h_σ=0.06250$ on 7168 spins. Calibrating with the shift of the measured line itself gives $c$ within about $1\%$ on 96 spins and within $1\%$ under $20\%$ random coupling errors. Interacting edge Majoranas in a fixed flux sector, treated with DMRG, keep $\mathcal{C}$ within $1.5\%$ while the edge velocity changes by $\pm13\%$. An exact simulation of a quantum-processor protocol, including the native Floquet circuit, returns $c$ within $3.5\%$ on 96 to 600 spins.

</details>


### [208] [Mixed-State Topological Transition and Emergent Fractionalized Supersymmetry in the Non-Abelian Kitaev Spin Liquid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38741)
*Zhou-Quan Wan,Hong Yao,Shao-Kai Jian*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究非阿贝尔 Kitaev 自旋液体在键退相位通道驱动下的混合态拓扑相变，并用无符号问题的蒙特卡洛方法揭示了退相位诱导的任意子凝聚、混合态拓扑序以及涌现 \mathcal N=1 超对称临界点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非阿贝尔自旋液体在耗散/退相干环境下的拓扑命运是开放量子多体物理的关键问题，但混合态拓扑序的判别与数值模拟长期缺乏可行框架；同时，拓扑-平凡时间界面上无隙 Majorana 模式在退相干下的临界行为尚不清楚。

Method: 将键退相位通道在向量化（Choi 态）图像中映射为两个时间反演共轭的分数化 Majorana 系统经 gapped Z2 规范场耦合，构造无符号问题（sign-problem-free）的蒙特卡洛模拟；结合任意子凝聚的图像分析与时间界面上 Chern 数失配的分析，并测量临界指数。

Result: 退相位增强驱动跨副本费米子对凝聚，使 Choi 态中 Ising⊠Isinḡ 转变为 toric-code 序，展开后得到内禀混合态拓扑序；在拓扑-平凡时间界面上，Chern 数失配强制存在无隙 Majorana 模，退相位驱动其进入涌现 \mathcal N=1 超对称临界点，模拟给出 ν=1.27(3)、η_b≈η_f≈0.40，与精确超对称值 5/4 和 2/5 吻合。超对称伙伴粒子是分数化的，表现为可区分的规范不变自旋弦。

Conclusion: 键退相位可将非阿贝尔 Kitaev 自旋液体转化为内禀混合态拓扑序，并在时间界面处实现涌现 \mathcal N=1 超对称的混合态拓扑相变；规范不变自旋弦为实验探测超对称伙伴提供了具体可观测量。

Abstract: We study a mixed-state topological transition in the non-Abelian Kitaev spin liquid driven by a bond-dephasing channel. In the vectorized picture, bond dephasing maps onto interactions between two time-reversed fractionalized Majorana systems coupled to gapped $\mathbb Z_2$ gauge fields, enabling sign-problem-free Monte Carlo simulations. We show that increasing dephasing condenses an inter-copy fermion pair, converting ${\rm Ising}\boxtimes\overline{\rm{Ising}}$ to toric-code order in the Choi state and yielding intrinsically mixed-state topological order upon unfolding. More interestingly, at a temporal interface between topological and trivial Kitaev states, a Chern-number mismatch enforces a gapless Majorana mode. Dephasing then drives a mixed-state topological transition at which the gapless mode develops emergent $\mathcal N=1$ supersymmetry. Our Monte Carlo simulations yield $ν=1.27(3)$ and $η_b\simeqη_f\simeq0.40$, consistent with the exact supersymmetric values $5/4$ and $2/5$. The supersymmetric partners are fractionalized and appear physically as distinct gauge-invariant spin strings, providing concrete observables for future experiments.

</details>


### [209] [Magnetism in a homometallic square-planar Cu$({\mathrm{II})}$$_4$Cu$({\mathrm{II})}$ metallacrown](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38989)
*Luca Bischof,Ariyan Tavakoli,Sascha Pietruska,Lena Spillecke,Ina Heckelmann,Changhyun Koo,Luca M. Carrella,Eva Rentschler,Hans Christian Schneider,Rüdiger Klingeler*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过引入位阻取代基抑制分子间相互作用，实验与DFT结合解析Cu₄Cu金属冠的交换耦合、S=1/2基态及g因子与激发态能隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典同金属金属冠Cu(II)[12-MC_Cu(II)N(Shi)-4]²⁻是研究交换耦合Cu(II)团簇磁性的理想平台，但分子间磁相互作用会干扰对其本征基态自旋结构与交换作用的精细研究，因此需要一种能有效抑制分子间耦合的衍生物。

Method: 在水杨羟肟酸配体中引入位阻庞大的取代基，得到改性衍生物Cu(II)[12-MC_Cu(II)N(L)-4]²⁻（Cu₄Cu）；采用静态磁化测量提取交换耦合常数，并以基于破缺对称性的从头算DFT进行佐证；结合X波段及高频/高场EPR确定g因子与基态自旋构型，并观测热致跃迁与激发态EPR信号。

Result: 静态磁化给出竞争性反铁磁交换：中心—外周位点间J₁=−8.1(2) THz（−33.5(8) meV），环上相邻位点间J₂=−4.40(12) THz（−18.2(5) meV），与DFT结果一致；低温下基态为S=1/2，EPR定出g_x=2.028、g_y=2.056、g_z=2.222（近轴向各向异性），与DFT相符；基态自旋构型由未占据态的d_xy特征表征；磁化数据显示双重态—四重态热跃迁能隙Δ_{1/2→3/2}=4.8(4) THz，并有激发态EPR信号佐证。

Conclusion: 位阻改性成功隔离了Cu₄Cu团簇的本征磁性，确立其为具有S=1/2基态、近轴向各向异性g因子与明确双重态—四重态能隙的交换耦合Cu(II)体系，为金属冠磁性的实验—从头算联合研究提供了可靠范例。

Abstract: The well-established homometallic metallacrown Cu(II)[12-MC$_\text{Cu(II)N(Shi)}$-4]$^{2-}$, comprising four square-planarly arranged Cu(II) ions surrounding a slightly off-plane central Cu(II) ion, provides an excellent platform for investigating magnetism in exchange-coupled Cu(II) clusters both by experimental and ab initio numerical methods. In the present work, sterically bulky substituents are introduced into the salicylhydroxamate ligand to obtain a modified derivative, Cu$({\mathrm{II})}$[12-MC$_\text{Cu$({\mathrm{II})}$N(L)}$-4]$^{2-}$ (Cu$_4$Cu), thereby effectively suppressing intermolecular magnetic interactions and enabling a detailed investigation of its intrinsic ground-state spin structure and exchange interactions. Static magnetization reveals competing antiferromagnetic exchange interactions of $J_1=-8.1(2)$ THz ($-33.5(8)$ meV) between the central and the peripheral sites and $J_2=-4.40(12)$ THz ($-18.2(5)$ meV) between neighboring sites on the ring of the metallacrown, which are corroborated by ab initio broken symmetry-based density functional theory (DFT). These couplings yield an $S=1/2$ ground state for Cu$_4$Cu at low temperatures, for which X-band and high-frequency/high-field electron paramagnetic resonance (EPR) precisely determine the $g$-factors $g_x = 2.028$, $g_y = 2.056$, and $g_z = 2.222$ close to axial anisotropy and in agreement with our DFT results. The ground-state spin-configuration is characterized by $d_{xy}$-character of the unoccupied states. Furthermore, our magnetization data evidence thermal transitions across a doublet-to-quartet gap of $Δ_{1/2\rightarrow3/2} = 4.8(4)$ THz which is also indicated by the emergence of an excited-state EPR signal.

</details>


### [210] [Atomic Charge Chirality from Electric Multipoles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39150)
*Satoru Hayami,Rikuto Oiwa*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出用电多极矩 $Q_{lm}$ 作为局域原子电荷分布手性的判据，给出11个手性点群区分对映体所需的最低秩多极成分，并指出无自旋 $s$-$p$-$d$-$f$ 基组（秩$\le 6$）无法编码 $C_6$、$D_6$、$O$ 的手性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局域原子电荷分布如何"携带"晶体学手性缺乏一个明确的微观判据：多极展开成分与点群手性之间的对应关系（哪一秩、哪一成分决定手性）尚未被系统建立，且原子轨道基组的有限秩可能从根本上限制手性信息的可编码性。

Method: 以电多极矩 $Q_{lm}$ 为工具，对11个手性晶体学点群逐一分析；针对轴向群 $C_n$、$D_n$（$n=2,3,4,6$），识别构成手性所需的代表最低秩多极内容，考察相邻秩 $l=n$ 与 $n+1$ 中同为 $m=n$ 的两个方位各向异性的相对取向；对立方群 $T$、$O$ 分别给出其手性实现。另在无自旋 $s$-$p$-$d$-$f$ 原子基下核算标量电荷密度所含多极的最大秩。

Result: 轴向群的手性源于两个方位各向异性（$m=n$，分别位于连续秩 $l=n$ 与 $n+1$）的相对取向，其共存产生随纬度变化的 $n$ 重电荷分布"扭曲"；$C_n$ 中允许的极向成分与该手性机制无关、并非手性所必需。立方群中 $T$ 与 $O$ 的实现方式不同，八面体手性首次出现在秩九。无自旋 $s$-$p$-$d$-$f$ 基的标量电荷密度仅含至秩六的多极，故无法编码 $C_6$、$D_6$、$O$ 的手性。

Conclusion: 电多极矩内容可作为晶体学手性的定量判据，并将多极秩、原子轨道自由度与手性的微观编码联系起来；同时给出基组秩对手性可描述性的硬性下限约束。

Abstract: We establish an electric-multipole ($Q_{lm}$) criterion for chirality of local atomic charge distributions. For the 11 crystallographic chiral point groups, we identify representative lowest-rank multipole contents required to distinguish the opposite handednesses. For the axial groups $C_n$ and $D_n$ with $n=2,3,4,6$, chirality arises from the relative orientation of two azimuthal anisotropies with $m=n$, belonging to consecutive ranks $l=n$ and $n+1$. Their coexistence produces a latitude-dependent twist of the $n$-fold charge distribution. The polar component, although allowed in $C_n$, is distinct from this chiral mechanism and is not required for handedness. The cubic groups $T$ and $O$ exhibit distinct realizations, with octahedral chirality first appearing at rank nine. We further show that, within a spinless $s$-$p$-$d$-$f$ atomic basis, the scalar charge density contains multipoles only up to rank six and therefore cannot encode the handedness of $C_6$, $D_6$, or $O$. These results connect electric-multipole content and atomic orbital degrees of freedom to the microscopic encoding of crystallographic handedness.

</details>


### [211] [A Zoo of Fermionic Relative Rényi Negativities in the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39349)
*Linjing Jiang,Pengfei Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出相对Rényi负性(relative Rényi negativities)作为混合态纠缠目击者，通过将费米子部分时间反演与尺寸超算符关联，建立路径积分表示并在大$N$下计算SYK及其推广模型的热态纠缠，发现对数量负性的可行代理及简单观测量不可见的一阶纠缠相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联费米子体系的混合态纠缠刻画困难：对数负性等诊断量在相互作用体系中计算代价高，精确对角化仅限于小尺寸；而扭转/非扭转Rényi负性等传统Rényi代理对可分离态也可能为正，缺乏作为纠缠判据的可靠性。

Method: 引入一族相对Rényi负性作为纠缠目击者；通过将费米子部分时间反演与尺寸超算符(size superoperator)相联系，得到这些Rényi负性的路径积分表示；利用大$N$展开，在SYK模型及Maldacena-Qi耦合SYK模型的热态中对其进行计算。

Result: 在原始SYK与Maldacena-Qi耦合SYK模型中识别出一类特殊的相对Rényi负性，可作为对数负性的合理代理；并揭示出对简单观测量不可见的一阶纠缠相变。

Conclusion: 相对Rényi负性是强关联费米子体系混合态纠缠的有效探针，并暗示存在一类以这些负性作为自由能的新型相变。

Abstract: Quantum entanglement is a fundamental resource that gives rise to correlations with no classical counterpart in strongly correlated fermionic systems. However, its characterization in mixed quantum states remains challenging. Established entanglement diagnostics, such as logarithmic negativity, are computationally demanding in interacting systems, and their evaluation by exact diagonalization is restricted to small system sizes. Meanwhile, conventional Rényi proxies, including twisted and untwisted Rényi negativities, can be positive even for separable states. In this work, we introduce a family of \textit{relative Rényi negativities} that serve as entanglement witnesses. By relating fermionic partial time reversal to the size superoperator, we establish a path-integral representation for these Rényi negativities, enabling their evaluation in thermal states of the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) model and its generalizations via a large-$N$ expansion. Applying this method, we investigate entanglement in both the original SYK model and the Maldacena-Qi coupled SYK model. We identify a special class of relative Rényi negativities which serve as plausible proxies for logarithmic negativity and uncover first-order entanglement transitions that are invisible to simple observables. These results highlight the utility of relative Rényi negativities as probes of mixed-state entanglement in strongly correlated fermionic systems and suggest the existence of a new class of transitions where these negativities serve as free energies.

</details>


### [212] [High-Field Heat Capacity of Atacamite Cu$_2$Cl(OH)$_3$ for $\mathbf{H} \parallel b$ axis: From Antiferromagnetic Order to Correlated Disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39669)
*L. Stödter,A. Demuer,T. Kotte,A. U. B. Wolter,B. Büchner,K. C. Rule,S. Süllow*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过最高35 T（H∥b）的比热测量，研究了阻挫锯齿链化合物atacamite中的场诱导自旋液态：低场长程反铁磁序在升场中消失，32 T以上出现随场向高温移动的宽峰，低温比热呈自旋能隙的指数激活行为，与H∥c下"锯齿链解耦为基/顶自旋子系统、部分极化M~Msat/2"的图像一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: atacamite Cu2Cl(OH)3是阻挫锯齿链磁体的候选材料，此前在H∥c方向提出了高场态解耦为两套自旋子系统的场景，需在另一晶向H∥b上加以检验，并直接探测高场态的能隙与自旋液体特征。

Method: 在H∥b方向施加最高35 T的高磁场，测量atacamite单晶的比热；追踪反铁磁相变异常随场的温度移动直至消失，并对高场低温柔比热做指数拟合以提取自旋能隙。

Result: 观察到反铁磁长程序的特征尖锐相变峰随场增强向低温移动并消失，进入无相变异常的自旋液态；32 T以上出现随场向高温移动的宽峰，低温比热可由指数行为很好地描述，表明存在热激活自旋能隙。

Conclusion: H∥b的高场结果与先前H∥c提出的图像相符：阻挫锯齿链在高场下解耦为基自旋与顶自旋两套子系统，形成M~Msat/2的部分极化态；该高场态表现为带自旋能隙的自旋液体类行为。

Abstract: We present the heat capacity of the frustrated sawtooth-chain compound atacamite Cu$_2$Cl(OH)$_3$ in high magnetic fields up to 35 T for $\mathbf{H} \parallel b$ axis. We clearly observe the change from antiferromagnetic long-range order, indicated by a sharp transition anomaly shifted to lower temperatures when increasing field, to a field-induced spin-liquid-like state with the absence of any transition anomaly. Here, in highest magnetic fields above 32 T a broad anomaly is present which is shifted to higher temperatures as the field is increased. The low-temperature heat capacity in the high-field region can be described well by an exponential behavior indicating a thermal activation over a spin gap. The findings are consistent with our scenario for the high-field state in atacamite for $\mathbf{H} \parallel c$ axis, where we proposed the sawtooth chains to decouple into two spin subsystems, basal and apical spin sites, leading to a partially polarized state with $M \sim M_\mathrm{sat}/2$.

</details>


### [213] [Phonon Fluctuations Enhanced Superconductivity in a Three-Band Cuprate Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39710)
*Jiarui Liu,Yao Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在无能级铜氧化物模型中，$B_{1g}$氧呼吸（buckling）声子对$d$-波超导的作用取决于其处于绝热还是反绝热区：热激发的绝热声子通过破坏准粒子相干性抑制配对，而反绝热声子同时增强相干性与吸引型$d$-波配对顶点，从而增强超导，且呈现显著电子-空穴非对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铜氧化物高温超导的微观起源受多重交织序影响仍是未解难题；实验与理论均提示声子（尤其是$B_{1g}$氧buckling模）可能起关键作用，但其微观机制与作用符号（增强或抑制）尚不明确，需要在一个可处理的少带模型中厘清。

Method: 在三带铜氧化物模型（Cu $d_{x^2-y^2}$与O $p_x/p_y$）中引入$B_{1g}$声子耦合，通过对比声子处于绝热区与反绝热区（相对给定温度）的情形，计算准粒子相干性、$d$-波配对顶点与超导序参量等多项诊断量，并分析掺杂载流子轨道特征带来的电子-空穴非对称性。

Result: 绝热声子被热占据后经准粒子相干性损失抑制$d$-波配对；反绝热声子则同时提升相干性与吸引型$d$-波配对顶点，在多项诊断量上一致表现为超导增强；该增强随掺杂呈现明显的电子-空穴非对称性，源于掺杂载流子不同的轨道成分。

Conclusion: $B_{1g}$声子可与电子关联协同，显著增强铜氧化物中的$d$-波超导配对，从而给出一个依赖于声子绝热性（能量尺度相对温度）的微观机制。

Abstract: The microscopic origin of high-$T_c$ superconductivity in cuprates remains an outstanding puzzle due to the interplay of intertwined orders. Experimental observations and theoretical studies have suggested crucial roles of phonons, with the $B_{1g}$ oxygen buckling mode emerging as a promising candidate. Here, we investigate the microscopic influence of this phonon in a three-band cuprate model. We find that the $B_{1g}$ phonon plays opposite roles in superconductivity depending on whether it lies in the adiabatic or antiadiabatic regime at a given temperature. Thermally populated adiabatic phonons suppress $d$-wave pairing through the loss of quasiparticle coherence, whereas antiadiabatic phonons simultaneously enhance coherence and the attractive $d$-wave pairing vertex, strengthening superconductivity across multiple diagnostics. This enhancement also exhibits pronounced electron-hole asymmetry arising from the different orbital characters of doped carriers. Our results therefore establish a microscopic mechanism through which $B_{1g}$ phonons can cooperate with electronic correlations to substantially enhance $d$-wave superconducting pairing in cuprates.

</details>


### [214] [Monte Carlo simulation of mixed spin-orbit ordering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39943)
*Yu. D. Panov,V. A. Ulitko*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 采用热浴算法的半经典蒙特卡洛方法研究磁场对最大纠缠Kugel-Khomskii模型热力学性质的影响，发现磁场诱导非零磁化，并在自旋-轨道关联函数非零的同一布里渊区位置同步出现轨道有序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究外加磁场如何影响最大纠缠Kugel-Khomskii模型的磁性与轨道自由度，以及自旋与轨道有序之间的耦合关系。

Method: 基于热浴算法（heat bath algorithm）的半经典蒙特卡洛（quasi-classical Monte Carlo）方法，计算单格点关联函数的温度依赖关系。

Result: 单格点关联函数的温度依赖显示，施加磁场后磁化变为非零；同时在自旋-轨道关联函数非零的同一布里渊区波矢处，轨道有序同步出现。

Conclusion: 磁场可同时诱导磁化与轨道有序，二者与自旋-轨道关联通过同一布里渊区波矢相耦合，体现自旋与轨道自由度的协同有序化。

Abstract: The effect of a magnetic field on the thermodynamic properties of the Kugel-Khomskii model with maximal entanglement using the quasi-classical Monte Carlo method with the heat bath algorithm. The temperature dependencies of single-site correlators show that the application of a magnetic field results in non-zero magnetization and the simultaneous appearance of an orbital ordering at the same point in the Brillouin zone where a non-zero spin-orbit correlator exists.

</details>


### [215] [Electronic liquid crystals and superconductivity from doping a moiré Mott insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39978)
*Yichi Zhang,Yiyu Xia,Zhongdong Han,Jekwan Lee,Brandon Bauer,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Wenjin Zhao,Kin Fai Mak,Jie Shan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在掺杂的扭角双层WSe2莫尔Mott绝缘体中，作者报道了条纹相（电子液晶）的普遍出现，并观测到条纹相到超导相的量子相变，以及金属条纹到钉扎绝缘条纹的额外热力学相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子液晶（条纹相、向列相）是自发破缺旋转对称性的自组织态，被认为是掺杂Mott绝缘体中短程与长程库仑相互作用折中的产物，并与高温超导（作为竞争序或交织序）密切相关。莫尔半导体提供了在Mott绝缘体附近可控研究条纹相与超导关系的平台。

Method: 采用电输运测量，在扭角双层WSe2中探测条纹相的形成与相变，包括从高温各向同性相到低温各向异性相的热力学相变及伴随的费米面重构证据。

Result: 条纹相在掺杂莫尔Mott绝缘体中普遍出现；低温各向异性相伴随费米面重构；在条纹-超导转变附近观测到金属条纹到钉扎绝缘条纹的额外热力学相变；条纹-超导转变呈现自相似性，凸显两种序的密切关联。

Conclusion: 结果表明莫尔材料中存在条纹相与超导的交织序，条纹-超导量子相变及条纹钉扎为研究莫尔体系中的交织序提供了新途径。

Abstract: Electronic liquid crystals, such as stripe phases and nematics, are self-organized states of matter with spontaneously broken rotational symmetry. They are expected to emerge in a doped Mott insulator as a compromise between short- and long-range Coulomb interactions, and are believed to be intimately connected to the emergence of high temperature superconductivity either as a competing order or an intertwined order. Moiré semiconductors provide a controllable platform to explore the relationship between stripe phases and superconductivity near a Mott insulator. Here we report the generic appearance of stripe phases in a doped moiré Mott insulator and the observation of a stripe to-superconductor quantum phase transition in twisted bilayer WSe2. The stripe phases were revealed by electrical transport studies as a thermodynamic phase transition from a high temperature isotropic phase to a low-temperature anisotropic phase accompanied by a Fermi surface reconstruction. Near the stripe-to-superconductor transition, we also observed an additional thermodynamic phase transition from metallic stripes to pinned insulating stripes. The stripe-to-superconductor transition further exhibits an intriguing self-similarity that highlights the intimate relationship between the two orders. Our results pave the path for studying intertwined orders in moiré materials.

</details>


### [216] [Static Response of the Strongly Coupled Electron Gas from Diffusion Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40093)
*Muhammed H. Güneş,Yubo Yang,Vitaly Gorelov,Miguel A. Morales,Matteo Gatti,Lucia Reining,Shiwei Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文用扩散蒙特卡洛（DMC）首次系统计算了均匀电子气在2≤r_s≤100范围内的静态密度响应函数χ(q)与局域场因子G(q)，给出计算流程的实用指南，并揭示低密度下G(q)在中q处出现非单调结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局域场因子G(q)刻画交换关联对静态微扰响应的修正，是介电屏蔽、有效电子-电子相互作用及TDDFT交换关联核的关键输入。然而精确的零温G(q)数据仅局限于金属密度（r_s≤10），低密度甚至强关联区间的可靠数据缺失，限制了对关联电子气的理解与基准检验。

Method: 采用扩散蒙特卡洛（DMC）计算2≤r_s≤100的静态密度响应函数χ(q)和G(q)：系统研究试探波函数中微扰势的优化，导出了最优微扰势的闭式表达式及线性响应窗口的密度依赖；检验了backflow关联与有限尺寸效应对结果的影响；并提出能在整个液相区重现数据的G(q)参数化形式。

Result: 微扰势的优化至关重要，选择不当甚至会反转响应符号；backflow关联不改变χ(q)，G(q)即便在极低密度下也仅弱依赖于系统尺寸，故Slater–Jastrow波函数与中等体系已足够。结果在金属密度下q>k_F区间与已有DMC数据一致，并扩展至q<k_F；与r_s=2的图解QMC一致。低密度下G(q)在中q区呈非单调结构，响应趋近无相互作用极限；与有限温度PIMC定量吻合，说明此前报道的有限温结构源自基态。

Conclusion: 该工作为未来QMC计算电子气及真实材料的响应性质提供了实用指南；借助可贯穿液相区的G(q)参数化，将中q非单调结构与对关联函数和静态结构因子中的短程有序联系起来，深化了对强关联电子气屏蔽行为的理解。

Abstract: The local field factor $G(q)$ of the HEG describes how exchange and correlation modify the response to a static perturbation, and enters dielectric screening, effective electron--electron interactions, and the xc kernel of TDDFT. Accurate zero-temperature data for $G(q)$, however, remain limited to metallic densities, $r_s\leq10$. In this work, we present diffusion Monte Carlo calculations of the static density response function $χ(q)$ and of $G(q)$ for $2\leq r_s\leq100$, and describe and discuss the methodological steps that enabled these calculations. We show how optimization of the perturbing potential in the trial wave function has an important effect, as a poor choice can reverse the sign of the response. A closed-form expression is provided for its optimal value, together with the density dependence of the linear-response window. We further show that backflow correlations leave $χ(q)$ unchanged and that $G(q)$ depends only weakly on system size even at very low density, so that Slater--Jastrow wave functions and modest system sizes suffice. Together, these results provide a practical guide for future QMC calculations of response properties of the electron gas and real materials. Our results agree with previous DMC data at metallic densities for $q>k_F$, and extend them below $k_F$. They also agree with diagrammatic QMC at $r_s=2$. At lower densities, our results show that $G(q)$ develops a nonmonotonic structure at intermediate-$q$, where the response approaches that of the noninteracting gas. Quantitative agreement is seen with finite-temperature path integral Monte Carlo, suggesting that the previously reported finite-temperature structure is inherited from the ground state. Using a parametrization of $G(q)$ that reproduces our data throughout the liquid phase, we relate this structure to short-range order in the pair correlation function and the static structure factor.

</details>


### [217] [Adiabatic Variational Principle: Born-Oppenheimer Theory for Emergent Electronic-Order Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40117)
*Yang Yang,Ali Rayat,Ho Jang,Yunhao Fan,Sankha Subhra Bakshi,Gia-Wei Chern*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出绝热变分原理（AVP），从相互作用多体哈密顿量直接推导序参量动力学，替代唯象相场方程，为电子序非平衡动力学提供微观基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微观多体方法已能精确描述平衡量子态，但涌现序的大尺度动力学模拟仍依赖预设自由能泛函的唯象方程，缺乏微观依据；传统相场描述中的绝热闭合假设也未被显式化。

Method: 假设微观弛豫远快于序参量演化（类比Born–Oppenheimer分离），使多体态绝热跟随集体构型；每个瞬时序参量构型对应受约束的准稳态，其拉格朗日乘子直接给出驱动力；再用非线性Mori–Zwanzig投影理论把可逆动力学与集体变量代数、耗散与涨落与消去自由度联系起来。

Result: 在电荷密度波与自旋密度波动力学中重现Allen–Cahn畴生长，解析演化中的畴网络及其微观电子结构，并导出巡游磁性的广义Landau–Lifshitz动力学。

Conclusion: AVP给出了与求解器无关的微观框架，可作为类相场模拟涌现电子序非平衡动力学的严格基础。

Abstract: Recent advances in microscopic many-body methods have enabled increasingly accurate descriptions of equilibrium quantum states, yet large-scale simulations of emergent-order dynamics typically rely on phenomenological equations with prescribed free-energy functionals. We introduce the Adiabatic Variational Principle (AVP), a framework for deriving order-parameter dynamics directly from an interacting many-body Hamiltonian. When microscopic relaxation is fast compared with order-parameter evolution, the many-body state follows the collective configuration adiabatically, in analogy with the Born--Oppenheimer separation of electronic and nuclear motion. This assumption makes explicit the adiabatic closure implicit in conventional phase-field descriptions. Each instantaneous order-parameter configuration then defines a constrained quasi-stationary many-body state, and the Lagrange multipliers enforcing these constraints directly provide the thermodynamic forces driving the collective evolution. Nonlinear Mori--Zwanzig projection theory further connects the reversible dynamics to the microscopic algebra of the collective variables and relates dissipation and fluctuations to the eliminated degrees of freedom. Applications to charge- and spin-density-wave dynamics recover Allen--Cahn domain growth, resolve evolving domain networks together with their microscopic electronic structure, and yield generalized Landau--Lifshitz dynamics for itinerant magnetism. The AVP thus provides a solver-independent microscopic foundation for phase-field-like simulations of the nonequilibrium dynamics of emergent electronic order.

</details>


### [218] [Investigation of perovskite related oxides: structural and magnetic properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40139)
*Antara Sarkar*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究不同结构钙钛矿中稀土(R)与过渡金属(TM)离子相互作用对磁性的影响，揭示轨道相互作用、无序、A/B位取代对超交换与双交换机制的调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钙钛矿结构多样且磁性依赖结构，在多铁、磁热与自旋电子器件中极具价值，因而需系统理解R与TM离子相互作用随结构变化的规律。

Method: 通过A位或B位取代引入结构畸变与无序，结合居里温度、自旋玻璃转变与自旋重取向转变的观测，分析轨道相互作用、有序/无序及R-TM相互作用对超交换和双交换的调节。

Result: 观察到随相互作用程度与结构性质不同的居里温度、自旋玻璃转变与自旋重取向转变；获得TM与R自旋相互作用、磁各向异性、轨道取向及无序诱导相互作用的实验证据。

Conclusion: 钙钛矿磁性由结构、轨道与无序共同决定，A/B位取代可有效调控超交换/双交换与磁转变行为，为多铁与自旋电子应用提供设计思路。

Abstract: Perovskites exist in varied structures and show structure dependent magnetic properties which is of great interest in the field of multiferroics, magnetocaloric and spintronic devices. Here we intend to study the various rare-earth(R) and transition metal (TM) ion interactions depending on their structures. Different Curie temperature, spinglass transition and spin reorientation transitions were observed depending on the degree of interactions and structural properties. It is interesting to study the role of orbital interactions, disorder in magnetic transitions leading to change in superexchange and double exchange interaction. On substitution at A-site or B-site results in change in magnetic interactions, as well as creates structural distortions. We also obtained results where the spin interaction of the transition metal and rare-earth interaction, magnetic anisotropy, orientations of orbitals, disorder induced interactions were observed.

</details>


### [219] [Cage-related crystal-field effects in the paramagnetic range of PrB$_6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40176)
*Mehdi Amara,Christine Opagiste,Natalya Yu. Shitsevalova*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过比热、热膨胀与磁性测量，揭示PrB₆在50 K以下顺磁区的新特征：磁熵接近双重态、低温磁化率似非磁基态，暗示晶场基态在硼笼内的动力学分裂，并观测到离心Jahn-Teller效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子散射表明PrB₆的晶场基态为三重态，但磁性行为的解释存在矛盾；同时CeB₆中存在类似的晶场基态动力学分裂现象，作者希望厘清PrB₆顺磁区的熵与磁化率行为，检验是否存在类似效应。

Method: 结合比热、热膨胀与磁化率测量；利用两次连续磁相变的潜热并改进晶格贡献描述，定量确定磁熵随温度的演化；并分析低温顺磁磁化率。

Result: 在T_N=6.7 K（一级磁有序）之上，每Pr³⁺磁熵接近双重态而非三重态，需超过30 K才能达到三重简并基态熵；低温磁化率呈非磁基态特征；20 K以下出现大热膨胀反常，与离心Jahn-Teller效应一致。

Conclusion: PrB₆中晶场三重基态在硼笼内发生动力学分裂，行为类似CeB₆；熵、磁化率与热膨胀三方面证据相互支持。

Abstract: New insights into the paramagnetic range of PrB$_6$ below 50 K are revealed through specific-heat, thermal-expansion and magnetic measurements. The latent heats of the two successive magnetic transitions, together with an improved description of the lattice contribution, enable a detailed determination of the thermal variation of the magnetic entropy. Immediately above the first-order magnetic ordering temperature ($T_N = 6.7$ K), the magnetic entropy per Pr$^{3+}$ is close to that of a doublet, instead of the triplet crystal-field ground state identified by inelastic neutron scattering. Temperatures in excess of 30 K are required to reach the entropy of the threefold-degenerate ground state. This is reminiscent of the CeB$_6$ case and, similarly, strongly suggests a dynamical splitting of the crystal-field ground state within the boron cage. Further evidence of this splitting is provided by the analysis of the low-temperature paramagnetic susceptibility, which resembles that of a non-magnetic ground state. In addition, a large thermal-expansion anomaly is observed below 20 K, consistent with the expected centrifugal Jahn-Teller effect.

</details>


### [220] [Topological Quantum Spin Liquids in a Ladder with Majorana fermions and p-wave Superconductivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40297)
*Magali Korolev,Fan Yang,Erik S. Sørensen,Karyn Le Hur*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究梯形（ribbon）几何中仅含短程关联的拓扑量子自旋液体的可能性，通过二维Kitaev蜂窝模型到条带几何的精确可解推广，结合量子场论、数学与DMRG方法，给出含三个自旋能隙自旋液体相与一个Majorana金属相的完整相图分类，并提出体-边对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨能否在整条相图上稳定存在仅具短程自旋关联的拓扑量子自旋液体，并据此寻找方格子上含π通量拓扑自旋模型的解。

Method: 构建Kitaev蜂窝模型到梯形/条带几何的精确可解推广模型；基态中静态涌现Z2规范场，由Majorana费米子产生0或π通量；采用量子场论分析、数学推导与密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）数值计算，并通过全域观测量进行分类。

Result: 相图含三个带自旋能隙的自旋液体相与一个Majorana费米子意义下的金属相；体-边对应表明能隙相边缘存在受保护零能Majorana模，拓扑液体相体内存在巡游Majorana费米子；可区分brickwall梯形（产生特殊线）与square梯形（允许无能隙Majorana液体相）的性质。

Conclusion: 该精确可解模型为方格子上含π通量的拓扑量子自旋模型求解提供希望，并可映射到拓扑超导模型。

Abstract: In this article, we address the possibility of topological quantum spin liquids with only short-range (spin) correlations in the whole phase diagram in a ladder system, through an exactly solvable model which represents a generalization of the two-dimensional Kitaev honeycomb spin model to a ribbon geometry. This system gives rise to $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauge fields emerging in a static way in the ground state through Majorana fermions which then produce either $0$ or $π$ flux. Through a quantum field theory perspective, mathematical and Density Matrix Renormalization Group results, we present our progress on the geometrical, topological and physical classification of this model through observables navigating on the whole phase diagram presenting three spin-liquid phases with a spin gap and a metallic phase in the sense of Majorana fermions. We propose a bulk-edge correspondence revealing the presence of protected zero-energy Majorana fermions at the edge in the phases with a gap and revealing also the presence of itinerant Majorana fermions in the bulk in the topological liquid phase. The proposed approach allows us to distinguish between properties of the brickwall ladder giving rise to a special line and those of the square ladder allowing for the gapless Majorana liquid phase. This model gives some hope on finding solutions for topological quantum spin models, e.g. with $π$ flux, on a square lattice geometry. This approach also allows for a correspondence onto models of topological superconductivity.

</details>


### [221] [Quasi-Phases of Quantum Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40526)
*Nick G. Jones,Paul Fendley,Nathanan Tantivasadakarn,Ruben Verresen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出"准相"(quasi-phase)概念：连续相变线终止后两侧有能隙区域虽在同一相中连通，但不存在可从临界理论小扰动获得的无能隙路径，需借助更大的多临界母理论描述，并在自旋链例子中发现c=3/2与c=9/5 orbifold结构及携带高维线性表示界面模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续相变线终止时，两侧区域是否可被视为类似水与蒸汽的独立"相"长期不明；任何尖锐刻画都须超越常规物相定义。作者希望在超出传统分类的框架下给出肯定答案。

Method: 引入准相定义（临界点附近的能隙区域，在整体相图中连通但无小扰动可达的能隙路径）；构造一维自旋链例子，其中终止型连续线中心荷c=1，用多临界c=3/2与c=9/5 orbifold统一描述延伸的临界线与连接两区域的能隙路径；通过弱化Lieb-Schultz-Mattis反常构造"商对称保护"与"表示对称保护"拓扑现象，并分析界面模的表示维数。

Result: 准相可区分并产生同一物相内区域间的界面模；统一描述需要更大的多临界母理论；界面模可携带高维线性表示，而非常规SPT的投影表示类；给出"红外必要但不紫外必要"的临界点判据，其限制强于仅存在余维一临界线。

Conclusion: 物相内部结构比传统分类所揭示的更丰富；准相为终止型连续线两侧区域提供严格刻画，并将对称保护拓扑、异常匹配与临界点必要性统一于多临界母理论框架。

Abstract: Within quantum many-body phase diagrams, continuous transition lines can appear and then terminate. However, it has remained unclear whether the neighboring regimes can be meaningfully described as distinct phases, akin to water and vapour, since any sharp characterization would have to go beyond the usual notion of phases of matter. We answer this in the affirmative by introducing quasi-phases: gapped regimes near a critical point that are connected in the broader phase diagram, but not by any gapped path accessible from small perturbations of the critical theory. The physical consequences include interface modes between regimes in the same phase of matter. More striking still, describing both the extended critical line and the gapped path connecting the regimes requires a bigger multicritical parent theory. In spin-chain examples with continuous terminating lines with central charge $c=1$, we find multicritical $c=3/2$ and $c=9/5$ orbifolds describing so-called 'quotient symmetry protected' and 'representation symmetry protected' topological phenomena. While one-dimensional symmetry-protected topological phases are labeled by projective representation classes, these quasi-phases can have interface modes carrying higher-dimensional linear representations. We turn this into a constructive principle through a weakening of the Lieb-Schultz-Mattis anomaly. Supporting distinct quasi-phases makes an unnecessary critical point infrared-necessary, even if it is unnecessary at the ultraviolet scale. This condition is more restrictive than simply having a codimension-one critical line, revealing richer structure within phases of matter than their conventional classification suggests.

</details>


### [222] [Algebraic theory of abelian fractons and beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40596)
*Wilbur Shirley,Evan Wickenden,Bowen Yang,Agnès Beaudry,Michael Hermele*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出 braided fusion complex（BFC）与 braided fusion theory（BFT）的代数框架，统一刻画 Abelian gapped fracton order 与拓扑相中具有受限迁移性和空间延展激发的融合与统计，并用其形式化 excitation-detector principle 与非简并性的等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 Abelian anyon 理论只适用于点状、可任意移动的激发，无法描述 fracton 相中受限迁移的激发和延展激发。需要一个能同时编码融合、迁移性与统计的代数框架，并给出相不变量与可实现性判据。

Method: 考虑具有有限融合阶的平移不变玻色系统，构造 BFC：用 chain complex 编码融合与迁移性，并附加编码统计的 braiding chain map；其等价类定义 BFT。对仅含点状激发的非简并理论，提取由 fusion module 及到其对偶统计的映射构成的 mutual statistics 理论；在 BFC 中给出 excitation-detector principle 的精确表述。

Result: 框架涵盖任意平移不变 Pauli code 及二维所有 Abelian anyon 理论（含手征理论）。导出对点状融合与迁移性的约束，例如 X-cube 与各向异性模型的 Pauli X 与 Z 扇区均不能单独出现在非简并 BFT 中。mutual statistics 理论是 BFT 的不变量，奇融合阶下与完整 BFT 等价；将 remote detection 解释为 mutual statistics 在 fracton order 中的推广。假设无穷空间基态唯一时，证明 excitation-detector principle 等价于非简并性。

Conclusion: 非简并 BFT 可望实现为某个 gapped 相的激发理论，且该 BFT 是相不变量（文中给出证据）。BFC/BFT 为 fracton 与拓扑相提供了统一的代数分类语言，并将激发-探测原则提升为可证明的非简并性判据。

Abstract: We develop an algebraic theory of fusion, mobility, and statistics in abelian gapped fracton orders and topological phases. The framework generalizes the algebraic description of abelian anyons to systems with restricted-mobility and spatially extended excitations. We consider translation-invariant bosonic systems whose pointlike and extended excitations have finite fusion order. The basic objects are braided fusion complexes (BFCs): chain complexes encoding fusion and mobility, equipped with a braiding chain map encoding statistics. Their equivalence classes define braided fusion theories (BFTs). Our framework encompasses arbitrary translation-invariant Pauli codes, as well as all abelian anyon theories in two spatial dimensions, including chiral theories. We give evidence for the conjecture that every nondegenerate BFT can be realized as the excitation theory of a gapped phase and that the BFT describing such a phase is a phase invariant. We derive constraints on pointlike fusion and mobility, showing, for example, that neither the Pauli X nor Z sectors of the X-cube and anisotropic models can occur alone in a nondegenerate BFT. For nondegenerate theories with only pointlike excitations, we extract a simpler theory of mutual statistics, consisting of a fusion module and a map to its statistical dual. This theory of mutual statistics is an invariant of the BFT and, at odd fusion order, gives an equivalent description of the full braided fusion theory. We interpret remote detection as the generalization of mutual statistics to fracton orders. In the BFC setting, we give a precise formulation of the excitation-detector principle: every nontrivial excitation can be detected by an operator supported at spatial infinity, and every nontrivial detector detects some excitation. Assuming a unique infinite-space ground state, we prove that this principle is equivalent to nondegeneracy.

</details>


### [223] [Variational wavefunctions for ideal anyon gas: superfluid and skyrmion Hall crystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40657)
*Ya-Hui Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文构造内嵌精确任意子统计的变分波函数（复合玻色子/费米子/parton 波函数 × 神经网关联，球面几何），在无自旋与 SU(2) semion 情形确认配对任意子超流优于晶体；对 SU(3) 味 θ=π/3 任意子给出 skyrmion 霍尔晶体与手征色超导体两个候选，后者能量最低，并据此解释 twisted MoTe₂ 中的重入量子反常霍尔与超导现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计角 θ∈(0,π) 的理想任意子气体基态长期未解，而分数陈绝缘体的最新实验观测重新激发了对该问题的兴趣；现有方法难以在兼顾精确任意子统计的同时刻画强关联液体相与晶体相的竞争。

Method: 在球面上构建严格内嵌任意子统计的变分波函数：以理论指导的复合玻色子、复合费米子与 parton 波函数为骨架，叠加神经网络关联因子；无自旋与 SU(2) semion、θ=π/3 无自旋任意子、以及 SU(3) 味 θ=π/3 任意子分别与最佳晶体态比较能量。SU(3) 情形下提出 skyrmion 霍尔晶体（任意子在自旋织构诱导的有效磁场中形成整数量子霍尔态）与六任意子团簇的手征色超导体（holon 金属的色味锁定配对后裔，波函数为超行列式而非 parton 行列式之积），并与 U(1)²、Z₃ 规范群的色味锁定相比较。

Result: 无自旋与 SU(2) semion 情形确认配对任意子超流，能量远低于最佳晶体态；θ=π/3 无自旋情形液体与晶体能量接近。SU(3) 味 θ=π/3 时色超导体能量优于晶体及 U(1)²、Z₃ 色味锁定相；对 twisted MoTe₂ 推断重入整数量子反常霍尔相为掺杂任意子的 skyrmion 霍尔晶体，观测到的超导可能为色超导体，并可跃迁为 f+if BCS 超导体，手征中心荷跳变 3。

Conclusion: 精确统计约束的神经网络变分方法可系统比较任意子液体与晶体相，给出 semion 超流及 SU(3) 味下色超导体占优的相图；该框架为 twisted MoTe₂ 的重入量子反常霍尔与超导提供统一解释，并预言手征中心荷跳变 3 的可检验特征。

Abstract: The ground state of an ideal gas of anyons with statistical angle $θ\in(0,π)$ is a long-standing open problem, with renewed interest from the recent observations of fractional Chern insulators. In this work we construct variational wavefunctions that build in the exact anyon statistics and combine theory-guided composite-boson, composite-fermion and parton wavefunctions with neural-network correlations on the sphere. For spinless and SU(2) semions we confirm the paired anyon superfluid, well below the best crystal states. At $θ=π/3$ liquids and crystals are closer in energy for the spinless case. For $θ=π/3$ anyons with SU(3) flavor, we find two candidates. One is a skyrmion Hall crystal, in which the anyons form an integer quantum Hall state in the effective magnetic field from the spin texture. The other is a chiral color superconductor of six-anyon clusters, the color-flavor-locked pairing descendant of a holon metal with SU(3) color gauge symmetry and SU(3) flavor symmetry; its wavefunction is a hyperdeterminant rather than a product of parton determinants. Within our variational ansatz the color superconductor has better energy than both crystals and the color-flavor locked phases with U(1)$^2$ and Z$_3$ gauge groups. For twisted MoTe$_2$ this suggests that the reentrant integer quantum anomalous Hall phase is the skyrmion Hall crystal of the doped anyons, and that the observed superconductivity may be the color superconductor, which can undergo a transition to an $f+if$ BCS superconductor with a jump of the chiral central charge by 3.

</details>
